from backend.app.core.config import get_settings
def _is_port_available(host: str, port: int) -> bool:
    probe_host = "0.0.0.0" if host in {"0.0.0.0", "::"} else host
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as sock:
        sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        try:
            sock.bind((probe_host, port))
        except OSError:
            return False
    return True
def main() -> None:
    settings = get_settings()
    if not _is_port_available(settings.host, settings.port):
        raise SystemExit(
            f"Port {settings.port} is already in use. "
            f"Change YUNXUN_PORT or PORT in the environment, then start the backend again."
        )
    uvicorn.run(
        "backend.app.main:app",
        host=settings.host,
        port=settings.port,
        reload=settings.debug,
    )
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

logger = logging.getLogger("yunxun.backend")
def create_app() -> FastAPI:
    settings = get_settings()
    validate_startup_settings(settings)
    log_runtime_status(logger, settings)
    init_db()
    app = FastAPI(
        title=settings.app_name,
        version=settings.app_version,
        debug=settings.debug,
        docs_url="/docs" if settings.docs_enabled else None,
        redoc_url="/redoc" if settings.docs_enabled else None,
    )
    @app.middleware("http")
    async def csrf_guard(request, call_next):
        try:
            if request.url.path.startswith("/api/"):
                validate_csrf_request(request)
        except HTTPException as exc:
            return await http_exception_handler(request, exc)
        return await call_next(request)
    app.add_middleware(
        CORSMiddleware,
        allow_origins=settings.allowed_origins,
        allow_credentials=True,
        allow_methods=settings.cors_methods,
        allow_headers=settings.cors_headers,
        expose_headers=["Content-Disposition", "X-Export-Row-Count"],
    )
    @app.middleware("http")
    async def request_context(request, call_next):
        request_id = normalize_request_id(request.headers.get("X-Request-ID"))
        context_token = set_request_id(request_id)
        started = time.perf_counter()
        status_code = 500
        try:
            content_length = request.headers.get("content-length", "")
            if content_length.isdigit() and int(content_length) > 10 * 1024 * 1024:
                response = error_response("请求体过大。", 413, code="REQUEST_TOO_LARGE")
            else:
                response = await call_next(request)
            status_code = response.status_code
            response.headers["X-Request-ID"] = request_id
            response.headers["X-Content-Type-Options"] = "nosniff"
            response.headers["Referrer-Policy"] = "no-referrer"
            response.headers["X-Frame-Options"] = "DENY"
            response.headers["Permissions-Policy"] = "camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=()"
            if not request.url.path.startswith(("/docs", "/redoc", "/openapi.json")):
                response.headers["Content-Security-Policy"] = "default-src 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'"
            return response

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 3 页 / 共 184 页
        finally:
            logger.info(
                "event=http_request method=%s path=%s status_code=%d duration_ms=%d request_id=%s",
                request.method,
                request.url.path,
                status_code,
                round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000),
                request_id,
            )
            reset_request_id(context_token)
    app.add_exception_handler(HTTPException, http_exception_handler)
    app.add_exception_handler(RequestValidationError, validation_exception_handler)
    app.add_exception_handler(Exception, unexpected_exception_handler)
    app.include_router(system.router)
    app.include_router(auth.router)
    app.include_router(tools.router)
    app.include_router(farm.router)
    logger.info("Application initialized", extra={"host": settings.host, "port": settings.port})
    return app
app = create_app()
from fastapi import Header, Request
from backend.app.services.auth import get_current_user_from_header
from backend.app.core.security import session_reference
def get_session_reference(request: Request, authorization: str | None = Header(default=None)) -> str:
    token = request.cookies.get("yunxun_session")
    if not token and authorization and authorization.startswith("Bearer "):
        token = authorization.split(" ", 1)[1].strip()
    return session_reference(token) if token else ""
def get_current_user(request: Request, authorization: str | None = Header(default=None)) -> dict[str, str]:
    cookie_token = request.cookies.get("yunxun_session")
    if cookie_token and authorization:
        cookie_user = get_current_user_from_header(f"Bearer {cookie_token}")
        bearer_user = get_current_user_from_header(authorization)
        if cookie_user["id"] != bearer_user["id"]:
            from fastapi import HTTPException
            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="认证身份冲突。")
        return cookie_user
    if cookie_token:
        return get_current_user_from_header(f"Bearer {cookie_token}")
    return get_current_user_from_header(authorization)

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/auth", tags=["auth"])
auth_rate_limiter = InMemoryRateLimiter()
credential_rate_limiter = InMemoryRateLimiter()
def _limit_credential_proof(user_id: str) -> None:
    credential_rate_limiter.cleanup()
    credential_rate_limiter.check(f"proof:{user_id}", 5, 600)
def _clear_browser_session(response: Response) -> None:
    response.delete_cookie("yunxun_session", path="/")
    response.delete_cookie(CSRF_COOKIE_NAME, path="/")
    response.headers["Cache-Control"] = "no-store"
@router.post("/change-password")
def change_password_api(request: PasswordChangeRequest, response: Response, user: dict = Depends(get_current_user)) -> dict[str, object]:
    _limit_credential_proof(user["id"])
    manage_credentials(user, request.current_password, new_password=request.new_password)
    _clear_browser_session(response)
    log_event(logger, "auth_password_changed", user_id=user["id"])
    return success_payload(message="密码已修改，全部设备已退出登录，旧恢复码已失效。请重新登录并生成新恢复码。")
@router.post("/recovery-code")
def recovery_code_api(request: PasswordProofRequest, response: Response, user: dict = Depends(get_current_user)) -> dict[str, object]:
    _limit_credential_proof(user["id"])
    code = manage_credentials(user, request.current_password)
    response.headers["Cache-Control"] = "no-store"
    log_event(logger, "auth_recovery_code_generated", user_id=user["id"])
    return success_payload(recovery_code=code, message="仅本次展示，请离线保存；旧恢复码已失效。")
@router.post("/recover-password")
def recover_password_api(request: PasswordRecoveryRequest, http_request: Request, response: Response) -> dict[str, object]:
    _limit_auth(http_request)
    host = http_request.client.host if http_request.client else "local"
    credential_rate_limiter.cleanup()
    credential_rate_limiter.check(f"recover-ip:{safe_fingerprint(host)}", 20, 600)
    credential_rate_limiter.check(f"recover-account:{safe_fingerprint(request.username.strip().lower())}", 5, 600)
    user_id = recover_password(request.username, request.recovery_code, request.new_password)
    _clear_browser_session(response)
    log_event(logger, "auth_password_recovered", user_id=user_id)
    return success_payload(message="密码已重置，恢复码已使用，全部设备已退出登录。请重新登录并生成新恢复码。")
def _limit_auth(request: Request) -> None:
    settings = get_settings()
    host = request.client.host if request.client else "local"
    auth_rate_limiter.check(
        f"auth:{safe_fingerprint(host)}",
        settings.auth_requests_per_minute,
        settings.auth_window_seconds,
    )
def _set_browser_session(response: Response, token: str) -> None:

    response.set_cookie(CSRF_COOKIE_NAME, create_csrf_token(), httponly=False, secure=settings.cookie_secure, samesite=settings.cookie_same_site, path="/", max_age=settings.token_hours * 3600)
@router.post("/register")
def register_api(request: RegisterRequest, http_request: Request, response: Response) -> dict[str, object]:
    _limit_auth(http_request)
    payload = register_user(request.username, request.password, request.display_name)
    _set_browser_session(response, str(payload["token"]))
    return success_payload(**payload)

def csrf_api(response: Response) -> dict[str, object]:
    settings = get_settings()
    response.set_cookie(CSRF_COOKIE_NAME, create_csrf_token(), httponly=False, secure=settings.cookie_secure, samesite=settings.cookie_same_site, path="/", max_age=settings.token_hours * 3600)
    return success_payload(message="CSRF token issued")

@router.post("/logout")
def logout_api(http_request: Request, response: Response, authorization: str | None = Header(default=None), request: LogoutRequest | None = None) -> dict[str, object]:
    current_session = get_session_reference(http_request, authorization)
    if request and current_session and not secrets.compare_digest(request.session_id, current_session):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="当前会话已变化，旧退出请求不能退出新会话。")
    if http_request.cookies.get("yunxun_session"):
        authorization = f"Bearer {http_request.cookies['yunxun_session']}"
    user_id: str | None = None
    if authorization and authorization.startswith("Bearer "):
        try:
            user_id = get_current_user_from_header(authorization)["id"]
        except HTTPException:
            user_id = None
    logout_user(authorization, user_id=user_id)
    response.headers["Cache-Control"] = "no-store"
    return success_payload(message="已退出登录。")

    payload = upgrade_guest_user(user, request.username, request.password, request.display_name)
    _set_browser_session(response, str(payload["token"]))

FARM_RECORD_KINDS = ("播种", "施肥", "打药", "灌溉", "除草", "采收", "其他")
def normalize_record_kind(kind: str) -> str:
    normalized = kind.strip()
    if normalized not in FARM_RECORD_KINDS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="作业类型只能是：" + "、".join(FARM_RECORD_KINDS) + "。")
    return normalized
def record_write_values(request: FarmRecordCreateRequest) -> dict:
    return {**request.model_dump(exclude={"submission_id"}), "kind": normalize_record_kind(request.kind),
            "auto_select_season": "season_id" not in request.model_fields_set}

@router.delete("/trash/{kind}/{entity_id}")
def trash_purge_api(kind: str, entity_id: str, confirm: bool = False,
    expected_revision: str = Query(..., min_length=1, max_length=128), user: dict = Depends(get_current_user)) -> dict:
    if kind not in {"plots", "records"}:
        raise HTTPException(404, "回收站类型不存在。")
    if not confirm:
        raise HTTPException(422, "请明确确认最终删除，此操作无法从回收站恢复。")
    (trash.purge_plot if kind == "plots" else trash.purge_record)(entity_id, user["id"], expected_revision)
    log_event(farm_logger, "trash_purge", user_id=user["id"], kind=kind, entity_id=entity_id)
    return success_payload(message="已最终删除，无法再从回收站恢复。")
@router.get("/farm-records")
def list_farm_records_api(
    plot_id: str | None = Query(default=None, max_length=64, description="按地块筛选。"),
    kind: str | None = Query(default=None, max_length=16, description="按作业类型筛选。"),
    date_from: IsoDate | None = None,
    date_to: IsoDate | None = None,
    season_id: str | None = Query(default=None, max_length=64, description="茬次 ID；unassigned 表示未归茬。"),
    economics_scope: str = Query(default="all", max_length=32, description="收支或待补录明细类型。"),
    data_scope: str = Query(default="all", max_length=16, description="all、real 或 demo，按记录来源筛选。"),
    year: int | None = Query(default=None, ge=1, le=9999, description="按作业日期所属年份筛选。"),
    limit: int = Query(default=20, ge=1, le=100, description="每页数量。"),
    cursor: str | None = Query(default=None, max_length=512, description="分页游标。"),
    user: dict[str, str] = Depends(get_current_user),
) -> dict[str, object]:
    try:
        parsed_cursor = decode_cursor(cursor) if cursor else None
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    records, has_more = list_user_farm_records(
        user["id"],
        plot_id=plot_id,
        limit=limit,
        cursor=parsed_cursor,
        kind=normalize_record_kind(kind) if kind is not None else None,
        date_from=date_from,
        date_to=date_to,
        season_id=season_id,
        economics_scope=economics_scope,
        data_scope=data_scope,
        year=year,
    )
    next_cursor = None
    if has_more and records:
        next_cursor = encode_cursor(records[-1]["happened_on"], records[-1]["id"])
    return success_payload(records=records, pagination={"has_more": has_more, "next_cursor": next_cursor})

@router.get("/tool-records")
def list_tool_records_api(
    kind: str | None = Query(default=None, description="记录类型：decision，不传则返回全部。"),
    limit: int = Query(default=20, ge=1, le=100, description="每页数量。"),
    cursor: str | None = Query(default=None, max_length=512, description="分页游标。"),
    user: dict[str, str] = Depends(get_current_user),
) -> dict[str, object]:
    normalized_kind = (kind or "").strip().lower() or None
    if normalized_kind and normalized_kind not in TOOL_RECORD_KINDS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="kind 只支持 decision。")
    try:
        parsed_cursor = decode_cursor(cursor) if cursor else None
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    records, has_more = list_tool_records_page(
        user["id"],
        kind=normalized_kind,
        limit=limit,
        cursor=parsed_cursor,

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 15 页 / 共 184 页
    )
    next_cursor = None
    if has_more and records:
        next_cursor = encode_cursor(records[-1]["created_at"], records[-1]["id"])
    return success_payload(records=records, pagination={"has_more": has_more, "next_cursor": next_cursor})

    cleanup_expired_tokens,
    create_auth_token,
    create_user,
    delete_auth_token,
    get_user_by_id,
    get_user_by_token_hash,
    get_user_by_username,

    update_user_profile,
    upgrade_guest_account,

USERNAME_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9_]+$")
logger = logging.getLogger("yunxun.backend.auth")
def register_user(username: str, password: str, display_name: str, *, is_guest: bool = False) -> dict[str, str | dict]:
    normalized_username = username.strip().lower()
    normalized_display_name = display_name.strip()
    if not USERNAME_PATTERN.fullmatch(normalized_username):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="用户名只能包含英文字母、数字或下划线。")
    if get_user_by_username(normalized_username):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="用户名已存在。")
    if not normalized_display_name:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="显示名称不能为空。")

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 16 页 / 共 184 页
    user_record = create_user(
        username=normalized_username,
        password_hash=hash_password(password),
        display_name=normalized_display_name,
        is_guest=is_guest,
    )
    log_event(logger, "auth_register_success", user_id=user_record["id"], username=normalized_username)
    token = issue_auth_token(user_record["id"], expected_password_hash=user_record["password_hash"])
    return {"user": public_user(user_record), "token": token, "session_id": session_reference(token)}
def login_user(username: str, password: str) -> dict[str, str | dict]:
    user_record = get_user_by_username(username.strip().lower())
    if not user_record or not verify_password(password, user_record["password_hash"]):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="用户名或密码不正确。")
    token = issue_auth_token(user_record["id"], expected_password_hash=user_record["password_hash"])
    log_event(logger, "auth_login_success", user_id=user_record["id"], username=user_record["username"])
    return {"user": public_user(user_record), "token": token, "session_id": session_reference(token)}
def guest_login() -> dict[str, str | dict]:
    suffix = uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
    log_event(logger, "auth_guest_login", guest_suffix=suffix)
    return register_user(
        username=f"guest_{suffix}",
        password=secrets.token_urlsafe(16),
        display_name=f"访客 {suffix[:4]}",
        is_guest=True,
    )
def upgrade_guest_user(user: dict, username: str, password: str, display_name: str) -> dict:
    if not user.get("is_guest"):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="当前账号不是可升级的访客账号，不能通过此入口修改账号。")
    normalized = username.strip().lower()
    if not USERNAME_PATTERN.fullmatch(normalized):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="用户名只能包含英文字母、数字或下划线。")
    token = create_token()
    expires_at = (now_utc() + timedelta(hours=get_settings().token_hours)).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    updated = upgrade_guest_account(user["id"], normalized, hash_password(password), display_name.strip(),
                                    hash_auth_token(token), expires_at)
    log_event(logger, "auth_guest_upgrade", user_id=updated["id"], username=normalized)
    return {"user": public_user(updated), "token": token, "session_id": session_reference(token)}
def issue_auth_token(user_id: str, *, expected_password_hash: str | None = None) -> str:
    settings = get_settings()
    token = create_token()
    expires_at = (now_utc() + timedelta(hours=settings.token_hours)).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    cleanup_expired_tokens()
    if expected_password_hash is None:
        create_auth_token(user_id, hash_auth_token(token), expires_at)
    else:
        create_auth_token(user_id, hash_auth_token(token), expires_at, expected_password_hash=expected_password_hash)
    return token
def get_current_user_from_header(authorization: str | None) -> dict[str, str]:
    if not authorization or not authorization.startswith("Bearer "):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="请先登录。")

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 17 页 / 共 184 页
    token = authorization.split(" ", 1)[1].strip()
    user_record = get_user_by_token_hash(hash_auth_token(token))
    if not user_record:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="登录状态已失效，请重新登录。")
    return user_record
def logout_user(authorization: str | None, user_id: str | None = None) -> None:
    if not authorization or not authorization.startswith("Bearer "):
        return
    token = authorization.split(" ", 1)[1].strip()
    delete_auth_token(hash_auth_token(token))
    log_event(logger, "auth_logout", user_id=user_id or "unknown")
def update_profile(user_id: str, display_name: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    user_record = get_user_by_id(user_id)
    if not user_record:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="用户不存在。")
    if not display_name.strip():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="显示名称不能为空。")
    updated_record = update_user_profile(user_id, display_name.strip())
    log_event(logger, "auth_profile_update", user_id=user_id)
    return public_user(updated_record)
def build_decision_reply(
    crop: str,
    stage: str,
    rain_prob: int,
    soil_moisture: int,
    temperature: float,
) -> str:
    wet = rain_prob >= 60 or soil_moisture >= 65
    dry = rain_prob <= 30 and soil_moisture <= 35
    if wet:
        water_advice = "今天先不浇水，低洼地块及时开沟排水。"
        fertilizer_advice = "雨前不要大水大肥，等田间能下脚后再少量追肥。"
        water_condition = "降雨概率或土壤湿度达到偏湿提示条件"
    elif dry:
        water_advice = "早晚小水慢灌，优先保苗保根。"
        fertilizer_advice = "可随水少量追肥，避开中午高温。"
        water_condition = "降雨概率与土壤湿度均达到偏干提示条件"
    else:
        water_advice = "保持现有墒情，今天重点巡田查虫查病。"
        fertilizer_advice = "长势弱的地块可少量补肥，长势正常的不用急着加量。"
        water_condition = "未触发偏湿或偏干提示条件"
    return f"""
**今日建议（水分条件提示）**
仅依据你填写或估计的降雨概率和土壤湿度给出提示，没有自动天气或传感器数据。作物、生长期和气温只作记录，不参与分支判断；这不是综合风险评估或用药依据，实际农事请结合现场情况核对。
1. {water_advice}
2. {fertilizer_advice}
3. 你记录的作物为 {crop}，生长期为“{stage}”；巡田时请核对实际长势。
4. 水分条件：{water_condition}。请自行核对后续天气与田间实际情况。
**用户填写的条件**
- 降雨概率：{rain_prob}%

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 18 页 / 共 184 页
- 土壤湿度：{soil_moisture}%
- 气温（仅记录）：{temperature:.1f}℃
""".strip()
"""面向用户的业务 CSV，使用标准库，无磁盘文件或数据库备份功能。"""
import csv
import io
import re
import unicodedata
from datetime import date
from typing import Any, Iterable
from urllib.parse import quote
from fastapi import Response
from backend.app.repositories import export_farm_records
from backend.app.services.farm import (
    require_plot_owner, require_season_owner, summarize_user_farm_economics,
    validate_farm_record_filters,
)
SOURCES = {"all": "全部记录含演示", "real": "真实记录", "demo": "演示记录"}
SCOPES = {"all": "全部账目", "cost": "已记录支出", "revenue": "已完整记录收入",
          "missing_cost": "费用未填", "incomplete_harvest": "采收待补录"}
ACCOUNTING_NOTE = "已记录收支差额=已记录收入-已记录支出；收入仅含产量和单价完整的采收；未记录的人工、地租等不包含在内"
def safe_csv_cell(value: Any) -> Any:
    if value is None:
        return ""
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        return value
    start = 0
    while start < len(value) and (value[start].isspace() or unicodedata.category(value[start]).startswith("C")):
        start += 1
    significant = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", value[start:start + 1])
    if (significant in {"=", "+", "-", "@"}
            or (value and unicodedata.category(value[0]).startswith("C"))):
        return "\t'" + value
    return value
def csv_response(filename: str, headers: list[str], rows: Iterable[list[Any]]) -> Response:
    output = io.StringIO(newline="")
    writer = csv.writer(output, quoting=csv.QUOTE_ALL, lineterminator="\r\n")
    writer.writerow(headers)
    count = 0
    for row in rows:
        writer.writerow([safe_csv_cell(value) for value in row])
        count += 1
    return Response(output.getvalue().encode("utf-8-sig"), media_type="text/csv; charset=utf-8", headers={
        "Content-Disposition": f"attachment; filename=yunxun-export.csv; filename*=UTF-8''{quote(filename, safe='')}",
        "Cache-Control": "no-store", "X-Export-Row-Count": str(count),
    })
def export_filename(title: str, user_id: str, filters: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    plot_id, season_id = filters.get("plot_id"), filters.get("season_id")
    season = require_season_owner(season_id, user_id) if season_id and season_id != "unassigned" else None
    effective_plot = plot_id or (season["plot_id"] if season else None)

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 19 页 / 共 184 页
    parts = [title, SOURCES[filters.get("data_scope", "all")]]
    parts.append(require_plot_owner(effective_plot, user_id)["name"] if effective_plot else "全部地块")
    if season:
        parts.extend([season["crop"], f"{season['started_on'] or '起始未填'}至{season['ended_on'] or '进行中'}"])
    else:
        parts.append("未归茬" if season_id else "全部茬次")
    if filters.get("kind"):
        parts.append(filters["kind"])
    if filters.get("year"):
        parts.append(f"作业{filters['year']}年")
    if filters.get("date_from") or filters.get("date_to"):
        parts.append(f"{filters.get('date_from') or '起始不限'}至{filters.get('date_to') or '结束不限'}")
    if filters.get("economics_scope", "all") != "all":
        parts.append(SCOPES[filters["economics_scope"]])
    parts.append(f"导出{date.today().isoformat()}")
    name = "_".join(parts)
    name = "".join("_" if unicodedata.category(char).startswith("C") else char for char in name)
    return re.sub(r'[<>:"/\\|?*]', "_", name).strip(". ") + ".csv"
def export_user_records(user_id: str, **filters: Any) -> Response:
    validate_farm_record_filters(user_id, **{key: value for key, value in filters.items()
        if key in {"plot_id", "season_id", "date_from", "date_to", "economics_scope", "data_scope"}})
    filename = export_filename("农事台账", user_id, filters)
    records = export_farm_records(user_id, **filters)
    def rows():
        for record in records:
            harvest, spray = record["kind"] == "采收", record["kind"] == "打药"
            complete = harvest and record["yield_kg"] is not None and record["unit_price"] is not None
            yield_kg, price = record["yield_kg"], record["unit_price"]
            assignment = "已归茬" if record["season_crop"] is not None else "茬次关联不可用" if record["season_id"] else "未归茬"
            yield [record["plot_name"], record["crop"], record["season_crop"], assignment,
                record["season_started_on"], record["season_ended_on"], record["happened_on"], record["kind"],
                record["material"] if record["kind"] in {"施肥", "打药"} else None, record["quantity"], record["cost"],
                yield_kg if harvest else None, price if harvest else None,
                yield_kg * price if complete else None, record["safe_days"] if spray else None,
                record["detail"], "演示记录" if record["is_demo"] else "真实记录"]
    return csv_response(filename, ["地块", "作业作物", "茬次作物", "归茬情况", "茬次开始日期", "茬次结束日期", "作业日期", "作业类型",
        "投入品", "用量（原填写内容及单位）", "费用（元）", "采收产量（公斤）", "采收单价（元/公斤）", "已记录收入（元）", "登记间隔期（天）", "说明", "记录来源"], rows())
def export_user_economics(user_id: str, **filters: Any) -> Response:
    result = summarize_user_farm_economics(user_id, **filters)
    filename = export_filename("每茬收支汇总", user_id, filters)
    def rows():
        for row in result["plots"]:
            cost_missing = row["recorded_cost_count"] == 0 and row["missing_cost_count"] > 0
            income_missing = row["complete_harvest_count"] == 0 and row["incomplete_harvest_count"] > 0
            yield_missing = row["recorded_yield_count"] == 0 and row["incomplete_harvest_count"] > 0
            yield [row["plot_name"], row["crop"], "已归茬" if row["season_id"] else "未归茬", row["started_on"], row["ended_on"],
                row["area_mu"], row["record_count"], None if cost_missing else row["total_cost"],
                None if yield_missing else row["total_yield_kg"], None if income_missing else row["total_revenue"], row["net_revenue"],
                None if cost_missing else row["cost_per_mu"], None if yield_missing else row["yield_per_mu"],
                None if income_missing else row["revenue_per_mu"], row["net_per_mu"], row["missing_cost_count"], row["incomplete_harvest_count"],

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 20 页 / 共 184 页
                row["recorded_cost_count"], row["complete_harvest_count"], row["recorded_yield_count"],
                filters.get("year") or "全部年份", "按作业日期", SOURCES[filters.get("data_scope", "all")], ACCOUNTING_NOTE]
    return csv_response(filename, ["地块", "茬次作物", "归茬情况", "茬次开始日期", "茬次结束日期", "当前登记面积（亩）", "记录次数",
        "已记录支出（元）", "已记录产量（公斤）", "已记录收入（元）", "已记录收支差额（元）", "亩均支出（元/亩）", "亩均产量（公斤/亩）",
        "亩均收入（元/亩）", "亩均收支差额（元/亩）", "费用未填（条）", "采收待补录（条）", "费用已填（条）", "完整采收（条）", "产量已填（条）",
        "作业年份", "年度口径", "统计数据来源", "核算说明"], rows())

def require_plot_owner(plot_id: str, user_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """校验地块归属。
    地块不存在和地块属于别人都返回 404：不让调用方通过状态码探测别人是否
    存在某个地块。
    """

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 21 页 / 共 184 页
    plot = get_plot(plot_id)
    if not plot or plot["user_id"] != user_id:
        raise not_found("地块不存在或已被删除。")
    return plot
def _plot_by_id(user_id: str, plot_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """从地块列表里取回单块地，这样带出的茬次字段与列表完全一致。"""
    for item in list_plots(user_id):
        if item["id"] == plot_id:
            return item
    raise not_found("地块不存在或已被删除。")
def require_season_owner(season_id: str, user_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    season = get_season(season_id)
    if not season or season["user_id"] != user_id:
        raise not_found("茬次不存在或已被删除。")
    return season
def list_user_seasons(user_id: str, *, plot_id: str | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    return list_seasons(user_id, plot_id=plot_id)
def create_user_season(
    user_id: str,
    client_host: str,
    plot_id: str,
    crop: str,
    started_on: str | None,
    notes: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    require_plot_owner(plot_id, user_id)
    active = get_active_season(plot_id)
    if active:
        raise conflict(f"该地块还有进行中的茬次（{active['crop']}），请先结束它再开始新茬。")
    season = create_season(
        user_id=user_id,
        plot_id=plot_id,
        crop=crop.strip(),
        started_on=started_on or None,
        notes=notes.strip(),
    )
    log_event(
        logger,
        "season_create",
        user_id=user_id,
        client_fingerprint=safe_fingerprint(client_host),
        season_id=season["id"],
        plot_id=plot_id,
        crop=season["crop"],
    )
    return season
def update_user_season(
    season_id: str,
    user_id: str,
    client_host: str,

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 22 页 / 共 184 页
    crop: str,
    started_on: str | None,
    ended_on: str | None,
    notes: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    existing = require_season_owner(season_id, user_id)
    if started_on and ended_on and ended_on < started_on:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="结束日期不能早于开始日期。")
    if ended_on is None:
        active = get_active_season(existing["plot_id"])
        if active and active["id"] != season_id:
            raise conflict("该地块已有进行中的茬次，请先结束它再重开旧茬。")
    season = update_season(
        season_id,
        crop=crop.strip(),
        started_on=started_on or None,
        ended_on=ended_on or None,
        notes=notes.strip(),
    )
    log_event(
        logger,
        "season_update",
        user_id=user_id,
        client_fingerprint=safe_fingerprint(client_host),
        season_id=season_id,
        active=season["active"],
    )
    return season
def delete_user_season(season_id: str, user_id: str, client_host: str) -> None:
    require_season_owner(season_id, user_id)
    delete_season(season_id)
    log_event(
        logger,
        "season_delete",
        user_id=user_id,
        client_fingerprint=safe_fingerprint(client_host),
        season_id=season_id,
    )

def delete_user_plot(plot_id: str, user_id: str, client_host: str, expected_revision: str | None = None) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """地块移入回收站，子项继承可见性；保留原响应计数字段。"""
    require_plot_owner(plot_id, user_id)
    removed_records, removed_tasks = delete_plot_with_records(plot_id, user_id, expected_revision)
    log_event(
        logger,
        "plot_delete",
        user_id=user_id,
        client_fingerprint=safe_fingerprint(client_host),
        plot_id=plot_id,
        removed_records=removed_records,
        removed_tasks=removed_tasks,
    )
    return removed_records, removed_tasks
def list_user_farm_records(
    user_id: str,
    *,
    plot_id: str | None,
    limit: int,
    cursor: tuple[str, str] | None,
    kind: str | None = None,
    date_from: str | None = None,
    date_to: str | None = None,
    season_id: str | None = None,
    economics_scope: str = "all",
    year: int | None = None,
    data_scope: str = "all",
) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], bool]:
    validate_farm_record_filters(user_id, plot_id=plot_id, season_id=season_id, date_from=date_from,
                                 date_to=date_to, economics_scope=economics_scope, data_scope=data_scope)
    return list_farm_records_page(user_id, plot_id=plot_id, limit=limit, cursor=cursor,
                                  kind=kind, date_from=date_from, date_to=date_to, season_id=season_id,
                                  economics_scope=economics_scope, year=year, data_scope=data_scope)
def validate_farm_record_filters(
    user_id: str, *, plot_id: str | None = None, season_id: str | None = None,
    date_from: str | None = None, date_to: str | None = None,
    economics_scope: str = "all", data_scope: str = "all",
) -> None:
    if date_from and date_to and date_from > date_to:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="筛选结束日期不能早于开始日期。")
    _validate_record_season_filter(user_id, plot_id, season_id)
    validate_data_scope(data_scope)
    if economics_scope not in ECONOMICS_SCOPES:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="收支明细类型无效。")
def _validate_record_season_filter(user_id: str, plot_id: str | None, season_id: str | None) -> None:
    if season_id and season_id != "unassigned":

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 25 页 / 共 184 页
        season = require_season_owner(season_id, user_id)
        if plot_id and season["plot_id"] != plot_id:
            raise not_found("茬次不属于该地块。")
def _prepare_farm_record(
    user_id: str,
    plot_id: str,
    kind: str,
    happened_on: str,
    crop: str,
    detail: str,
    quantity: str,
    cost: float | None,
    material: str = "",
    safe_days: int | None = None,
    yield_kg: float | None = None,
    unit_price: float | None = None,
    season_id: str | None = None,
    auto_select_season: bool = True,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    require_plot_owner(plot_id, user_id)
    if season_id is not None:
        season = require_season_owner(season_id, user_id)
        if season["plot_id"] != plot_id:
            raise not_found("茬次不属于该地块。")
    return dict(
        plot_id=plot_id,
        season_id=season_id,
        auto_select_season=auto_select_season,
        kind=kind,
        happened_on=happened_on,
        crop=crop.strip(),
        detail=detail.strip(),
        quantity=quantity.strip(),
        cost=cost,
        material=material.strip() if kind in {"施肥", "打药"} else "",
        safe_days=safe_days if kind == "打药" else None,
        yield_kg=yield_kg if kind == "采收" else None,
        unit_price=unit_price if kind == "采收" else None,
    )
def create_user_farm_record(
    user_id: str,
    client_host: str,
    plot_id: str,
    kind: str,
    happened_on: str,
    crop: str,
    detail: str,
    quantity: str,
    cost: float | None,
    material: str = "",

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 26 页 / 共 184 页
    safe_days: int | None = None,
    yield_kg: float | None = None,
    unit_price: float | None = None,
    season_id: str | None = None,
    auto_select_season: bool = True,
    submission_id: str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    values = _prepare_farm_record(
        user_id, plot_id, kind, happened_on, crop, detail, quantity, cost,
        material, safe_days, yield_kg, unit_price, season_id, auto_select_season,
    )
    record_id = uuid.uuid5(uuid.NAMESPACE_URL, f"yunxun:{user_id}:{submission_id}").hex if submission_id else None
    record = create_farm_record(user_id=user_id, record_id=record_id, **values)
    log_event(
        logger,
        "farm_record_create",
        user_id=user_id,
        client_fingerprint=safe_fingerprint(client_host),
        record_id=record["id"],
        plot_id=plot_id,
        kind=kind,
        happened_on=happened_on,
    )
    return record
def update_user_farm_record(
    record_id: str, user_id: str, client_host: str, **values: Any,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    existing = get_farm_record(record_id)
    if not existing or existing["user_id"] != user_id:
        raise not_found("农事记录不存在或已被删除。")
    prepared = _prepare_farm_record(user_id=user_id, **values)
    record = update_farm_record(record_id, user_id, **prepared)
    log_event(logger, "farm_record_update", user_id=user_id,
              client_fingerprint=safe_fingerprint(client_host), record_id=record_id,
              plot_id=record["plot_id"], kind=record["kind"], happened_on=record["happened_on"])
    return record
def delete_user_farm_record(record_id: str, user_id: str, client_host: str) -> None:
    record = get_farm_record(record_id)
    if not record or record["user_id"] != user_id:
        raise not_found("农事记录不存在或已被删除。")
    delete_farm_record(record_id, user_id)
    log_event(
        logger,
        "farm_record_delete",
        user_id=user_id,
        client_fingerprint=safe_fingerprint(client_host),
        record_id=record_id,
        plot_id=record["plot_id"],
    )
def summarize_user_farm_records(user_id: str) -> dict[str, int]:

def summarize_user_farm_economics(
    user_id: str, *, plot_id: str | None = None, season_id: str | None = None, year: int | None = None, data_scope: str = "all",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if plot_id:
        require_plot_owner(plot_id, user_id)
    _validate_record_season_filter(user_id, plot_id, season_id)
    validate_data_scope(data_scope)
    return {
        "plots": summarize_farm_economics(user_id, plot_id=plot_id, season_id=season_id, year=year, data_scope=data_scope),
        "available_years": list_farm_record_years(user_id, data_scope),
        "year_basis": "happened_on",
    }
def validate_data_scope(data_scope: str) -> None:
    if data_scope not in {"all", "real", "demo"}:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="数据来源只能是全部、真实或演示。")

def update_user_farm_task(
    task_id: str,
    user_id: str,
    client_host: str,
    plot_id: str | None,
    title: str,
    due_on: str,
    notes: str,
    done: bool,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    require_task_owner(task_id, user_id)
    if plot_id:
        require_plot_owner(plot_id, user_id)
    task = update_farm_task(
        task_id,
        plot_id=plot_id or None,
        title=title.strip(),
        due_on=due_on,
        notes=notes.strip(),
        done=done,
    )
    log_event(
        logger,
        "farm_task_update",
        user_id=user_id,
        client_fingerprint=safe_fingerprint(client_host),
        task_id=task_id,
        done=done,
    )
    return task
def delete_user_farm_task(task_id: str, user_id: str, client_host: str) -> None:
    require_task_owner(task_id, user_id)
    delete_farm_task(task_id)

        logger,
        "farm_task_delete",
        user_id=user_id,
        client_fingerprint=safe_fingerprint(client_host),
        task_id=task_id,
    )

def build_health_payload() -> dict[str, object]:
    status = build_runtime_status(get_settings())
    return {
        "app_name": status["app_name"],
        "app_version": status["app_version"],
        "environment": status["environment"],
        "backend_url": status["backend_url"],
        "requests_per_minute": status["requests_per_minute"],
        "debug": status["debug"],
        "database_path": Path(str(status["database_path"])).name,
        "allowed_origins": status["allowed_origins"],
        "warnings": status["warnings"],
    }
def build_liveness_payload() -> dict[str, object]:
    settings = get_settings()
    return {
        "status": "ok",
        "service": settings.app_name,
        "version": settings.app_version,
        "environment": settings.environment,
        "request_id": get_request_id(),
    }
def build_readiness_payload() -> dict[str, object]:
    settings = get_settings()
    database = check_database_ready()
    checks = {
        "application": True,
        "database": database["ready"],
        "schema_version": database["schema_version"],
    }
    return {
        "status": "ready" if checks["application"] and database["ready"] else "degraded",
        "checks": checks,
        "database": Path(settings.db_path).name,
        "warnings": build_runtime_status(settings)["warnings"],
        "request_id": get_request_id(),
    }
def check_database_ready() -> dict[str, object]:
    try:
        with get_connection() as conn:
            version = int(conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version").fetchone()[0])
            tables = {row[0] for row in conn.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
    except (OSError, sqlite3.Error):
        return {"ready": False, "schema_version": None}
    return {"ready": version == SCHEMA_VERSION and CORE_TABLES.issubset(tables), "schema_version": version}

rate_limiter = InMemoryRateLimiter()
def _persist_tool_record_safely(**record: object) -> None:
    """历史记录落库失败只记日志，不影响农活建议主流程。"""
    try:
        create_tool_record(**record)
    except Exception:
        logger.warning("Failed to persist tool record kind=%s", record.get("kind"), exc_info=True)
def create_decision_advice(
    user_id: str,
    client_host: str,
    crop: str,
    stage: str,
    rain_prob: int,
    soil_moisture: int,
    temperature: float,
) -> dict[str, str]:
    settings = get_settings()
    rate_limiter.check(f"decision:{user_id}:{client_host}", settings.requests_per_minute)
    log_event(
        logger,
        "decision_request",
        user_id=user_id,
        client_fingerprint=safe_fingerprint(client_host),
        crop=crop,
        stage=stage,
        rain_prob=rain_prob,
        soil_moisture=soil_moisture,
        temperature=temperature,
    )
    reply = build_decision_reply(
        crop=crop,
        stage=stage,
        rain_prob=rain_prob,
        soil_moisture=soil_moisture,
        temperature=temperature,
    )
    _persist_tool_record_safely(
        user_id=user_id,
        kind="decision",
        crop=crop.strip() or "当前作物",
        payload={
            "stage": stage,
            "rain_prob": rain_prob,
            "soil_moisture": soil_moisture,
            "temperature": temperature,
        },
        result=reply,

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 31 页 / 共 184 页
        mode="local",
    )
    return {"reply": reply}

def validate_iso_date(value: str) -> str:
    date.fromisoformat(value)
    return value

def add_days(iso_date: str | None, days: int | None) -> str | None:
    """在农事日期上加天数；任一参数缺失或日期格式异常时返回 None。"""
    if not iso_date or days is None:
        return None
    try:
        base = date.fromisoformat(iso_date)
    except ValueError:
        return None
    return (base + timedelta(days=days)).isoformat()

        "display_name": record["display_name"],
        "created_at": record["created_at"],
    }
def _fetchone(query: str, params: tuple[Any, ...]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    with get_connection() as conn:
        row = conn.execute(query, params).fetchone()
    return dict(row) if row else None

    user_id = uuid.uuid4().hex

                """
                INSERT INTO users (id, username, password_hash, display_name, created_at, updated_at, is_guest)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """,
                (user_id, username, password_hash, display_name, timestamp, timestamp, int(is_guest)),
            )

                raise conflict("用户名已存在，请换一个用户名。") from exc
            raise
        row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?", (user_id,)).fetchone()
    return dict(row)

    updated_at = now_iso()
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute(
            """
            UPDATE users
            SET display_name = ?, updated_at = ?
            WHERE id = ?
            """,
            (display_name, updated_at, user_id),
        )
        row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?", (user_id,)).fetchone()
    return dict(row)
def upgrade_guest_account(
    user_id: str, username: str, password_hash: str, display_name: str,
    token_hash: str, expires_at: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """原地升级及会话替换必须一起提交；不复制或修改业务实体。"""
    timestamp = now_iso()
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        current = conn.execute("SELECT is_guest FROM users WHERE id=?", (user_id,)).fetchone()
        if not current:
            raise not_found("账号不存在。")
        if not current["is_guest"]:
            raise AppError(code=ErrorCode.GUEST_ALREADY_UPGRADED, message="账号已经保存，不能重复升级。请使用正式账号登录。", status_code=409)
        try:
            conn.execute("""UPDATE users SET username=?, password_hash=?, display_name=?, is_guest=0, updated_at=?
                WHERE id=? AND is_guest=1""", (username, password_hash, display_name, timestamp, user_id))
        except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:
            if exc.sqlite_errorcode == sqlite3.SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE and "users.username" in str(exc):
                raise conflict("用户名已存在，请换一个用户名。") from exc
            raise
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM auth_tokens WHERE user_id=?", (user_id,))
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO auth_tokens (token_hash,user_id,expires_at,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?)",
                     (token_hash, user_id, expires_at, timestamp))
        row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id=?", (user_id,)).fetchone()
    return dict(row)

        conn.execute(

            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)

def get_user_by_token_hash(token_hash: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    with get_connection() as conn:
        token_row = conn.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM auth_tokens WHERE token_hash = ? AND expires_at >= ?",
            (token_hash, now_iso()),
        ).fetchone()
        if not token_row:
            return None
        user_row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?", (token_row["user_id"],)).fetchone()
    return dict(user_row) if user_row else None

            ),

def list_tool_records_page(
    user_id: str,
    *,
    kind: str | None = None,
    limit: int = 20,
    cursor: tuple[str, str] | None = None,
) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], bool]:
    conditions = ["user_id = ?"]
    params: list[Any] = [user_id]
    if kind:
        conditions.append("kind = ?")
        params.append(kind)
    if cursor:
        conditions.append("(created_at < ? OR (created_at = ? AND id < ?))")
        params.extend([cursor[0], cursor[0], cursor[1]])
    params.append(limit + 1)
    where_sql = " AND ".join(conditions)
    with get_connection() as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM tool_records WHERE " + where_sql
            + " ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT ?",
            tuple(params),
        ).fetchall()
    has_more = len(rows) > limit
    return [public_tool_record(dict(row)) for row in rows[:limit]], has_more
def count_tool_records_by_kind(user_id: str) -> dict[str, int]:
    with get_connection() as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(
            """
            SELECT kind, COUNT(*) AS total
            FROM tool_records
            WHERE user_id = ?
            GROUP BY kind
            """,
            (user_id,),
        ).fetchall()
    return {str(row["kind"]): int(row["total"]) for row in rows}

def summarize_tool_records(user_id: str, *, days: int = 14, top_crops: int = 5) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """近 N 天按日计数与作物 Top K 汇总，供统计面板展示。
    按本机时区归日，而不是直接切 UTC 时间戳：created_at 存的是 UTC，在东八区
    早上 8 点前记的建议会被算到前一天。窗口只有十几天，取回来在 Python 里归日
    的代价可以忽略。窗口起点往前多取一天，覆盖时区偏移造成的边界。
    """
    local_today = datetime.now().date()
    since = (local_today - timedelta(days=days - 1)).isoformat()
    window_start = (local_today - timedelta(days=days)).isoformat()
    with get_connection() as conn:
        stamp_rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT created_at FROM tool_records WHERE user_id = ? AND created_at >= ?",
            (user_id, window_start),
        ).fetchall()
        crop_rows = conn.execute(
            """
            SELECT crop, COUNT(*) AS total
            FROM tool_records
            WHERE user_id = ?
            GROUP BY crop
            ORDER BY total DESC, crop ASC
            LIMIT ?
            """,
            (user_id, top_crops),
        ).fetchall()
    by_day: dict[str, int] = {}
    for row in stamp_rows:
        try:
            local_day = datetime.fromisoformat(row["created_at"]).astimezone().date().isoformat()
        except ValueError:
            continue
        if local_day >= since:
            by_day[local_day] = by_day.get(local_day, 0) + 1
    return {
        "since": since,
        "days": days,
        "by_day": by_day,
        "top_crops": [{"crop": str(row["crop"]), "total": int(row["total"])} for row in crop_rows],
    }
def public_plot(record: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """地块投影。
    crop / planted_on 不再是地块自己的列，而是从茬次推导：优先进行中的那一茬，
    没有则退回最近一茬（「上次种的是玉米」对农户仍然有用）。这样台账的作物默认值、
    今日农活的地块带入都不用改。
    """
    season_id = record.get("season_id")
    season_ended_on = record.get("season_ended_on")
    is_active = season_id is not None and season_ended_on is None
    return {
        "is_demo": bool(record.get("is_demo", False)),

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 38 页 / 共 184 页
        "id": record["id"],
        "name": record["name"],
        "area_mu": float(record["area_mu"]),
        "soil_type": record["soil_type"],
        "irrigation": record["irrigation"],
        "crop": record.get("season_crop") or "",
        "planted_on": record.get("season_started_on"),
        "notes": record["notes"],
        "record_count": int(record.get("record_count") or 0),
        "open_task_count": int(record.get("open_task_count") or 0),
        "active_season": (
            {
                "id": season_id,
                "crop": record.get("season_crop") or "",
                "started_on": record.get("season_started_on"),
            }
            if is_active
            else None
        ),
        "season_record_count": int(record.get("season_record_count") or 0) if is_active else 0,
        "created_at": record["created_at"],
        "updated_at": record["updated_at"],
    }
def _insert_plot(
    conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: str, name: str, area_mu: float,
    soil_type: str, irrigation: str, notes: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    plot_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
    timestamp = now_iso()
    conn.execute(
        """INSERT INTO plots (id, user_id, name, area_mu, soil_type, irrigation, notes, created_at, updated_at)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
        (plot_id, user_id, name, area_mu, soil_type, irrigation, notes, timestamp, timestamp),
    )
    return public_plot(dict(conn.execute("SELECT * FROM plots WHERE id=?", (plot_id,)).fetchone()))
def create_plot(
    user_id: str, name: str, area_mu: float, soil_type: str, irrigation: str, notes: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    with get_connection() as conn:
        return _insert_plot(conn, user_id, name, area_mu, soil_type, irrigation, notes)
def create_plot_with_first_season(
    user_id: str, name: str, area_mu: float, soil_type: str, irrigation: str,
    notes: str, crop: str, started_on: str | None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """一个业务操作只有一个提交点；第二次 INSERT 失败时地块也回滚。"""
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        plot = _insert_plot(conn, user_id, name, area_mu, soil_type, irrigation, notes)
        _insert_season(conn, user_id, plot["id"], crop, started_on, "")
        return plot

def get_plot(plot_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    return _fetchone("SELECT * FROM plots WHERE id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL", (plot_id,))
def list_plots(user_id: str, *, reference_date: str | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """地块列表，附带作业记录条数与最近一次打药的安全期状态。
    安全期状态并进地块列表而不是单开接口：地块页和台账页都要用它，
    合并后前端只需一次请求、一个数据来源，不必维护两份可能不同步的状态。
    """
    with get_connection() as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(
            """
            SELECT p.*,
                s.id AS season_id,
                s.crop AS season_crop,
                s.started_on AS season_started_on,
                s.ended_on AS season_ended_on,
                (
                    SELECT COUNT(*) FROM farm_records r WHERE r.plot_id = p.id AND r.user_id=p.user_id AND r.deleted_at IS NULL
                ) AS record_count,
                (
                    SELECT COUNT(*) FROM farm_records r WHERE r.season_id = s.id AND r.user_id=p.user_id AND r.deleted_at IS NULL
                ) AS season_record_count,
                (
                    SELECT COUNT(*) FROM farm_tasks t WHERE t.plot_id = p.id AND t.user_id=p.user_id AND t.done_at IS NULL
                ) AS open_task_count
            FROM plots p
            LEFT JOIN plot_seasons s ON s.id = (
                SELECT s2.id FROM plot_seasons s2 WHERE s2.plot_id = p.id AND s2.user_id=p.user_id
                ORDER BY (s2.ended_on IS NULL) DESC, s2.started_on DESC, s2.id DESC LIMIT 1
            )
            WHERE p.user_id = ? AND p.deleted_at IS NULL
            ORDER BY p.updated_at DESC, p.id DESC
            """,
            (user_id,),
        ).fetchall()
    safety_by_plot = {
        item["plot_id"]: item
        for item in list_harvest_safety(
            user_id,
            reference_date=reference_date or datetime.now().date().isoformat(),
        )
    }
    plots = []
    for row in rows:
        plot = public_plot(dict(row))
        plot["harvest_safety"] = safety_by_plot.get(plot["id"])
        plots.append(plot)
    return plots

    return int(row["total"]) if row else 0
def _update_plot_row(
    conn: sqlite3.Connection, plot_id: str, name: str, area_mu: float,
    soil_type: str, irrigation: str, notes: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    conn.execute(
        """UPDATE plots SET name=?, area_mu=?, soil_type=?, irrigation=?, notes=?, updated_at=?
        WHERE id=? AND deleted_at IS NULL""",
        (name, area_mu, soil_type, irrigation, notes, now_iso(), plot_id),
    )
    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM plots WHERE id=? AND deleted_at IS NULL", (plot_id,)).fetchone()
    if row is None:
        raise not_found("地块不存在或已移入回收站。")
    return public_plot(dict(row))
def update_plot(
    plot_id: str, name: str, area_mu: float, soil_type: str, irrigation: str, notes: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    with get_connection() as conn:
        return _update_plot_row(conn, plot_id, name, area_mu, soil_type, irrigation, notes)
def update_plot_with_active_season(
    plot_id: str, user_id: str, name: str, area_mu: float, soil_type: str,
    irrigation: str, notes: str, crop: str | None, started_on: str | None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """兼容旧地块编辑：地块与当前茬次同改同回滚，不使用锁外的当前茬次快照。"""
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        _validate_farm_links(conn, user_id, plot_id)
        plot = _update_plot_row(conn, plot_id, name, area_mu, soil_type, irrigation, notes)
        if crop:
            active = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM plot_seasons WHERE plot_id=? AND user_id=? AND ended_on IS NULL",
                                  (plot_id, user_id)).fetchone()
            if active:
                _update_season_row(conn, active["id"], crop, started_on, None, active["notes"])
        return plot

def create_farm_task(
    user_id: str,
    plot_id: str | None,
    title: str,
    due_on: str,
    notes: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    task_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
    created_at = now_iso()
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        _validate_farm_links(conn, user_id, plot_id)
        conn.execute(
            """
            INSERT INTO farm_tasks (id, user_id, plot_id, title, due_on, done_at, notes, created_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, NULL, ?, ?)
            """,
            (task_id, user_id, plot_id, title, due_on, notes, created_at),
        )
        row = conn.execute(FARM_TASK_SELECT + " WHERE t.id = ?", (task_id,)).fetchone()
    return public_farm_task(dict(row))
TASK_VISIBLE = "(t.plot_id IS NULL OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM plots p WHERE p.id=t.plot_id AND p.user_id=t.user_id AND p.deleted_at IS NULL))"
def get_farm_task(task_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    return _fetchone(FARM_TASK_SELECT + " WHERE t.id = ? AND " + TASK_VISIBLE, (task_id,))
def list_open_farm_tasks(user_id: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """未完成待办，按到期日升序；同一到期日再按创建时间保证顺序稳定。"""
    with get_connection() as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(
            FARM_TASK_SELECT + " WHERE t.user_id = ? AND " + TASK_VISIBLE + " AND t.done_at IS NULL ORDER BY t.due_on ASC, t.id ASC",
            (user_id,),
        ).fetchall()
    return [public_farm_task(dict(row)) for row in rows]
def list_recent_done_farm_tasks(user_id: str, *, limit: int = 20) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    with get_connection() as conn:

            FARM_TASK_SELECT + " WHERE t.user_id = ? AND " + TASK_VISIBLE + " AND t.done_at IS NOT NULL ORDER BY t.done_at DESC LIMIT ?",
            (user_id, limit),
        ).fetchall()
    return [public_farm_task(dict(row)) for row in rows]

def _insert_season(
    conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: str, plot_id: str, crop: str,
    started_on: str | None, notes: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    season_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
    timestamp = now_iso()
    _validate_farm_links(conn, user_id, plot_id)
    try:
        conn.execute(
            """INSERT INTO plot_seasons

                (id, user_id, plot_id, crop, started_on, ended_on, notes, created_at, updated_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, NULL, ?, ?, ?)""",
            (season_id, user_id, plot_id, crop, started_on, notes, timestamp, timestamp),
        )
    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:
        if exc.sqlite_errorcode == sqlite3.SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE and "plot_seasons.plot_id" in str(exc):
            raise conflict("该地块已有进行中的茬次，请刷新后重试。") from exc
        raise
    return public_season(dict(conn.execute("SELECT * FROM plot_seasons WHERE id=?", (season_id,)).fetchone()))

def get_active_season(plot_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """地块进行中的那一茬；部分唯一索引保证最多只有一条。"""
    return _fetchone(
        "SELECT * FROM plot_seasons WHERE plot_id = ? AND ended_on IS NULL AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM plots p WHERE p.id=plot_seasons.plot_id AND p.user_id=plot_seasons.user_id AND p.deleted_at IS NULL)",

    )
def list_seasons(user_id: str, *, plot_id: str | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    conditions = ["user_id = ?", "EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM plots p WHERE p.id=plot_seasons.plot_id AND p.user_id=plot_seasons.user_id AND p.deleted_at IS NULL)"]
    params: list[Any] = [user_id]
    if plot_id:
        conditions.append("plot_id = ?")
        params.append(plot_id)
    with get_connection() as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM plot_seasons WHERE " + " AND ".join(conditions)
            + " ORDER BY started_on DESC, id DESC",
            tuple(params),
        ).fetchall()
    return [public_season(dict(row)) for row in rows]
def _validate_season_record_dates(
    conn: sqlite3.Connection, season_id: str, started_on: str | None, ended_on: str | None,
) -> None:
    if started_on and ended_on and ended_on < started_on:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="结束日期不能早于开始日期。")
    impact = conn.execute(
        """SELECT COUNT(*) AS total, COALESCE(SUM(deleted_at IS NOT NULL), 0) AS trashed
        FROM farm_records WHERE season_id=? AND
            ((? IS NOT NULL AND happened_on<?) OR (? IS NOT NULL AND happened_on>?))""",
        (season_id, started_on, started_on, ended_on, ended_on),
    ).fetchone()
    if impact["total"]:
        raise conflict(f"修改日期会使 {impact['total']} 条台账超出该茬次范围（含回收站 {impact['trashed']} 条）。"
                       "请核对台账，保留能够覆盖这些作业日期的茬次起止日期后重试；未修改任何记录。")

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 44 页 / 共 184 页
def _update_season_row(
    conn: sqlite3.Connection, season_id: str, crop: str, started_on: str | None,
    ended_on: str | None, notes: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    current = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM plot_seasons WHERE id=?", (season_id,)).fetchone()
    if not current:
        raise not_found("茬次不存在。")
    _validate_farm_links(conn, current["user_id"], current["plot_id"])
    _validate_season_record_dates(conn, season_id, started_on, ended_on)
    try:
        conn.execute(
            """UPDATE plot_seasons SET crop=?, started_on=?, ended_on=?, notes=?, updated_at=? WHERE id=?""",
            (crop, started_on, ended_on, notes, now_iso(), season_id),
        )
    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:
        if exc.sqlite_errorcode == sqlite3.SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE and "plot_seasons.plot_id" in str(exc):
            raise conflict("该地块已有进行中的茬次，请刷新后重试。") from exc
        raise
    return public_season(dict(conn.execute("SELECT * FROM plot_seasons WHERE id=?", (season_id,)).fetchone()))
def update_season(
    season_id: str, crop: str, started_on: str | None, ended_on: str | None, notes: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        return _update_season_row(conn, season_id, crop, started_on, ended_on, notes)
def delete_season(season_id: str) -> None:
    """删掉一茬。它的作业记录变回「未归茬」，不连带删除。"""
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        current = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM plot_seasons WHERE id=?", (season_id,)).fetchone()
        if not current:
            raise not_found("茬次不存在。")
        _validate_farm_links(conn, current["user_id"], current["plot_id"])
        if conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM farm_records WHERE season_id=? AND deleted_at IS NOT NULL LIMIT 1", (season_id,)).fetchone():
            raise conflict("该茬次关联回收站台账，请先恢复或最终删除这些台账。")
        conn.execute("UPDATE farm_records SET season_id = NULL WHERE season_id = ?", (season_id,))
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM plot_seasons WHERE id = ?", (season_id,))
FARM_RECORD_SELECT = """
    SELECT r.*, COALESCE(p.name, '') AS plot_name, COALESCE(s.crop, '') AS season_crop
    FROM farm_records r
    LEFT JOIN plots p ON p.id = r.plot_id AND p.user_id=r.user_id
    LEFT JOIN plot_seasons s ON s.id = r.season_id AND s.plot_id=r.plot_id AND s.user_id=r.user_id
"""
def public_farm_record(record: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    raw_cost = record["cost"]
    raw_yield = record["yield_kg"]
    raw_price = record["unit_price"]
    raw_safe_days = record["safe_days"]
    return {
        "is_demo": bool(record.get("is_demo", False)),

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 45 页 / 共 184 页
        "id": record["id"],
        "plot_id": record["plot_id"],
        "plot_name": record["plot_name"],
        "kind": record["kind"],
        "happened_on": record["happened_on"],
        "crop": record["crop"],
        "season_id": record["season_id"],
        "season_crop": record["season_crop"],
        "material": record["material"],
        "detail": record["detail"],
        "quantity": record["quantity"],
        "cost": None if raw_cost is None else float(raw_cost),
        "safe_days": None if raw_safe_days is None else int(raw_safe_days),
        "earliest_harvest_on": add_days(record["happened_on"], int(raw_safe_days))
        if record["kind"] == "打药" and raw_safe_days is not None else None,
        "yield_kg": None if raw_yield is None else float(raw_yield),
        "unit_price": None if raw_price is None else float(raw_price),
        "created_at": record["created_at"],
    }
def _validate_farm_links(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: str, plot_id: str | None, season_id: str | None = None) -> None:
    """持有写锁后再核对引用，防止清空演示与保存交错产生已失效的茬次引用。"""
    if plot_id and not conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM plots WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND deleted_at IS NULL", (plot_id, user_id)).fetchone():
        raise conflict("关联地块已变化，请刷新后重新选择。")
    if season_id and not conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM plot_seasons WHERE id=? AND plot_id=? AND user_id=?", (season_id, plot_id, user_id)).fetchone():
        raise conflict("关联茬次已变化，请刷新后重新选择。")
def _resolve_record_season(
    conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: str, plot_id: str, season_id: str | None,
    happened_on: str, auto_select_season: bool,
) -> sqlite3.Row | None:
    """调用方已持有写锁；与茬次改期串行决定最终日期、归茬与默认作物。"""
    _validate_farm_links(conn, user_id, plot_id, season_id)
    if season_id is not None:
        season = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM plot_seasons WHERE id=? AND plot_id=? AND user_id=?",
                              (season_id, plot_id, user_id)).fetchone()
        if ((season["started_on"] and happened_on < season["started_on"])
                or (season["ended_on"] and happened_on > season["ended_on"])):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="作业日期不在所选茬次的起止日期内。")
        return season
    if not auto_select_season:
        return None
    candidates = conn.execute("""SELECT * FROM plot_seasons WHERE user_id=? AND plot_id=?
        AND (started_on IS NULL OR started_on<=?) AND (ended_on IS NULL OR ended_on>=?)""",
        (user_id, plot_id, happened_on, happened_on)).fetchall()
    if len(candidates) > 1 or (candidates and not candidates[0]["started_on"]):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="无法按日期唯一确定茬次，请选择具体茬次或明确选择未归茬。")
    return candidates[0] if candidates else None
def create_farm_record(
    user_id: str,
    plot_id: str,
    kind: str,

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 46 页 / 共 184 页
    happened_on: str,
    crop: str,
    detail: str,
    quantity: str,
    cost: float | None,
    material: str = "",
    safe_days: int | None = None,
    yield_kg: float | None = None,
    unit_price: float | None = None,
    season_id: str | None = None,
    record_id: str | None = None,
    auto_select_season: bool = False,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    record_id = record_id or uuid.uuid4().hex
    created_at = now_iso()
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        season = _resolve_record_season(conn, user_id, plot_id, season_id, happened_on, auto_select_season)
        season_id = season["id"] if season else None
        crop = crop or (season["crop"] if season else "")
        conn.execute(
            """
            INSERT INTO farm_records
                (id, user_id, plot_id, kind, happened_on, crop, material, detail, quantity,
                 cost, safe_days, yield_kg, unit_price, season_id, created_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            ON CONFLICT(id) DO NOTHING
            """,
            (
                record_id, user_id, plot_id, kind, happened_on, crop, material, detail, quantity,
                cost, safe_days, yield_kg, unit_price, season_id, created_at,
            ),
        )
        row = conn.execute(FARM_RECORD_SELECT + " WHERE r.id = ?", (record_id,)).fetchone()
        if row["deleted_at"] is not None:
            raise conflict("该次提交的台账已在回收站，请恢复已有台账，不要重复记账。")
        expected = dict(user_id=user_id, plot_id=plot_id, season_id=season_id, kind=kind,
                        happened_on=happened_on, crop=crop, detail=detail, quantity=quantity,
                        cost=cost, material=material, safe_days=safe_days, yield_kg=yield_kg, unit_price=unit_price)
        if any(row[key] != value for key, value in expected.items()):
            raise conflict("该次提交已保存且内容不同，请刷新台账后编辑已有记录。")
    return public_farm_record(dict(row))
RECORD_VISIBLE = "r.deleted_at IS NULL AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM plots p WHERE p.id=r.plot_id AND p.user_id=r.user_id AND p.deleted_at IS NULL)"
def get_farm_record(record_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    return _fetchone(FARM_RECORD_SELECT + " WHERE r.id = ? AND " + RECORD_VISIBLE, (record_id,))
def update_farm_record(record_id: str, user_id: str, **values: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """一次更新全部业务字段，保留记录 ID、用户和创建时间。"""
    columns = ("plot_id", "season_id", "kind", "happened_on", "crop", "detail", "quantity",
               "cost", "material", "safe_days", "yield_kg", "unit_price")
    with get_connection() as conn:

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 47 页 / 共 184 页
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        season = _resolve_record_season(conn, user_id, values["plot_id"], values["season_id"],
                                        values["happened_on"], values.get("auto_select_season", False))
        values["season_id"] = season["id"] if season else None
        values["crop"] = values["crop"] or (season["crop"] if season else "")
        try:
            result = conn.execute(
                "UPDATE farm_records SET " + ", ".join(f"{column} = ?" for column in columns)
                + " WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM plots p WHERE p.id=farm_records.plot_id AND p.deleted_at IS NULL)",
                tuple(values[column] for column in columns) + (record_id, user_id),
            )
        except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:
            if exc.sqlite_errorcode == sqlite3.SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_FOREIGNKEY:
                raise conflict("关联地块或茬次已变化，请刷新后重新选择。") from exc
            raise
        if result.rowcount == 0:
            raise not_found("农事记录不存在或已被删除。")
        row = conn.execute(FARM_RECORD_SELECT + " WHERE r.id = ?", (record_id,)).fetchone()
    return public_farm_record(dict(row))
ECONOMICS_SCOPES = {
    "all": "",
    "cost": "r.cost IS NOT NULL",
    "revenue": "r.kind = '采收' AND r.yield_kg IS NOT NULL AND r.unit_price IS NOT NULL",
    "missing_cost": "r.cost IS NULL",
    "incomplete_harvest": "r.kind = '采收' AND (r.yield_kg IS NULL OR r.unit_price IS NULL)",
}
def _farm_record_conditions(
    user_id: str, *, plot_id: str | None = None, season_id: str | None = None,
    kind: str | None = None, date_from: str | None = None, date_to: str | None = None,
    year: int | None = None, economics_scope: str = "all", data_scope: str = "all",
) -> tuple[list[str], list[Any]]:
    """统计与追溯台账共用同一组筛选条件；年度按作业日期划分。"""
    conditions = ["r.user_id = ?", RECORD_VISIBLE]
    params: list[Any] = [user_id]
    if data_scope != "all":
        conditions.append("r.is_demo = ?")
        params.append(int(data_scope == "demo"))
    for column, operator, value in (("plot_id", "=", plot_id), ("kind", "=", kind),
                                    ("happened_on", ">=", date_from), ("happened_on", "<=", date_to)):
        if value:
            conditions.append(f"r.{column} {operator} ?")
            params.append(value)
    if season_id == "unassigned":
        conditions.append("r.season_id IS NULL")
    elif season_id:
        conditions.append("r.season_id = ?")
        params.append(season_id)
    if year is not None:
        conditions.append("r.happened_on BETWEEN ? AND ?")
        params.extend([f"{year:04d}-01-01", f"{year:04d}-12-31"])

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 48 页 / 共 184 页
    scope_condition = ECONOMICS_SCOPES[economics_scope]
    if scope_condition:
        conditions.append(f"({scope_condition})")
    return conditions, params
def list_farm_records_page(
    user_id: str,
    *,
    plot_id: str | None = None,
    limit: int = 20,
    cursor: tuple[str, str] | None = None,
    kind: str | None = None,
    date_from: str | None = None,
    date_to: str | None = None,
    season_id: str | None = None,
    economics_scope: str = "all",
    year: int | None = None,
    data_scope: str = "all",
) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], bool]:
    """按作业日期倒序翻页；游标复用 (happened_on, id)。"""
    conditions, params = _farm_record_conditions(
        user_id, plot_id=plot_id, kind=kind, date_from=date_from, date_to=date_to,
        season_id=season_id, economics_scope=economics_scope, year=year, data_scope=data_scope,
    )
    if cursor:
        conditions.append("(r.happened_on < ? OR (r.happened_on = ? AND r.id < ?))")
        params.extend([cursor[0], cursor[0], cursor[1]])
    params.append(limit + 1)
    with get_connection() as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(
            FARM_RECORD_SELECT + " WHERE " + " AND ".join(conditions)
            + " ORDER BY r.happened_on DESC, r.id DESC LIMIT ?",
            tuple(params),
        ).fetchall()
    has_more = len(rows) > limit
    return [public_farm_record(dict(row)) for row in rows[:limit]], has_more
def delete_farm_record(record_id: str, user_id: str | None = None) -> None:
    from backend.app.repositories_trash import trash_record
    trash_record(record_id, user_id)
def export_farm_records(user_id: str, **filters: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """单次读取全部匹配数据，不带分页；关联信息也必须属于本人及该地块。"""
    conditions, params = _farm_record_conditions(user_id, **filters)
    with get_connection() as conn:
        rows = conn.execute("""
            SELECT r.*, p.name AS plot_name, s.crop AS season_crop,
                   s.started_on AS season_started_on, s.ended_on AS season_ended_on
            FROM farm_records r
            JOIN plots p ON p.id=r.plot_id AND p.user_id=r.user_id
            LEFT JOIN plot_seasons s ON s.id=r.season_id AND s.plot_id=r.plot_id AND s.user_id=r.user_id
            WHERE """ + " AND ".join(conditions) + " ORDER BY r.happened_on DESC, r.id DESC", tuple(params)).fetchall()
    return [dict(row) for row in rows]
