def count_farm_records(user_id: str) -> int:
    with get_connection() as conn:
        row = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM farm_records r WHERE r.user_id = ? AND " + RECORD_VISIBLE, (user_id,)).fetchone()
    return int(row["total"]) if row else 0
def list_harvest_safety(user_id: str, *, reference_date: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """按地块汇总截至查询日已登记的间隔期，取约束最晚的一条。
    提醒保守覆盖同一地块的全部茬次和作物，换茬或改作物不会解除尚未到期的
    提醒；未归茬记录也参与。这里只计算用户填入的天数，不判断标签合规性。
    """
    with get_connection() as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(
            """
            SELECT plot_id, plot_name, material, happened_on, safe_days FROM (
                SELECT
                    r.plot_id AS plot_id,
                    p.name AS plot_name,
                    r.material AS material,
                    r.happened_on AS happened_on,
                    r.safe_days AS safe_days,
                    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
                        PARTITION BY r.plot_id
                        ORDER BY date(r.happened_on, '+' || r.safe_days || ' days') DESC,
                                 r.happened_on DESC, r.id DESC
                    ) AS row_number
                FROM farm_records r
                JOIN plots p ON p.id = r.plot_id
                WHERE r.user_id = ? AND r.deleted_at IS NULL AND p.deleted_at IS NULL AND p.user_id=r.user_id AND r.kind = '打药' AND r.safe_days IS NOT NULL
                    AND r.happened_on <= ?
            )
            WHERE row_number = 1
            ORDER BY happened_on DESC
            """,
            (user_id, reference_date),
        ).fetchall()
    try:
        today = date.fromisoformat(reference_date)
    except ValueError:
        return []
    safety: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for row in rows:
        safe_days = int(row["safe_days"])
        earliest = add_days(row["happened_on"], safe_days)
        days_remaining = None
        if earliest:
            days_remaining = (date.fromisoformat(earliest) - today).days
        safety.append(
            {
                "plot_id": row["plot_id"],
                "plot_name": row["plot_name"],
                "material": row["material"],

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 50 页 / 共 184 页
                "happened_on": row["happened_on"],
                "safe_days": safe_days,
                "earliest_harvest_on": earliest,
                "days_remaining": days_remaining,
                "in_safe_window": days_remaining is not None and days_remaining > 0,
            }
        )
    return safety
def _economics_row(row: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """把一条汇总行补齐亩均指标。面积异常时亩均给 None，而不是让查询报除零。"""
    area = float(row["area_mu"])
    total_cost = float(row["total_cost"])
    total_yield = float(row["total_yield_kg"])
    total_revenue = float(row["total_revenue"])
    return {
        "is_demo": bool(row.get("is_demo", False)),
        "season_id": row["season_id"],
        "plot_id": row["plot_id"],
        "plot_name": row["plot_name"],
        "crop": row["crop"],
        "started_on": row["started_on"],
        "ended_on": row["ended_on"],
        "area_mu": area,
        "record_count": int(row["record_count"]),
        **{name: int(row[name]) for name in (
            "recorded_cost_count", "missing_cost_count", "harvest_count", "complete_harvest_count",
            "incomplete_harvest_count", "recorded_yield_count",
        )},
        "total_cost": round(total_cost, 2),
        "total_yield_kg": round(total_yield, 2),
        "total_revenue": round(total_revenue, 2),
        "net_revenue": round(total_revenue - total_cost, 2),
        "cost_per_mu": round(total_cost / area, 2) if area > 0 else None,
        "yield_per_mu": round(total_yield / area, 2) if area > 0 else None,
        "revenue_per_mu": round(total_revenue / area, 2) if area > 0 else None,
        "net_per_mu": round((total_revenue - total_cost) / area, 2) if area > 0 else None,
    }
def summarize_farm_economics(
    user_id: str, *, plot_id: str | None = None, season_id: str | None = None, year: int | None = None, data_scope: str = "all",
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """按茬次汇总投入与产出，未归茬的记录单独成行。
    升级前是按地块汇总的；有了茬次就按茬次算——否则种第二茬之后，上一茬的
    化肥钱会和这一茬的收成混在一起。明确未归茬或无法确定归属的记录单独
    分组，不丢数据。年度仅按作业日期过滤，金额字段保持数字并由完整度计数
    表达缺失，页面据此区分待补录与有效零值。
    """
    conditions, params = _farm_record_conditions(user_id, plot_id=plot_id, season_id=season_id, year=year, data_scope=data_scope)
    cte = "WITH filtered_records AS (SELECT r.* FROM farm_records r WHERE " + " AND ".join(conditions) + ") "
    season_conditions = ["s.user_id = ?", "p.deleted_at IS NULL"]
    season_params: list[Any] = [user_id]

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 51 页 / 共 184 页
    if plot_id:
        season_conditions.append("s.plot_id = ?")
        season_params.append(plot_id)
    if season_id == "unassigned":
        season_conditions.append("0 = 1")
    elif season_id:
        season_conditions.append("s.id = ?")
        season_params.append(season_id)
    if data_scope != "all":
        season_conditions.append("(s.is_demo = ? OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM filtered_records f WHERE f.season_id = s.id AND f.plot_id = s.plot_id))")
        season_params.append(int(data_scope == "demo"))
    quality_columns = """
        COUNT(r.cost) AS recorded_cost_count,
        SUM(CASE WHEN r.id IS NOT NULL AND r.cost IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS missing_cost_count,
        SUM(CASE WHEN r.kind = '采收' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS harvest_count,
        SUM(CASE WHEN r.kind = '采收' AND r.yield_kg IS NOT NULL AND r.unit_price IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS complete_harvest_count,
        SUM(CASE WHEN r.kind = '采收' AND (r.yield_kg IS NULL OR r.unit_price IS NULL) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS incomplete_harvest_count,
        SUM(CASE WHEN r.kind = '采收' AND r.yield_kg IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS recorded_yield_count,
    """
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN")
        season_rows = conn.execute(
            cte + """
            SELECT
                s.id AS season_id,
                s.is_demo AS is_demo,
                s.crop AS crop,
                s.started_on AS started_on,
                s.ended_on AS ended_on,
                p.id AS plot_id,
                p.name AS plot_name,
                p.area_mu AS area_mu,
                """ + quality_columns + """ COUNT(r.id) AS record_count,
                COALESCE(SUM(r.cost), 0) AS total_cost,
                COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN r.kind = '采收' THEN r.yield_kg END), 0) AS total_yield_kg,
                COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN r.kind = '采收' THEN r.yield_kg * r.unit_price END), 0) AS total_revenue
            FROM plot_seasons s
            JOIN plots p ON p.id = s.plot_id AND p.user_id = s.user_id
            LEFT JOIN filtered_records r ON r.season_id = s.id AND r.plot_id = p.id
            WHERE """ + " AND ".join(season_conditions) + """
            GROUP BY s.id, s.crop, s.started_on, s.ended_on, p.id, p.name, p.area_mu
            """ + (" HAVING COUNT(r.id) > 0 " if year is not None and not season_id else "")
            + " ORDER BY s.started_on DESC, s.id DESC",
            tuple(params + season_params),
        ).fetchall()
        unassigned_rows = conn.execute(
            cte + """
            SELECT
                NULL AS season_id,
                p.is_demo AS is_demo,

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 52 页 / 共 184 页
                '' AS crop,
                NULL AS started_on,
                NULL AS ended_on,
                p.id AS plot_id,
                p.name AS plot_name,
                p.area_mu AS area_mu,
                """ + quality_columns + """ COUNT(r.id) AS record_count,
                COALESCE(SUM(r.cost), 0) AS total_cost,
                COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN r.kind = '采收' THEN r.yield_kg END), 0) AS total_yield_kg,
                COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN r.kind = '采收' THEN r.yield_kg * r.unit_price END), 0) AS total_revenue
            FROM filtered_records r
            JOIN plots p ON p.id = r.plot_id AND p.user_id = r.user_id
            WHERE r.season_id IS NULL
            GROUP BY p.id, p.name, p.area_mu
            ORDER BY p.name ASC
            """,
            tuple(params),
        ).fetchall()
    return [_economics_row(dict(row)) for row in list(season_rows) + list(unassigned_rows)]
def list_farm_record_years(user_id: str, data_scope: str = "all") -> list[int]:
    with get_connection() as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT DISTINCT CAST(substr(happened_on, 1, 4) AS INTEGER) AS year "
            "FROM farm_records r WHERE user_id = ? AND " + RECORD_VISIBLE + " "
            + ("AND is_demo = ? " if data_scope != "all" else "") + "ORDER BY year DESC",
            (user_id, int(data_scope == "demo")) if data_scope != "all" else (user_id,),
        ).fetchall()
    return [row["year"] for row in rows if 1 <= row["year"] <= 9999]
import logging
from typing import Any
from backend.app.core.request_context import get_request_id
SENSITIVE_FIELDS = {
    "password",
    "password_hash",
    "token",
    "authorization",
    "api_key",
    "nonce",
    "cookie",
    "headers",
    "prompt",
    "response",
    "image_base64",
}
def _format_value(key: str, value: Any) -> str:
    if key.casefold() in SENSITIVE_FIELDS:
        return "[redacted]"
    if value is None:
        return "none"
    text = str(value).replace("\n", " ").replace("\r", " ").strip()

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 53 页 / 共 184 页
    return text[:160]
def log_event(logger: logging.Logger, event: str, **fields: Any) -> None:
    request_id = get_request_id()
    if request_id:
        fields.setdefault("request_id", request_id)
    ordered_fields = " ".join(f"{key}={_format_value(key, value)}" for key, value in sorted(fields.items()))
    if ordered_fields:
        logger.info("event=%s %s", event, ordered_fields)
    else:
        logger.info("event=%s", event)
from __future__ import annotations
import os
import sqlite3
from contextlib import closing
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from backend.app.core.database import SCHEMA_VERSION
CORE_TABLES = {"users", "auth_tokens"}
def validate_database(path: Path) -> int:
    if not path.is_file():
        raise ValueError(f"数据库文件不存在：{path.name}")
    try:
        with closing(sqlite3.connect(f"file:{path}?mode=ro", uri=True)) as conn:
            if conn.execute("PRAGMA integrity_check").fetchone()[0] != "ok":
                raise ValueError("数据库完整性检查失败。")
            version = int(conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version").fetchone()[0])
            tables = {row[0] for row in conn.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
    except sqlite3.Error as exc:
        raise ValueError("数据库文件无法打开或已经损坏。") from exc
    if version > SCHEMA_VERSION:
        raise ValueError(f"数据库 Schema 版本 {version} 高于当前支持版本 {SCHEMA_VERSION}。")
    if not CORE_TABLES.issubset(tables):
        raise ValueError("数据库缺少必要数据表。")
    return version
def create_backup(source: Path, backup_dir: Path, *, keep: int = 10) -> Path:
    validate_database(source)
    backup_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    timestamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ")
    destination = backup_dir / f"yunxun-{timestamp}.db"
    with closing(sqlite3.connect(source)) as source_conn, closing(sqlite3.connect(destination)) as destination_conn:
        source_conn.backup(destination_conn)
    validate_database(destination)
    backups = sorted(backup_dir.glob("yunxun-*.db"), key=lambda item: item.stat().st_mtime, reverse=True)
    for expired in backups[max(1, keep):]:
        expired.unlink()
    return destination
def restore_backup(current: Path, backup: Path, backup_dir: Path) -> Path:
    validate_database(backup)
    safety_backup = create_backup(current, backup_dir)
    temporary = current.with_suffix(current.suffix + ".restore.tmp")

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 54 页 / 共 184 页
    try:
        temporary.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        with closing(sqlite3.connect(backup)) as source_conn, closing(sqlite3.connect(temporary)) as destination_conn:
            source_conn.backup(destination_conn)
        validate_database(temporary)
        os.replace(temporary, current)
        current.with_name(current.name + "-wal").unlink(missing_ok=True)
        current.with_name(current.name + "-shm").unlink(missing_ok=True)
        validate_database(current)
    except Exception:
        temporary.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise
    return safety_backup
import os

def _getenv(name: str, default: str | None = None) -> str | None:
    value = os.getenv(name)
    if value is None:
        return default
    return value.strip()
def _format_bounds(minimum: int | float | None, maximum: int | float | None) -> str:
    parts: list[str] = []
    if minimum is not None:
        parts.append(f"不能小于 {minimum}")
    if maximum is not None:
        parts.append(f"不能大于 {maximum}")
    return "，".join(parts)
def _parse_int(
    name: str,
    value: str | None,
    *,
    default: int,
    minimum: int | None = None,
    maximum: int | None = None,
) -> int:
    if value is None:
        return default
    try:
        parsed = int(value.strip())
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} 必须是整数，当前值为 {value!r}。") from exc
    if minimum is not None and parsed < minimum:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} 配置无效：{parsed}，{_format_bounds(minimum, maximum)}。")
    if maximum is not None and parsed > maximum:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} 配置无效：{parsed}，{_format_bounds(minimum, maximum)}。")

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 55 页 / 共 184 页
    return parsed
def _parse_bool(name: str, value: str | None, *, default: bool = False) -> bool:
    if value is None:
        return default
    normalized = value.strip().lower()
    truthy = {"1", "true", "yes", "y", "on"}
    falsy = {"0", "false", "no", "n", "off"}
    if normalized in truthy:
        return True
    if normalized in falsy:
        return False
    raise ValueError(f"{name} 必须是布尔值，可用 true/false、yes/no、1/0，当前值为 {value!r}。")
def _parse_csv(name: str, value: str | None, fallback: list[str] | None = None) -> list[str]:
    fallback = fallback or []
    if value is None:
        return fallback
    items = [item.strip() for item in value.split(",")]
    normalized: list[str] = []
    for item in items:
        if item and item not in normalized:
            normalized.append(item)
    return normalized or fallback
def _parse_optional_str(name: str, value: str | None, *, default: str = "") -> str:
    if value is None:
        return default
    cleaned = value.strip()
    if "\x00" in cleaned:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} 不能包含空字符。")
    return cleaned
def _resolve_database_path(raw_database_url: str) -> str:
    if raw_database_url.startswith("sqlite:///"):
        raw_path = raw_database_url.removeprefix("sqlite:///")
    else:
        raw_path = raw_database_url
    path = Path(raw_path)
    if not path.is_absolute():
        path = PROJECT_ROOT / raw_path
    return str(path.resolve())

    @property
    def allowed_origins(self) -> list[str]:
        return [origin.lower() for origin in _parse_csv(
            "YUNXUN_ALLOWED_ORIGINS",
            self.allowed_origins_raw,
            [
                "http://localhost:5173",

    @property
    def cors_methods(self) -> list[str]:
        methods = _parse_csv("YUNXUN_CORS_METHODS", self.cors_methods_raw, ["GET", "POST", "PATCH", "DELETE", "OPTIONS"])
        return [method.upper() for method in methods]
    @property
    def cors_headers(self) -> list[str]:
        headers = _parse_csv(
            "YUNXUN_CORS_HEADERS",
            self.cors_headers_raw,
            ["Authorization", "Content-Type", "X-CSRF-Token"],
        )
        if "X-CSRF-Token" not in headers:
            headers.append("X-CSRF-Token")
        return headers

        return self.debug or self.environment != "production"

        return self.environment.strip().lower() == "production"
def validate_startup_settings(settings: Settings) -> None:
    environment = settings.environment.strip().lower()
    if not settings.jwt_secret.strip():
        raise ValueError("YUNXUN_JWT_SECRET 不能为空。")
    if environment == "production":
        if settings.jwt_secret == "change-me-in-production" or len(settings.jwt_secret) < 32:
            raise ValueError("生产环境的 YUNXUN_JWT_SECRET 必须是至少 32 字符的随机值。")
        if settings.debug:
            raise ValueError("生产环境禁止启用 YUNXUN_DEBUG。")
        if not settings.allowed_origins or "*" in settings.allowed_origins:
            raise ValueError("生产环境必须配置明确的 YUNXUN_ALLOWED_ORIGINS，禁止使用通配符。")
        if not settings.cookie_secure:
            raise ValueError("生产环境必须启用 YUNXUN_COOKIE_SECURE。")
    settings.normalized_log_level
@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
def get_settings() -> Settings:
    default_database_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "backend" / "yunxun.db"
    port = _parse_int("YUNXUN_PORT", _getenv("YUNXUN_PORT", _getenv("PORT", "8001")), default=8001, minimum=1, maximum=65535)
    token_hours = _parse_int("YUNXUN_TOKEN_EXPIRE_HOURS", _getenv("YUNXUN_TOKEN_EXPIRE_HOURS"), default=168, minimum=1, maximum=24 * 365)
    database_url = _getenv("YUNXUN_DATABASE_URL", f"sqlite:///{default_database_path}") or f"sqlite:///{default_database_path}"
    return Settings(
        app_name=_parse_optional_str("YUNXUN_APP_NAME", _getenv("YUNXUN_APP_NAME"), default="云寻智慧农业工作台软件"),
        app_version=_parse_optional_str("YUNXUN_APP_VERSION", _getenv("YUNXUN_APP_VERSION"), default="1.0.0"),
        environment=_parse_optional_str("YUNXUN_ENV", _getenv("YUNXUN_ENV"), default="development"),
        debug=_parse_bool("YUNXUN_DEBUG", _getenv("YUNXUN_DEBUG"), default=False),
        host=_parse_optional_str("YUNXUN_HOST", _getenv("YUNXUN_HOST"), default="0.0.0.0"),
        port=port,
        backend_url=_parse_optional_str("YUNXUN_BACKEND_URL", _getenv("YUNXUN_BACKEND_URL"), default=f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}"),
        jwt_secret=_parse_optional_str("YUNXUN_JWT_SECRET", _getenv("YUNXUN_JWT_SECRET"), default="change-me-in-production"),
        database_url=database_url,
        db_path=_resolve_database_path(_getenv("YUNXUN_DB_PATH", database_url) or database_url),
        allowed_origins_raw=_getenv("YUNXUN_ALLOWED_ORIGINS", "") or "",
        cors_methods_raw=_getenv("YUNXUN_CORS_METHODS", "GET,POST,PATCH,DELETE,OPTIONS") or "GET,POST,PATCH,DELETE,OPTIONS",
        cors_headers_raw=_getenv(
            "YUNXUN_CORS_HEADERS",
            "Authorization,Content-Type,X-CSRF-Token",
        )
        or "Authorization,Content-Type,X-CSRF-Token",
        requests_per_minute=_parse_int("YUNXUN_REQUESTS_PER_MINUTE", _getenv("YUNXUN_REQUESTS_PER_MINUTE"), default=20, minimum=1, maximum=600),
        auth_requests_per_minute=_parse_int("YUNXUN_AUTH_REQUESTS_PER_MINUTE", _getenv("YUNXUN_AUTH_REQUESTS_PER_MINUTE"), default=20, minimum=1, maximum=2000),
        auth_window_seconds=_parse_int("YUNXUN_AUTH_WINDOW_SECONDS", _getenv("YUNXUN_AUTH_WINDOW_SECONDS"), default=60, minimum=1, maximum=3600),
        token_hours=token_hours,
        log_level=_parse_optional_str("YUNXUN_LOG_LEVEL", _getenv("YUNXUN_LOG_LEVEL"), default="INFO"),
        cookie_secure=_parse_bool("YUNXUN_COOKIE_SECURE", _getenv("YUNXUN_COOKIE_SECURE"), default=False),
        cookie_same_site=_parse_optional_str("YUNXUN_COOKIE_SAME_SITE", _getenv("YUNXUN_COOKIE_SAME_SITE"), default="lax").lower(),
    )

import secrets
from fastapi import HTTPException, Request
CSRF_COOKIE_NAME = "yunxun_csrf"
CSRF_HEADER_NAME = "X-CSRF-Token"
SAFE_METHODS = {"GET", "HEAD", "OPTIONS"}
def create_csrf_token() -> str:
    return secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
def validate_csrf_request(request: Request) -> None:
    if request.method in SAFE_METHODS or request.url.path.startswith("/health"):
        return
    if request.headers.get("authorization", "").startswith("Bearer ") or not request.cookies.get("yunxun_session"):
        return
    cookie_token = request.cookies.get(CSRF_COOKIE_NAME)
    header_token = request.headers.get(CSRF_HEADER_NAME)
    if not cookie_token or not header_token or not secrets.compare_digest(cookie_token, header_token):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="CSRF 验证失败，请刷新页面后重试。")
import logging
import sqlite3
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Iterator
from fastapi import HTTPException
from backend.app.core.config import get_settings
logger = logging.getLogger("yunxun.backend.database")
SCHEMA_VERSION = 12
def get_db_path() -> Path:
    return Path(get_settings().db_path)
def ensure_parent_dir() -> None:
    get_db_path().parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
@contextmanager
def get_connection() -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:
    ensure_parent_dir()
    connection = sqlite3.connect(get_db_path(), timeout=10)
    connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    connection.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")
    connection.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 10000")
    try:
        yield connection
        connection.commit()
    except Exception:
        connection.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        connection.close()
def _table_columns(conn: sqlite3.Connection, table_name: str) -> set[str]:
    rows = conn.execute(f"PRAGMA table_info({table_name})").fetchall()
    return {row[1] for row in rows}
def _create_auth_tokens_table(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 59 页 / 共 184 页
    conn.execute(
        """
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS auth_tokens (
            token_hash TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            expires_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            FOREIGN KEY(user_id) REFERENCES users(id)
        )
        """
    )
def _ensure_auth_tokens_schema(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    columns = _table_columns(conn, "auth_tokens")
    if not columns:
        _create_auth_tokens_table(conn)
        return
    if "token_hash" not in columns:
        logger.warning("Invalidating legacy plaintext auth tokens during schema migration.")
        conn.execute("DROP TABLE auth_tokens")
        _create_auth_tokens_table(conn)
def _apply_schema_v1(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    statements = [
        """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, username TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
        password_hash TEXT NOT NULL, display_name TEXT NOT NULL, preferred_model TEXT NOT NULL,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL, updated_at TEXT NOT NULL)""",
        """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chat_sessions (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        title TEXT NOT NULL, feature TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'chat', model_name TEXT NOT NULL,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL, updated_at TEXT NOT NULL, FOREIGN KEY(user_id) REFERENCES users(id))""",
        """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chat_messages (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        role TEXT NOT NULL, content TEXT NOT NULL, created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        FOREIGN KEY(session_id) REFERENCES chat_sessions(id))""",
        """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS idempotency_requests (owner_id TEXT NOT NULL, key_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
        request_fingerprint TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(status IN ('in_flight','completed','failed')),
        lease_id TEXT NOT NULL, response_status INTEGER, response_body TEXT, created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL, expires_at TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(owner_id,key_hash))""",
        "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_chat_sessions_user_feature_updated ON chat_sessions(user_id,feature,updated_at DESC,id DESC)",
        "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_chat_messages_session_created ON chat_messages(session_id,created_at DESC,id DESC)",
        "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_idempotency_expires_at ON idempotency_requests(expires_at)",
    ]
    for statement in statements:
        conn.execute(statement)
    _ensure_auth_tokens_schema(conn)
    conn.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_auth_tokens_expires_at ON auth_tokens(expires_at)")

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS user_model_credentials (

            user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            provider TEXT NOT NULL,

def _apply_schema_v3(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    """Remove the user-managed model credential surface while preserving all user data."""
    if "user_model_credentials" in _table_names(conn):
        count = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM user_model_credentials").fetchone()[0]
        logger.info("Removing legacy user model credentials rows=%s", count)
    conn.execute("DROP INDEX IF EXISTS idx_chat_sessions_model_config")
    if "model_config_id" in _table_columns(conn, "chat_sessions"):
        conn.execute("ALTER TABLE chat_sessions DROP COLUMN model_config_id")
    conn.execute("DROP INDEX IF EXISTS idx_model_credentials_user_updated")
    conn.execute("DROP INDEX IF EXISTS idx_model_credentials_one_default")
    conn.execute("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS user_model_credentials")

def _apply_schema_v5(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    """Persist image diagnosis and farm decision records for the user stats panel."""
    conn.execute(
        """
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tool_records (
            id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            kind TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(kind IN ('vision','decision')),
            crop TEXT NOT NULL,
            payload TEXT,
            result TEXT NOT NULL,

    )
    conn.execute(

        "ON tool_records(user_id, kind, created_at DESC, id DESC)"

def _table_names(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> set[str]:
    rows = conn.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table'").fetchall()
    return {row[0] for row in rows}
def _apply_schema_v9(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    """新增茬次（种植季），并把地块上的当季作物与定植日期搬进茬次表。
    搬完就删掉 plots.crop / plots.planted_on：留着会和茬次表形成两个真相来源。
    该地块既有的作业记录一并归到这条茬次上，这样升级前后看到的投入产出口径一致
    （升级前本来就是按地块汇总的）。
    """
    conn.execute(
        """
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS plot_seasons (
            id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            plot_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            crop TEXT NOT NULL,
            started_on TEXT,
            ended_on TEXT,
            notes TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            FOREIGN KEY(user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
            FOREIGN KEY(plot_id) REFERENCES plots(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
        )
        """
    )

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 63 页 / 共 184 页
    conn.execute(
        "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_plot_seasons_user_plot_started "
        "ON plot_seasons(user_id, plot_id, started_on DESC, id DESC)"
    )
    conn.execute(
        "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_plot_seasons_one_active "
        "ON plot_seasons(plot_id) WHERE ended_on IS NULL"
    )
    if "season_id" not in _table_columns(conn, "farm_records"):
        conn.execute("ALTER TABLE farm_records ADD COLUMN season_id TEXT")
    conn.execute(
        "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_farm_records_season_created "
        "ON farm_records(season_id, happened_on DESC, id DESC)"
    )
    plot_columns = _table_columns(conn, "plots")
    if "crop" in plot_columns:
        timestamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        rows = conn.execute("SELECT id, user_id, crop, planted_on FROM plots").fetchall()
        migrated = 0
        for row in rows:
            plot_id, user_id, crop, planted_on = row[0], row[1], row[2], row[3]
            if not (crop or "").strip():
                continue
            season_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO plot_seasons
                    (id, user_id, plot_id, crop, started_on, ended_on, notes, created_at, updated_at)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, NULL, '', ?, ?)
                """,
                (season_id, user_id, plot_id, crop.strip(), planted_on, timestamp, timestamp),
            )
            conn.execute("UPDATE farm_records SET season_id = ? WHERE plot_id = ?", (season_id, plot_id))
            migrated += 1
        if migrated:
            logger.info("Migrated %s plot crops into plot_seasons.", migrated)
        conn.execute("ALTER TABLE plots DROP COLUMN crop")
        conn.execute("ALTER TABLE plots DROP COLUMN planted_on")

def _apply_schema_v6(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    """移除 AI 能力：删除会话、消息与幂等表，并去掉用户模型偏好字段。"""
    conn.execute("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS chat_messages")
    conn.execute("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS chat_sessions")
    conn.execute("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS idempotency_requests")
    if "preferred_model" in _table_columns(conn, "users"):
        logger.info("Removing users.preferred_model during schema migration.")
        conn.execute("ALTER TABLE users DROP COLUMN preferred_model")
def _apply_schema_v10(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    """仅增加来源和引导状态，既有账号和业务数据均按真实数据保留。"""
    for table, columns in {
        "users": ("is_guest", "onboarding_skipped"),
        "plots": ("is_demo",), "plot_seasons": ("is_demo",),
        "farm_records": ("is_demo",), "farm_tasks": ("is_demo",),
    }.items():
        for column in columns:
            if column not in _table_columns(conn, table):
                conn.execute(f"ALTER TABLE {table} ADD COLUMN {column} INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0")
    conn.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS demo_data_state (
            user_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            loaded INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
            FOREIGN KEY(user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
        )
    """)
def migrate_schema(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    current = int(conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version").fetchone()[0])
    starting_version = current
    if current > SCHEMA_VERSION:
        raise sqlite3.DatabaseError(

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 65 页 / 共 184 页
            f"数据库 Schema 版本 {current} 高于当前代码支持的 {SCHEMA_VERSION}。"
        )
    applied: list[str] = []
    if current < 1:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        try:
            _apply_schema_v1(conn)
            conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version = 1")
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        applied.append("1_initial_secure_schema")
        current = 1
    if current < 2:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        try:
            _apply_schema_v2(conn)
            conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version = 2")
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        applied.append("2_user_model_credentials")
        current = 2
    if current < 3:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        try:
            _apply_schema_v3(conn)
            conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version = 3")
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        applied.append("3_remove_user_model_credentials")
        current = 3
    if current < 4:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        try:
            _apply_schema_v4(conn)
            conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version = 4")
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        applied.append("4_session_pinning")
        current = 4
    if current < 5:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        try:

                conn.execute(f"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_{table}_user_deleted ON {table}(user_id, deleted_at)")

def init_db() -> None:
    db_path = get_db_path()
    try:
        ensure_parent_dir()
        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, timeout=10)
        try:
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")
            conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 10000")
            current, applied = migrate_schema(conn)

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 68 页 / 共 184 页
            conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL")
            conn.commit()
        finally:
            conn.close()
    except OSError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"数据库路径不可写：{db_path}") from exc
    except sqlite3.Error as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="数据库初始化失败，请检查 SQLite 文件权限。") from exc
    logger.info(
        "SQLite database initialized: %s schema_before=%s schema_target=%s migrations=%s",
        db_path, current, SCHEMA_VERSION, ",".join(applied) or "none",
    )
"""统一异常与错误码体系。
把过去散落在各处的 ``raise HTTPException(detail=...)`` 收敛为一套带稳定
机器可读 ``code`` 的领域异常。这样前端可以按 ``code`` 做精确分支，而不是
只能猜测 HTTP 状态码；同时把面向用户的话术集中在一处维护。
``AppError`` 继承自 ``HTTPException``，因此：
* 现有 ``assertRaises(HTTPException)`` 形式的单测无需改动仍然成立；
* 仍由 ``exceptions.http_exception_handler`` 统一处理，只需在该处理器里
  读取 ``code`` 字段并入参到响应体即可，不破坏既有错误载荷结构。
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from fastapi import HTTPException
class ErrorCode:
    """稳定的错误码常量。
    命名遵循 ``DOMAIN_REASON`` 约定。新增场景时只在这里追加常量，避免
    在调用处出现魔法字符串。
    """
    AUTH_REQUIRED = "AUTH_REQUIRED"
    GUEST_ALREADY_UPGRADED = "GUEST_ALREADY_UPGRADED"
    CONFLICT = "CONFLICT"
    NOT_FOUND = "NOT_FOUND"
    BAD_REQUEST = "BAD_REQUEST"
    RATE_LIMITED = "RATE_LIMITED"
class AppError(HTTPException):
    """携带稳定 ``code`` 的领域异常。
    与普通 ``HTTPException`` 的区别：响应体里会多一个 ``code`` 字段（见
    :func:`backend.app.core.exceptions.error_response`），其余行为完全一致，
    所以老调用方和测试都不受影响。
    """
    code: str = ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST
    def __init__(self, *, code: str, message: str, status_code: int) -> None:
        super().__init__(status_code=status_code, detail=message)
        self.code = code
        self.message = message
def rate_limited(retry_after: int) -> AppError:
    error = AppError(
        code=ErrorCode.RATE_LIMITED,
        message=f"请求太频繁了，请 {retry_after} 秒后再试。",
        status_code=429,

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 69 页 / 共 184 页
    )
    error.headers = {"Retry-After": str(retry_after)}
    return error
def conflict(message: str) -> AppError:
    """状态冲突：例如地块已有进行中的茬次，不能同时开两茬。"""
    return AppError(code=ErrorCode.CONFLICT, message=message, status_code=409)
def not_found(message: str = "资源不存在或已被删除。") -> AppError:
    return AppError(code=ErrorCode.NOT_FOUND, message=message, status_code=404)
import logging
from typing import Any
from fastapi import HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from backend.app.core.errors import AppError
logger = logging.getLogger("yunxun.backend")
def success_payload(**payload: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"success": True, **payload}
def error_response(message: str, status_code: int, *, code: str | None = None) -> JSONResponse:
    """构造统一的错误响应。
    ``code`` 仅在 ``AppError``（或显式传入）时出现，普通错误载荷保持
    ``{"success": False, "error": message}`` 的历史结构，确保向后兼容。
    """
    body: dict[str, Any] = {"success": False, "error": message}
    if code:
        body["code"] = code
    return JSONResponse(status_code=status_code, content=body)
async def http_exception_handler(_: Request, exc: HTTPException) -> JSONResponse:
    detail = exc.detail if isinstance(exc.detail, str) else "请求处理失败。"
    code = getattr(exc, "code", None)
    response = error_response(detail, exc.status_code, code=code)
    if exc.headers:
        response.headers.update(exc.headers)
    return response
async def validation_exception_handler(_: Request, exc: RequestValidationError) -> JSONResponse:
    first_error = exc.errors()[0] if exc.errors() else {}
    field_name = ".".join(str(item) for item in first_error.get("loc", [])[1:]) or "参数"
    message = first_error.get("msg", "参数格式不正确。")
    return error_response(f"{field_name}: {message}", 422)
async def unexpected_exception_handler(_: Request, exc: Exception) -> JSONResponse:
    logger.exception("Unhandled application error", exc_info=exc)
    return error_response("服务暂时不可用，请稍后重试。", 500)
"""分页工具。
列表接口统一使用基于游标（cursor）的翻页方式：把上一页最后一条记录的
``(created_at, id)`` 编码成一个不透明字符串交给客户端，下一页请求带回来
即可继续向后读取。相比页码分页，游标分页在数据持续写入时不会出现重复或
漏读。
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import base64
import json

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 70 页 / 共 184 页
def encode_cursor(created_at: str, item_id: str) -> str:
    raw = json.dumps([created_at, item_id], separators=(",", ":")).encode()
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(raw).decode().rstrip("=")
def decode_cursor(value: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    try:
        raw = base64.urlsafe_b64decode(value + "=" * (-len(value) % 4))
        created_at, item_id = json.loads(raw)
        if not isinstance(created_at, str) or not isinstance(item_id, str) or not created_at or not item_id:
            raise ValueError
        return created_at, item_id
    except (ValueError, TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise ValueError("cursor 无效。") from exc
import time
from collections import deque
from dataclasses import dataclass
from threading import RLock
from typing import Callable, Deque
from backend.app.core.errors import rate_limited
TimeProvider = Callable[[], float]
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RateLimitResult:
    key: str
    allowed: bool
    limit: int
    remaining: int
    reset_after_seconds: float
@dataclass
class RateLimitBucket:
    attempts: Deque[float]
    window_seconds: float
    def prune(self, now: float) -> int:
        removed = 0
        while self.attempts and now - self.attempts[0] >= self.window_seconds:
            self.attempts.popleft()
            removed += 1
        return removed
    def reset_after(self, now: float) -> float:
        if not self.attempts:
            return 0.0
        return max(0.0, self.window_seconds - (now - self.attempts[0]))
class InMemoryRateLimiter:
    def __init__(self, time_provider: TimeProvider | None = None) -> None:
        self._time_provider = time_provider or time.monotonic
        self._buckets: dict[str, RateLimitBucket] = {}
        self._lock = RLock()
    def check(self, key: str, limit: int, window_seconds: float = 60.0) -> RateLimitResult:
        self._validate_request(key, limit, window_seconds)
        with self._lock:
            now = self._time_provider()
            bucket = self._buckets.get(key)

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 71 页 / 共 184 页
            if bucket is None:
                bucket = RateLimitBucket(attempts=deque(), window_seconds=window_seconds)
                self._buckets[key] = bucket
            else:
                bucket.window_seconds = window_seconds
            bucket.prune(now)
            if len(bucket.attempts) >= limit:
                reset_after = bucket.reset_after(now)
                raise rate_limited(max(1, int(reset_after)))
            bucket.attempts.append(now)
            remaining = max(0, limit - len(bucket.attempts))
            return RateLimitResult(
                key=key,
                allowed=True,
                limit=limit,
                remaining=remaining,
                reset_after_seconds=bucket.reset_after(now),
            )
    def cleanup(self, window_seconds: float | None = None) -> int:
        with self._lock:
            now = self._time_provider()
            removed_keys: list[str] = []
            for key, bucket in self._buckets.items():
                if window_seconds is not None and not bucket.attempts:
                    bucket.window_seconds = window_seconds
                bucket.prune(now)
                if not bucket.attempts:
                    removed_keys.append(key)
            for key in removed_keys:
                self._buckets.pop(key, None)
            return len(removed_keys)
    def reset(self, key: str | None = None) -> bool:
        with self._lock:
            if key is None:
                had_entries = bool(self._buckets)
                self._buckets.clear()
                return had_entries
            return self._buckets.pop(key, None) is not None
    def snapshot(self) -> dict[str, list[float]]:
        with self._lock:
            return {key: list(bucket.attempts) for key, bucket in self._buckets.items()}
    @staticmethod
    def _validate_request(key: str, limit: int, window_seconds: float) -> None:
        if not key or not key.strip():
            raise ValueError("rate limit key 不能为空。")
        if limit <= 0:
            raise ValueError("rate limit limit 必须大于 0。")
        if window_seconds <= 0:
            raise ValueError("rate limit window_seconds 必须大于 0。")

_REQUEST_ID = ContextVar("request_id", default="")
_SAFE_REQUEST_ID = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9._-]{8,64}$")
def normalize_request_id(value: str | None) -> str:
    candidate = (value or "").strip()
    return candidate if _SAFE_REQUEST_ID.fullmatch(candidate) else uuid.uuid4().hex

    return _REQUEST_ID.set(value)
def reset_request_id(token) -> None:
    _REQUEST_ID.reset(token)
def get_request_id() -> str:
    return _REQUEST_ID.get()

def build_runtime_warnings(settings: Settings) -> list[str]:
    warnings: list[str] = []
    if settings.jwt_secret == "change-me-in-production":
        warnings.append("本机安全设置未完成（仍在使用默认密钥），正式启用前请联系部署人员。")
    if not settings.allowed_origins_raw.strip():
        warnings.append("本机安全设置未完成（未指定允许访问的来源），局域网访问前请联系部署人员。")
    return warnings

        "app_name": settings.app_name,
        "app_version": settings.app_version,
        "environment": settings.environment,
        "debug": settings.debug,
        "host": settings.host,
        "port": settings.port,
        "backend_url": settings.backend_url,
        "database_path": settings.db_path,
        "allowed_origins": settings.allowed_origins,
        "requests_per_minute": settings.requests_per_minute,
        "token_hours": settings.token_hours,
        "warnings": build_runtime_warnings(settings),
    }
def log_runtime_status(logger: logging.Logger, settings: Settings) -> None:
    status = build_runtime_status(settings)
    for name, configured in (("YUNXUN_JWT_SECRET", settings.jwt_secret != "change-me-in-production"),
                             ("YUNXUN_ALLOWED_ORIGINS", bool(settings.allowed_origins_raw.strip()))):
        if not configured:
            logger.warning("Runtime config gap: %s is not set for production use.", name)
    logger.info(
        "Runtime status: environment=%s debug=%s host=%s port=%s database=%s origins=%s",
        status["environment"],
        status["debug"],
        status["host"],

import hashlib
import hmac
import secrets
from backend.app.core.config import get_settings
def hash_password(password: str, salt: str | None = None) -> str:
    salt_value = salt or secrets.token_hex(16)
    digest = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac("sha256", password.encode("utf-8"), salt_value.encode("utf-8"), 120000)
    return f"{salt_value}${digest.hex()}"
def verify_password(password: str, password_hash: str) -> bool:
    salt, _ = password_hash.split("$", 1)
    return secrets.compare_digest(hash_password(password, salt), password_hash)
def hash_recovery_code(code: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(b"yunxun:recovery:v1:" + code.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
def create_token() -> str:
    return secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
def hash_auth_token(raw_token: str) -> str:
    secret = get_settings().jwt_secret.encode("utf-8")
    return hmac.new(secret, raw_token.encode("utf-8"), hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()
def session_reference(raw_token: str) -> str:
    secret = get_settings().jwt_secret.encode("utf-8")
    return hmac.new(secret, b"yunxun:logout:v1:" + raw_token.encode("utf-8"), hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()
def safe_fingerprint(value: str) -> str:
    secret = get_settings().jwt_secret.encode("utf-8")
    return hmac.new(secret, value.encode("utf-8"), hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()[:12]

from datetime import datetime, timezone
def now_utc() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)
def now_iso() -> str:
    return now_utc().isoformat(timespec="seconds")

"""凭据变更与会话撤销共用 SQLite 写事务；不修改用户 ID 或业务数据。"""
import secrets
import sqlite3
from fastapi import HTTPException
from backend.app.core.database import get_connection
from backend.app.core.security import hash_password, hash_recovery_code, verify_password
from backend.app.core.time import now_iso
INVALID_RECOVERY = "账号或恢复凭据无效，请核对后重试；无法恢复时请联系部署者。"
def replace_password_and_revoke_sessions(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: str, password_hash: str) -> None:
    conn.execute("UPDATE users SET password_hash=?, recovery_code_hash=NULL, updated_at=? WHERE id=?",
                 (password_hash, now_iso(), user_id))
    conn.execute("DELETE FROM auth_tokens WHERE user_id=?", (user_id,))
def manage_credentials(user: dict, current_password: str, *, new_password: str | None = None) -> str | None:

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 74 页 / 共 184 页
    code = secrets.token_urlsafe(32) if new_password is None else None
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        current = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id=?", (user["id"],)).fetchone()
        if not current or current["is_guest"]:
            raise HTTPException(403, "请先将访客保存为正式账号，再管理密码。")
        if not secrets.compare_digest(current["password_hash"], user["password_hash"]) or not verify_password(current_password, current["password_hash"]):
            raise HTTPException(400, "原密码不正确，或登录状态已变化，请重新登录后重试。")
        if new_password is not None:
            if current_password == new_password:
                raise HTTPException(400, "新密码不能与原密码相同。")
            replace_password_and_revoke_sessions(conn, user["id"], hash_password(new_password))
        else:
            conn.execute("UPDATE users SET recovery_code_hash=?, updated_at=? WHERE id=?",
                         (hash_recovery_code(code), now_iso(), user["id"]))
    return code
def recover_password(username: str, recovery_code: str, new_password: str) -> str:
    digest = hash_recovery_code(recovery_code.strip())
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        current = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM users WHERE username=?", (username.strip().lower(),)).fetchone()
        expected = current["recovery_code_hash"] if current and current["recovery_code_hash"] else "0" * 64
        matches = secrets.compare_digest(digest, expected)
        if not matches or not current or current["is_guest"] or not current["recovery_code_hash"]:
            raise HTTPException(400, INVALID_RECOVERY)
        replace_password_and_revoke_sessions(conn, current["id"], hash_password(new_password))
        user_id = current["id"]
    return user_id

def onboarding_status(user_id: str) -> dict:
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN")
        user = conn.execute("SELECT onboarding_skipped FROM users WHERE id = ?", (user_id,)).fetchone()
        if not user:
            raise not_found("用户不存在。")
        plot = conn.execute("SELECT id FROM plots WHERE user_id = ? AND is_demo = 0 AND deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY created_at, id LIMIT 1", (user_id,)).fetchone()
        season = conn.execute("""SELECT s.id FROM plot_seasons s JOIN plots p ON p.id = s.plot_id
            WHERE s.user_id = ? AND p.user_id = ? AND p.deleted_at IS NULL AND s.is_demo = 0 AND p.is_demo = 0 LIMIT 1""", (user_id, user_id)).fetchone()
        record = conn.execute("""SELECT r.id FROM farm_records r JOIN plots p ON p.id = r.plot_id
            WHERE r.user_id = ? AND p.user_id = ? AND p.deleted_at IS NULL AND r.deleted_at IS NULL AND r.is_demo = 0 AND p.is_demo = 0 LIMIT 1""", (user_id, user_id)).fetchone()
        state = conn.execute("SELECT loaded FROM demo_data_state WHERE user_id = ?", (user_id,)).fetchone()
        demo_count = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM plots WHERE user_id=? AND is_demo=1 AND deleted_at IS NULL", (user_id,)).fetchone()[0]
        for table in ("plot_seasons", "farm_records", "farm_tasks"):
            join = "LEFT JOIN" if table == "farm_tasks" else "JOIN"

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 75 页 / 共 184 页

            demo_count += conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {table} x {join} plots p ON p.id=x.plot_id AND p.user_id=x.user_id WHERE x.user_id=? AND x.is_demo=1 AND {visible}"
                + (" AND x.deleted_at IS NULL" if table == "farm_records" else ""), (user_id,)).fetchone()[0]
    return {"plot_done": plot is not None, "season_done": season is not None, "record_done": record is not None,
            "first_plot_id": plot["id"] if plot else None, "skipped": bool(user["onboarding_skipped"]),
            "demo_loaded": bool(state and state["loaded"]), "demo_count": demo_count}

def load_demo_data(user_id: str) -> bool:
    """返回是否已加载。写锁覆盖状态判断、种子创建与标记，失败全部回滚。"""
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        user = conn.execute("SELECT is_guest FROM users WHERE id = ?", (user_id,)).fetchone()
        if not user or not user["is_guest"]:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="仅访客可加载演示数据。")
        state = conn.execute("SELECT loaded FROM demo_data_state WHERE user_id = ?", (user_id,)).fetchone()
        if state and state["loaded"]:
            return True
        timestamp = now_iso()
        today = date.today()
        day = lambda offset: (today + timedelta(days=offset)).isoformat()
        plot_id, season_id = uuid.uuid4().hex, uuid.uuid4().hex
        note = "演示数据，仅用于了解功能，不代表实际经营成果。"
        conn.execute("""INSERT INTO plots
            (id,user_id,name,area_mu,soil_type,irrigation,notes,created_at,updated_at,is_demo)
            VALUES (?,?,?,2,'壤土','滴灌',?,?,?,1)""", (plot_id, user_id, "演示菜地", note, timestamp, timestamp))
        conn.execute("""INSERT INTO plot_seasons
            (id,user_id,plot_id,crop,started_on,notes,created_at,updated_at,is_demo)
            VALUES (?,?,?,'番茄',?,?,?,?,1)""", (season_id, user_id, plot_id, day(-100), note, timestamp, timestamp))
        for kind, offset, cost, quantity, yield_kg, price in (
            ("播种", -95, 120, "示例苗款", None, None),
            ("施肥", -60, 80, "示例肥料", None, None),
            ("采收", -1, 0, "", 300, 2),
        ):
            conn.execute("""INSERT INTO farm_records
                (id,user_id,plot_id,season_id,kind,happened_on,crop,detail,quantity,cost,yield_kg,unit_price,created_at,is_demo)
                VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,'番茄',?,?,?,?,?,?,1)""",
                (uuid.uuid4().hex, user_id, plot_id, season_id, kind, day(offset), note, quantity, cost, yield_kg, price, timestamp))
        for title, offset in (("演示·复查墒情", -1), ("演示·整理采收账目", 0)):
            conn.execute("""INSERT INTO farm_tasks (id,user_id,plot_id,title,due_on,notes,created_at,is_demo)
                VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,1)""", (uuid.uuid4().hex, user_id, plot_id, title, day(offset), note, timestamp))
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO demo_data_state (user_id,loaded) VALUES (?,1) ON CONFLICT(user_id) DO UPDATE SET loaded=1", (user_id,))
    return False

        for table in ("plot_seasons", "farm_records", "farm_tasks"):

                raise conflict("演示关联资料仍在回收站，请先处理回收站，再清空演示数据。")
        for table in ("farm_records", "farm_tasks"):
            removed[table] = conn.execute(f"DELETE FROM {table} WHERE user_id = ? AND is_demo = 1", (user_id,)).rowcount
        removed["plot_seasons"] = conn.execute("""DELETE FROM plot_seasons WHERE user_id = ? AND is_demo = 1
            AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM farm_records r WHERE r.season_id = plot_seasons.id)""", (user_id,)).rowcount
        retained["plot_seasons"] = conn.execute("UPDATE plot_seasons SET is_demo=0 WHERE user_id=? AND is_demo=1", (user_id,)).rowcount
        removed["plots"] = conn.execute("""DELETE FROM plots WHERE user_id = ? AND is_demo = 1
            AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM farm_records r WHERE r.plot_id = plots.id)
            AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM farm_tasks t WHERE t.plot_id = plots.id)
            AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM plot_seasons s WHERE s.plot_id = plots.id)""", (user_id,)).rowcount
        retained["plots"] = conn.execute("UPDATE plots SET is_demo=0 WHERE user_id=? AND is_demo=1", (user_id,)).rowcount
        conn.execute("UPDATE demo_data_state SET loaded=0 WHERE user_id=?", (user_id,))
    return {"removed": removed, "retained": retained}
"""两类业务对象的回收站；地块子项继承父项可见性，不复制历史快照。"""
import hashlib
import json
import sqlite3
from backend.app.core.database import get_connection
from backend.app.core.errors import conflict, not_found
from backend.app.core.time import now_utc
def _owned(conn, table, entity_id, user_id):
    row = conn.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {table} WHERE id=?", (entity_id,)).fetchone()
    if not row or (user_id is not None and row["user_id"] != user_id):
        raise not_found("资料不存在或已最终删除。")
    return row
def _impact(conn, plot):
    rows = {}
    for table, fields in (("plot_seasons", "id,updated_at"), ("farm_records", "id,deleted_at"), ("farm_tasks", "id,done_at")):
        rows[table] = conn.execute(f"SELECT {fields} FROM {table} WHERE plot_id=? AND user_id=? ORDER BY id", (plot["id"], plot["user_id"])).fetchall()
    revision = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps([plot["id"], plot["deleted_at"], plot["updated_at"],
        *[[tuple(row) for row in rows[table]] for table in rows]], ensure_ascii=True).encode()).hexdigest()
    return {"season_count": len(rows["plot_seasons"]), "record_count": len(rows["farm_records"]),
        "trashed_record_count": sum(row["deleted_at"] is not None for row in rows["farm_records"]),
        "task_count": len(rows["farm_tasks"]), "open_task_count": sum(row["done_at"] is None for row in rows["farm_tasks"]),
        "revision": revision}
def _check_revision(expected, actual):
    if expected != actual:
        raise conflict("资料或关联数量已变化，请刷新后重新核对删除范围。")
def _record_revision(record):
    return hashlib.sha256(f"{record['id']}:{record['deleted_at']}".encode()).hexdigest()
def _check_plot_links(conn, plot):
    for table in ("plot_seasons", "farm_records", "farm_tasks"):
        if conn.execute(f"SELECT 1 FROM {table} WHERE plot_id=? AND user_id<>? LIMIT 1", (plot["id"], plot["user_id"])).fetchone():
            raise conflict("地块关联资料的归属异常，未修改数据，请联系部署者核对。")

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 77 页 / 共 184 页
    if conn.execute("""SELECT 1 FROM farm_records r JOIN plot_seasons s ON s.id=r.season_id
        WHERE s.plot_id=? AND (r.plot_id<>s.plot_id OR r.user_id<>s.user_id) LIMIT 1""", (plot["id"],)).fetchone():
        raise conflict("茬次存在范围外的台账关联，未修改数据，请联系部署者核对。")
def plot_delete_impact(plot_id: str, user_id: str) -> dict:
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN")
        plot = _owned(conn, "plots", plot_id, user_id)
        if plot["deleted_at"] is not None:
            raise not_found("地块已在回收站，请到回收站查看。")
        return _impact(conn, plot)
def trash_plot(plot_id: str, user_id: str | None, expected_revision: str | None = None) -> dict:
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        plot = _owned(conn, "plots", plot_id, user_id)
        if plot["deleted_at"] is not None:
            raise not_found("地块已移入回收站。")
        _check_plot_links(conn, plot)
        impact = _impact(conn, plot)
        if expected_revision is not None:
            _check_revision(expected_revision, impact["revision"])
        conn.execute("UPDATE plots SET deleted_at=? WHERE id=?", (now_utc().isoformat(timespec="microseconds"), plot_id))
    return impact
def trash_record(record_id: str, user_id: str | None) -> None:
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        record = _owned(conn, "farm_records", record_id, user_id)
        plot = _owned(conn, "plots", record["plot_id"], record["user_id"])
        if record["deleted_at"] is not None or plot["deleted_at"] is not None:
            raise not_found("台账或所属地块已移入回收站。")
        conn.execute("UPDATE farm_records SET deleted_at=? WHERE id=?", (now_utc().isoformat(timespec="microseconds"), record_id))
def list_trash(user_id: str) -> dict:
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN")
        plots = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM plots WHERE user_id=? AND deleted_at IS NOT NULL ORDER BY deleted_at DESC,id DESC", (user_id,)).fetchall()
        records = conn.execute("""SELECT r.*,p.name AS plot_name,p.deleted_at AS parent_deleted_at
            FROM farm_records r JOIN plots p ON p.id=r.plot_id AND p.user_id=r.user_id
            WHERE r.user_id=? AND r.deleted_at IS NOT NULL ORDER BY r.deleted_at DESC,r.id DESC""", (user_id,)).fetchall()
        return {"plots": [{"id": row["id"], "name": row["name"], "is_demo": bool(row["is_demo"]), "deleted_at": row["deleted_at"], **_impact(conn, row)} for row in plots],
            "records": [{key: row[key] for key in ("id", "plot_id", "plot_name", "kind", "happened_on", "deleted_at", "parent_deleted_at")}
                        | {"is_demo": bool(row["is_demo"]), "revision": _record_revision(row)} for row in records],
            "retention": "保留至你主动最终删除；当前没有自动清理。"}
def restore_plot(plot_id: str, user_id: str) -> bool:
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        plot = _owned(conn, "plots", plot_id, user_id)
        if plot["deleted_at"] is None:
            return False
        _check_plot_links(conn, plot)
        if conn.execute("""SELECT 1 FROM farm_records r WHERE r.plot_id=? AND r.deleted_at IS NULL AND r.season_id IS NOT NULL
            AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM plot_seasons s WHERE s.id=r.season_id AND s.plot_id=r.plot_id AND s.user_id=r.user_id) LIMIT 1""", (plot_id,)).fetchone():

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 78 页 / 共 184 页
            raise conflict("台账茬次关联异常，未恢复数据，请联系部署者核对。")
        active = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM plot_seasons WHERE plot_id=? AND ended_on IS NULL", (plot_id,)).fetchone()[0]
        if active > 1:
            raise conflict("检测到多个进行中茬次，未恢复或覆盖数据；请联系部署者核对后再试。")
        try:
            conn.execute("UPDATE plots SET deleted_at=NULL WHERE id=?", (plot_id,))
        except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:
            raise conflict("恢复遇到数据约束冲突，未覆盖现有资料，请核对后重试。") from exc
    return True
def restore_record(record_id: str, user_id: str) -> bool:
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        record = _owned(conn, "farm_records", record_id, user_id)
        plot = _owned(conn, "plots", record["plot_id"], user_id)
        if plot["deleted_at"] is not None:
            raise conflict("请先恢复所属地块，再恢复这条单独删除的台账。")
        if record["deleted_at"] is None:
            return False
        if record["season_id"]:
            season = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM plot_seasons WHERE id=? AND plot_id=? AND user_id=?", (record["season_id"], record["plot_id"], user_id)).fetchone()
            if not season:
                raise conflict("所属茬次关联已变化，未恢复台账，请先核对茬次资料。")
            if ((season["started_on"] and record["happened_on"] < season["started_on"])
                    or (season["ended_on"] and record["happened_on"] > season["ended_on"])):
                raise conflict("作业日期与茬次当前起止日期不符，未恢复；请先核对茬次日期。")
        conn.execute("UPDATE farm_records SET deleted_at=NULL WHERE id=?", (record_id,))
    return True
def purge_plot(plot_id: str, user_id: str, expected_revision: str) -> dict:
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        plot = _owned(conn, "plots", plot_id, user_id)
        if plot["deleted_at"] is None:
            raise conflict("只能最终删除回收站中的地块，请刷新后核对。")
        _check_plot_links(conn, plot)
        impact = _impact(conn, plot)
        _check_revision(expected_revision, impact["revision"])
        for table in ("farm_records", "farm_tasks", "plot_seasons"):
            conn.execute(f"DELETE FROM {table} WHERE plot_id=? AND user_id=?", (plot_id, user_id))
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM plots WHERE id=? AND user_id=?", (plot_id, user_id))
    return impact
def purge_record(record_id: str, user_id: str, expected_revision: str) -> None:
    with get_connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        record = _owned(conn, "farm_records", record_id, user_id)
        if record["deleted_at"] is None:
            raise conflict("只能最终删除单独移入回收站的台账。")
        _check_revision(expected_revision, _record_revision(record))
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM farm_records WHERE id=? AND user_id=?", (record_id, user_id))

      && (!season.started_on || season.started_on <= today) && (!season.ended_on || today <= season.ended_on));

  }

  function handleDecisionChange(field: keyof typeof defaultDecisionForm, value: string | number) {

  function handleDecisionPlotChange(plotId: string) {

  async function handleDecisionSubmit() {
    if (!accountCurrent() || adviceSaveAction.isRunning() || generatingVersion.current === decisionVersion.current) return;
    const version = ++decisionVersion.current;
    generatingVersion.current = version;
    const input = { ...decisionForm };
    const plotId = selectedPlotId;
    const plotName = plots.items.find((item) => item.id === plotId)?.name ?? "";
    updateDecisionResult(null);
    setDecisionBusy(true);
    setError("");
    const current = () => accountCurrent() && decisionVersion.current === version;
    try {
      const response = await api.post<{ success: true; reply: string }>("/api/decision", {
        crop: input.crop,
        stage: input.stage,
        rain_prob: input.rainProb,
        soil_moisture: input.soilMoisture,
        temperature: input.temperature,
      });
      if (!current()) return;
      updateDecisionResult({ version, input, plotId, plotName, reply: response.data.reply, saved: false });
      setError("");
    } catch (requestError) {
      if (current()) setError(getErrorMessage(requestError));
    } finally {
      if (current()) setDecisionBusy(false);
      if (generatingVersion.current === version) generatingVersion.current = null;
    }
  }

import { FormEvent, useState } from "react";
import { useAsyncGuard } from "../hooks/useAsyncGuard";

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 88 页 / 共 184 页
import { useSessionGuard } from "../hooks/useSessionBoundary";
import { api, getApiErrorInfo, getErrorMessage } from "../lib/api";
function passwordError(password: string, confirmation: string) {
  if (password.length < 8 || password.length > 64 || !password.trim()) return "新密码需为 8–64 位，不能全为空格。";
  if (password !== confirmation) return "两次输入的新密码不一致。";
  return "";
}
function NewPasswordFields({ password, confirmation, onPassword, onConfirmation }: {
  password: string; confirmation: string; onPassword: (value: string) => void; onConfirmation: (value: string) => void;
}) {
  return <>
    <label className="field"><span>新密码</span><input type="password" autoComplete="new-password" required minLength={8} maxLength={64} value={password} onChange={(event) => onPassword(event.target.value)} /></label>
    <label className="field"><span>再次输入新密码</span><input type="password" autoComplete="new-password" required maxLength={64} value={confirmation} onChange={(event) => onConfirmation(event.target.value)} /></label>
  </>;
}
export function AccountSecurity({ onReturnToLogin }: { onReturnToLogin: (message: string) => void }) {
  const isCurrent = useSessionGuard();
  const [oldPassword, setOldPassword] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmation, setConfirmation] = useState("");
  const [proof, setProof] = useState("");
  const [code, setCode] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [uncertain, setUncertain] = useState(false);
  const action = useAsyncGuard();
  async function change(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const validation = passwordError(password, confirmation);
    if (validation) { setError(validation); return; }
    await action.run(async () => {
      setError(""); setUncertain(false);
      try {
        const response = await api.post<{ message: string }>("/api/auth/change-password", { current_password: oldPassword, new_password: password });
        if (isCurrent()) onReturnToLogin(response.data.message);
      } catch (requestError) {
        if (!isCurrent()) return;
        const info = getApiErrorInfo(requestError);
        const unknown = !info.status || info.status >= 500 || info.status === 401;
        setUncertain(unknown);
        setError(unknown ? "改密结果未确认或会话已失效。请返回登录尝试新密码；若原密码仍有效，可登录后重试。" : info.message);
      }
    });
  }
  async function generate(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    await action.run(async () => {
      setError(""); setCode("");
      try {
        const response = await api.post<{ recovery_code: string }>("/api/auth/recovery-code", { current_password: proof });
        if (!isCurrent()) return;

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 89 页 / 共 184 页
        setCode(response.data.recovery_code); setProof("");
      } catch (requestError) {
        if (!isCurrent()) return;
        const info = getApiErrorInfo(requestError);
        setError(info.retryable ? "未能确认生成结果，旧码可能已失效。请重新生成并以最后收到的恢复码为准。" : info.message);
      }
    });
  }
  return <section className="panel account-security" aria-label="账号安全设置">
    <h3>修改密码</h3>
    <p>成功后全部设备都需重新登录，旧恢复码也会失效。原有地块和记录保留。</p>
    {error && <p role="alert">{error}</p>}
    {uncertain && <button type="button" className="secondary-button" onClick={() => onReturnToLogin("请使用新密码登录核对改密结果；若原密码仍有效，可登录后重试。")}>返回登录核对</button>}
    <form className="field-stack" onSubmit={change}>
      <label className="field"><span>原密码</span><input type="password" autoComplete="current-password" required maxLength={64} value={oldPassword} onChange={(event) => setOldPassword(event.target.value)} /></label>
      <NewPasswordFields password={password} confirmation={confirmation} onPassword={setPassword} onConfirmation={setConfirmation} />
      <button className="primary-button" disabled={action.busy} type="submit">修改密码并退出全部设备</button>
    </form>
    <h3>离线恢复码</h3>
    <p>由你本人离线保管，只展示一次、只能成功使用一次。重新生成会替换旧码。生成恢复码不会退出当前会话。</p>
    <p>恢复码和密码都丢失时，请联系部署者线下核验身份后辅助恢复；无法核验时不能恢复。访客须先保存为正式账号。</p>
    {code ? <div className="recovery-secret" role="status"><p>请现在离线保存，关闭后无法再次查看。</p><code aria-label="本次恢复码">{code}</code><button className="secondary-button" type="button" onClick={() => setCode("")}>我已离线保存，关闭</button></div>
      : <form className="field-stack" onSubmit={generate}>
        <label className="field"><span>生成恢复码用的当前密码</span><input type="password" autoComplete="current-password" required maxLength={64} value={proof} onChange={(event) => setProof(event.target.value)} /></label>
        <button type="submit" className="secondary-button" disabled={action.busy}>生成或更换恢复码</button>
      </form>}
  </section>;
}
export function PasswordRecovery({ onReturnToLogin }: { onReturnToLogin: (username: string, message?: string) => void }) {
  const isCurrent = useSessionGuard();
  const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
  const [code, setCode] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmation, setConfirmation] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const action = useAsyncGuard();
  async function submit(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const validation = passwordError(password, confirmation);
    if (validation) { setError(validation); return; }
    await action.run(async () => {
      setError("");
      try {
        const response = await api.post<{ message: string }>("/api/auth/recover-password", { username: username.trim(), recovery_code: code.trim(), new_password: password });
        if (isCurrent()) onReturnToLogin(username.trim(), response.data.message);
      } catch (requestError) { if (isCurrent()) setError(getErrorMessage(requestError)); }
    });
  }
  return <section className="panel account-security recovery-page" aria-label="用恢复码重置密码">
    <h2>用恢复码重置密码</h2>

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 90 页 / 共 184 页
    <p>需要你之前离线保存的恢复码。成功后恢复码失效，全部设备需重新登录，原有记录保留。</p>
    <p>没有恢复码或已经丢失，请联系部署者线下核验身份后辅助恢复。用户名和显示名称不能作为恢复凭据。</p>
    {error && <p role="alert">{error}</p>}
    <form className="field-stack" onSubmit={submit}>
      <label className="field"><span>账号用户名</span><input autoComplete="username" required maxLength={32} value={username} onChange={(event) => setUsername(event.target.value)} /></label>
      <label className="field"><span>离线恢复码</span><input type="password" autoComplete="off" required maxLength={128} value={code} onChange={(event) => setCode(event.target.value)} /></label>
      <NewPasswordFields password={password} confirmation={confirmation} onPassword={setPassword} onConfirmation={setConfirmation} />
      <button type="submit" className="primary-button" disabled={action.busy}>确认重置密码</button>
      <button type="button" className="ghost-button" disabled={action.busy} onClick={() => onReturnToLogin(username)}>返回登录</button>
    </form>
    <p>若上次提交中断，可返回登录尝试新密码，再决定是否重试。</p>
  </section>;
}

import { useId, useRef } from "react";
import { useDialogFocus } from "../hooks/useDialogFocus";
interface ConfirmDialogProps {
  open: boolean;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  confirmLabel: string;
  busy: boolean;
  confirmDisabled?: boolean;
  onConfirm: () => void;

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 93 页 / 共 184 页
  onCancel: () => void;
}
export function ConfirmDialog(props: ConfirmDialogProps) {
  const { open, title, description, confirmLabel, busy, onConfirm, onCancel } = props;
  const dialog = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  const titleId = useId();
  useDialogFocus(dialog, open, () => { if (!busy) onCancel(); });
  if (!open) return null;
  return (
    <div className="dialog-backdrop" role="presentation" onMouseDown={() => { if (!busy) onCancel(); }}>
      <section
        className="confirm-dialog"
        ref={dialog}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby={titleId}
        tabIndex={-1}
        onMouseDown={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <h3 id={titleId}>{title}</h3>
        <p>{description}</p>
        <div className="inline-actions confirm-dialog__actions">
          <button className="ghost-button" type="button" onClick={onCancel} disabled={busy}>
            取消
          </button>
          <button className="secondary-button danger-button" type="button" onClick={onConfirm} disabled={busy || props.confirmDisabled}>
            {busy ? "处理中…" : confirmLabel}
          </button>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
import { useState } from "react";
import { useAsyncGuard } from "../hooks/useAsyncGuard";
import { downloadCsv } from "../lib/csvDownload";
import { getErrorMessage } from "../lib/api";
interface CsvExportButtonProps {
  endpoint: string;
  params: object;
  label: string;
  unit: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
}
export function CsvExportButton({ endpoint, params, label, unit, disabled }: CsvExportButtonProps) {
  const action = useAsyncGuard();
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  async function download() {
    await action.run(async () => {

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 94 页 / 共 184 页
      setMessage(""); setError("");
      try {
        const result = await downloadCsv(endpoint, { ...params } as Record<string, string>, `${label}.csv`);
        setMessage(result.count === 0 ? "没有符合条件的数据，已生成仅含表头的 CSV 并开始下载。" : `已生成点击时筛选范围${result.count === undefined ? "" : `的 ${result.count} ${unit}`}并开始下载：${result.filename}`);
      } catch (requestError) { setError(getErrorMessage(requestError, "CSV 导出失败，请稍后重试。")); }
    });
  }
  return <div className="csv-export">
    <button type="button" className="secondary-button" disabled={disabled || action.busy} onClick={() => void download()}>{action.busy ? "导出中…" : label}</button>
    {message && <p className="csv-export-feedback" role="status">{message}</p>}
    {error && <p className="csv-export-feedback" role="alert">{error}</p>}
  </div>;
}
