import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { LedgerWorkspace } from "./LedgerWorkspace";
import { CsvExportButton } from "./CsvExportButton";
import { TaskInput } from "../hooks/useTasks";
import { useRequestLeave } from "../hooks/useUnsavedChanges";
import { api, getErrorMessage } from "../lib/api";
import { formatDate } from "../lib/date";
import { EconomicsFilters, EconomicsScope, FarmRecordFilters, Plot, PlotEconomics, PlotSeason } from "../types";
interface EconomicsPanelProps {
  plots: Plot[];
  seasons: PlotSeason[];
  filters: EconomicsFilters;
  onFiltersChange: (filters: EconomicsFilters) => void;
  onError: (message: string) => void;
  onRecordsChanged: () => void | Promise<void>;
  onQuickTask: (input: TaskInput) => Promise<unknown>;
}
const scopeNames: Record<EconomicsScope, string> = {
  all: "全部账目", cost: "已记录支出", revenue: "已完整记录收入",
  missing_cost: "费用未填", incomplete_harvest: "采收待补录",
};
const money = (value: number | null) => value === null ? "—" : `¥${value}`;
const kilograms = (value: number | null) => value === null ? "—" : `${value} 公斤`;
const sum = (rows: PlotEconomics[], key: "total_cost" | "total_revenue" | "net_revenue" | "missing_cost_count" | "incomplete_harvest_count" | "recorded_cost_count" | "complete_harvest_count") =>
  Math.round(rows.reduce((total, row) => total + row[key], 0) * 100) / 100;
export function EconomicsPanel({ plots, seasons, filters, onFiltersChange, onError, onRecordsChanged, onQuickTask }: EconomicsPanelProps) {
  const requestLeave = useRequestLeave();
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<PlotEconomics[]>([]);
  const [years, setYears] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const requestVersion = useRef(0);
  const [trace, setTrace] = useState<{ title: string; filters: FarmRecordFilters } | null>(null);
  const reload = useCallback(async () => {
    const version = ++requestVersion.current;
    setLoading(true);
    setRows([]);
    setError("");
    try {
      const params = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(filters).filter(([, value]) => value));
      const response = await api.get<{ plots: PlotEconomics[]; available_years: number[] }>("/api/farm-records/economics", { params });
      if (version !== requestVersion.current) return;
      setRows(response.data.plots);
      setYears(response.data.available_years);
    } catch (requestError) {
      if (version === requestVersion.current) setError(getErrorMessage(requestError));
    } finally {

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 98 页 / 共 184 页
      if (version === requestVersion.current) setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [filters]);
  useEffect(() => {
    void reload();
    return () => { requestVersion.current += 1; };
  }, [reload]);
  function changeFilter(field: keyof EconomicsFilters, value: string) {
    requestVersion.current += 1;
    setRows([]);
    setLoading(true);
    onFiltersChange({ ...filters, [field]: value, ...(field === "plot_id" ? { season_id: "" } : {}),
      ...(field === "data_scope" ? { plot_id: "", season_id: "", year: "" } : {}) });
  }
  function openTrace(item: PlotEconomics | null, scope: EconomicsScope) {
    setTrace({
      title: `${item ? `${item.plot_name} · ${item.crop || "未归茬"}` : "当前统计范围"} · ${scopeNames[scope]}`,
      filters: {
        data_scope: filters.data_scope ?? "real",
        plot_id: item ? item.plot_id : filters.plot_id,
        season_id: item ? item.season_id ?? "unassigned" : filters.season_id, economics_scope: scope,
        kind: "", date_from: filters.year ? `${filters.year.padStart(4, "0")}-01-01` : "",
        date_to: filters.year ? `${filters.year.padStart(4, "0")}-12-31` : "",
      },
    });
  }
  function traceButton(item: PlotEconomics, scope: EconomicsScope, label: string) {
    return <button type="button" className="economics-link"
      aria-label={`查看${scopeNames[scope]}台账：${item.plot_name} · ${item.crop || "未归茬"} · ${item.started_on ?? "日期未填"}`}
      onClick={() => openTrace(item, scope)}>{label}</button>;
  }
  function cost(item: PlotEconomics) {
    return traceButton(item, item.recorded_cost_count === 0 && item.missing_cost_count > 0 ? "missing_cost" : "cost",
      item.recorded_cost_count === 0 && item.missing_cost_count > 0 ? "待补录" : money(item.total_cost));
  }
  function income(item: PlotEconomics) {
    const missing = item.complete_harvest_count === 0 && item.incomplete_harvest_count > 0;
    return traceButton(item, missing ? "incomplete_harvest" : "revenue", missing ? "待补录" : money(item.total_revenue));
  }
  function quality(item: PlotEconomics) {
    return <div className="economics-quality">
      {traceButton(item, "missing_cost", `费用未填 ${item.missing_cost_count} 条`)}
      {traceButton(item, "incomplete_harvest", `采收待补录 ${item.incomplete_harvest_count} 条`)}
    </div>;
  }
  function period(item: PlotEconomics) {
    return `${item.started_on ? formatDate(item.started_on) : "开始日未填"} ~ ${item.ended_on ? formatDate(item.ended_on) : item.season_id ? "进行中" : "未归茬"}`;
  }
  function perMu(item: PlotEconomics) {
    return <div className="economics-per-mu">

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 99 页 / 共 184 页
      <span>支出 {item.recorded_cost_count === 0 && item.missing_cost_count > 0 ? "待补录" : money(item.cost_per_mu)}</span>
      <span>收入 {item.complete_harvest_count === 0 && item.incomplete_harvest_count > 0 ? "待补录" : money(item.revenue_per_mu)}</span>
      <span>差额 {money(item.net_per_mu)}</span>
      <span>产量 {item.recorded_yield_count === 0 && item.incomplete_harvest_count > 0 ? "待补录" : kilograms(item.yield_per_mu)}</span>
    </div>;
  }
  if (trace) {
    return <div className="panel--full">
      <div className="panel economics-trace-header">
        <h3>收支台账明细</h3>
        <p>{trace.title}；{filters.year ? `${filters.year} 年作业日期范围` : "全部年份"}。可直接编辑补录，返回后保留统计筛选。</p>
        <button className="secondary-button" type="button" onClick={() => requestLeave(() => { setTrace(null); void reload(); })}>返回收支统计</button>
      </div>
      <LedgerWorkspace plots={plots} seasons={seasons} initialFilters={trace.filters} onError={onError}
        onQuickTask={onQuickTask} onRecordsChanged={async () => { await onRecordsChanged(); await reload(); }} />
    </div>;
  }
  const missingCost = sum(rows, "missing_cost_count");
  const missingHarvest = sum(rows, "incomplete_harvest_count");
  const yearOptions = Array.from(new Set([...years, ...(filters.year ? [Number(filters.year)] : [])])).sort((a, b) => b - a);
  return <div className="panel panel--full economics-panel">
    <div className="panel__header"><div><h3>每茬收支</h3><p>已记录收支差额 = 已记录收入 − 已记录支出。</p></div></div>
    <p className="economics-note">仅汇总已填写的费用与产量；收入仅包含产量、单价都完整的采收记录。未记录的人工、地租等成本不包含在内。</p>
    <p className="economics-note">年度按作业日期划分，跨年茬次只计所选年份内的记录；全部年份显示整茬累计。亩均指标按地块当前登记面积计算。</p>
    <div className="field-grid economics-filters">
      <label className="field"><span>收支数据来源</span><div className="field-control field-control--select">
        <select value={filters.data_scope ?? "real"} onChange={(event) => changeFilter("data_scope", event.target.value)}>
          <option value="real">真实记录</option><option value="demo">演示记录</option><option value="all">全部记录（含演示）</option>
        </select>
      </div></label>
      <label className="field"><span>统计地块</span><div className="field-control field-control--select">
        <select value={filters.plot_id} onChange={(event) => changeFilter("plot_id", event.target.value)}>
          <option value="">全部地块</option>{plots.map((plot) => <option key={plot.id} value={plot.id}>{plot.name}</option>)}
        </select>
      </div></label>
      <label className="field"><span>统计茬次</span><div className="field-control field-control--select">
        <select value={filters.season_id} onChange={(event) => changeFilter("season_id", event.target.value)}>
          <option value="">全部茬次（含历史）</option><option value="unassigned">未归茬</option>
          {seasons.filter((season) => !filters.plot_id || season.plot_id === filters.plot_id).map((season) => <option key={season.id} value={season.id}>
            {plots.find((plot) => plot.id === season.plot_id)?.name} · {season.crop}（{season.started_on ?? "开始日未填"} 至 {season.ended_on ?? "进行中"}）
          </option>)}
        </select>
      </div></label>
      <label className="field"><span>作业年份</span><div className="field-control field-control--select">
        <select value={filters.year} onChange={(event) => changeFilter("year", event.target.value)}>
          <option value="">全部年份</option>{yearOptions.map((year) => <option key={year} value={year}>{year} 年</option>)}
        </select>
      </div></label>
    </div>
    <p className="demo-notice">{filters.data_scope === "demo" ? "演示收支：示例金额不代表真实经营成果。" : filters.data_scope === "all" ? "全部记录可能包含演示金额，请切换真实记录查看自己的收支。" : "当前仅汇总真实记录，不包含演示金额。"}</p>

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 100 页 / 共 184 页
    <div className="csv-export-actions">
      <CsvExportButton endpoint="/api/farm-records/economics/export.csv" params={filters} label="导出收支汇总 CSV" unit="行汇总" disabled={loading || !!error} />
      <CsvExportButton endpoint="/api/farm-records/export.csv" params={filters} label="导出对应台账 CSV" unit="条台账" disabled={loading || !!error} />
    </div>
    <p className="economics-note">导出当前筛选范围全部数据；选定历史茬次可导出单茬收支及对应明细。CSV 用于留档和分析，不能作为完整数据库恢复备份。</p>
    {error && <p role="alert">{error}</p>}
    {loading ? <p role="status">正在核对收支…</p> : !error && <>
      <div className="stats-summary">
        <div className="stat-card"><span>已记录支出</span><strong><button type="button" className="economics-link" aria-label="查看当前范围支出台账"
          onClick={() => openTrace(null, sum(rows, "recorded_cost_count") === 0 && missingCost > 0 ? "missing_cost" : "cost")}>{sum(rows, "recorded_cost_count") === 0 && missingCost > 0 ? "待补录" : money(sum(rows, "total_cost"))}</button></strong></div>
        <div className="stat-card"><span>已记录收入</span><strong><button type="button" className="economics-link" aria-label="查看当前范围收入台账"
          onClick={() => openTrace(null, sum(rows, "complete_harvest_count") === 0 && missingHarvest > 0 ? "incomplete_harvest" : "revenue")}>{sum(rows, "complete_harvest_count") === 0 && missingHarvest > 0 ? "待补录" : money(sum(rows, "total_revenue"))}</button></strong></div>
        <div className="stat-card"><span>已记录收支差额</span><strong><button type="button" className="economics-link" aria-label="查看当前范围全部台账"
          onClick={() => openTrace(null, "all")}>{money(sum(rows, "net_revenue"))}</button></strong></div>
      </div>
      <p className="economics-completeness">当前范围：
        <button type="button" className="economics-link" aria-label="查看当前范围费用未填台账" onClick={() => openTrace(null, "missing_cost")}>费用未填 {missingCost} 条</button>，
        <button type="button" className="economics-link" aria-label="查看当前范围采收待补录台账" onClick={() => openTrace(null, "incomplete_harvest")}>采收待补录 {missingHarvest} 条</button>。
        同一记录可能同时出现在两类待补录中，待补录金额尚未计入差额。点击数字可查看组成台账。</p>
      {rows.length === 0 ? <p className="stats-empty">当前筛选下没有收支记录，请调整地块、茬次或年份。</p> : <>
        <div className="economics-table-wrap"><table className="economics-table">
          <thead><tr><th>地块</th><th>茬次</th><th>起止</th><th>登记面积</th><th>记录次数</th><th>已记录支出</th><th>已记录产量</th><th>已记录收入</th><th>已记录收支差额</th><th>亩均指标</th><th>待补录</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>{rows.map((item) => <tr key={`${item.plot_id}:${item.season_id ?? "unassigned"}`}>
            <td>{item.plot_name}{item.is_demo && <span className="demo-badge">演示资料</span>}</td><td>{item.season_id ? item.crop : "未归茬"}</td><td>{period(item)}</td><td>{item.area_mu} 亩</td>
            <td>{traceButton(item, "all", `${item.record_count} 次`)}</td><td>{cost(item)}</td>
            <td>{item.recorded_yield_count === 0 && item.incomplete_harvest_count > 0 ? "待补录" : kilograms(item.total_yield_kg)}</td>
            <td>{income(item)}</td><td className={item.net_revenue < 0 ? "economics-negative" : ""}>{traceButton(item, "all", money(item.net_revenue))}</td>
            <td>{perMu(item)}</td><td>{quality(item)}</td>
          </tr>)}</tbody>
        </table></div>
        <div className="economics-cards">{rows.map((item) => <article className="economics-season-card" key={`${item.plot_id}:${item.season_id ?? "unassigned"}`}>
          <h4>{item.plot_name} · {item.season_id ? item.crop : "未归茬"}{item.is_demo && <span className="demo-badge">演示资料</span>}</h4><p>{period(item)}</p>
          <dl><dt>登记面积</dt><dd>{item.area_mu} 亩</dd><dt>记录次数</dt><dd>{traceButton(item, "all", `${item.record_count} 次`)}</dd>
            <dt>已记录支出</dt><dd>{cost(item)}</dd><dt>已记录收入</dt><dd>{income(item)}</dd>
            <dt>已记录收支差额</dt><dd>{traceButton(item, "all", money(item.net_revenue))}</dd>
            <dt>已记录产量</dt><dd>{item.recorded_yield_count === 0 && item.incomplete_harvest_count > 0 ? "待补录" : kilograms(item.total_yield_kg)}</dd>
            <dt>亩均指标</dt><dd>{perMu(item)}</dd></dl>{quality(item)}
        </article>)}</div>
      </>}
    </>}
  </div>;
}
import { CalendarPlus } from "lucide-react";
import { forwardRef, useImperativeHandle, useState } from "react";
import { TaskInput } from "../hooks/useTasks";
import { useAsyncGuard } from "../hooks/useAsyncGuard";
import { useSessionGuard } from "../hooks/useSessionBoundary";
import { useRequestLeave, useUnsavedChanges } from "../hooks/useUnsavedChanges";
import { api, getErrorMessage } from "../lib/api";
import { addDays, formatDate, todayIso } from "../lib/date";

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 101 页 / 共 184 页
import { LedgerDraft } from "../lib/taskLedger";
import { newSubmissionId } from "../lib/submission";
import { FarmRecord, FarmRecordKind, Plot, PlotSeason } from "../types";
export const recordKinds: FarmRecordKind[] = ["播种", "施肥", "打药", "灌溉", "除草", "采收", "其他"];
const followUps: Partial<Record<FarmRecordKind, { days: number; label: string }>> = {
  播种: { days: 7, label: "查苗情与出苗率" },
  施肥: { days: 7, label: "复查追肥后长势" },
  打药: { days: 3, label: "复查防效" },
  灌溉: { days: 2, label: "查看墒情与积水" },
  除草: { days: 10, label: "复查杂草复发" },
};
export interface FarmRecordFormHandle {
  edit: (record: FarmRecord) => void;
  discardDeletedRecord: (recordId: string) => void;
}
interface FarmRecordFormProps {
  plots: Plot[];
  seasons: PlotSeason[];
  initialDraft?: LedgerDraft;
  initialSubmissionId?: string;
  initialPlotId?: string;
  initialSeasonId?: string;
  compact?: boolean;
  action?: ReturnType<typeof useAsyncGuard>;
  onSaved?: (record: FarmRecord) => void;
  onCancel?: () => void;
  onError: (message: string) => void;
  onRecordsChanged: () => void | Promise<void>;
  onQuickTask: (input: TaskInput) => Promise<unknown>;
}
interface RecordResponse { success: true; record: FarmRecord }
function emptyForm(plotId = "") {
  return {
    plot_id: plotId, kind: "施肥" as FarmRecordKind, happened_on: todayIso(), crop: "",
    quantity: "", cost: "", material: "", safe_days: "", yield_kg: "", unit_price: "", detail: "",
  };
}
export const FarmRecordForm = forwardRef<FarmRecordFormHandle, FarmRecordFormProps>(function FarmRecordForm(props, ref) {
  const { plots, seasons, initialDraft, initialPlotId, initialSeasonId, compact, onSaved, onCancel, onError, onRecordsChanged, onQuickTask } = props;
  const [form, setForm] = useState(() => ({ ...emptyForm(initialPlotId || (initialDraft ? "" : plots[0]?.id)), ...initialDraft }));
  const [submissionId, setSubmissionId] = useState(() => props.initialSubmissionId ?? newSubmissionId());
  const [editingRecord, setEditingRecord] = useState<FarmRecord | null>(null);
  const [selectedSeason, setSelectedSeason] = useState(initialDraft?.season_id ?? initialSeasonId ?? "");
  const [followUp, setFollowUp] = useState<TaskInput | null>(null);
  const localAction = useAsyncGuard();
  const action = props.action ?? localAction;
  const requestLeave = useRequestLeave();
  const sessionCurrent = useSessionGuard();
  const [cleanSignature, setCleanSignature] = useState(() => JSON.stringify([form, selectedSeason]));
  const [saveUnconfirmed, setSaveUnconfirmed] = useState(false);

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 102 页 / 共 184 页
  const [saveNotice, setSaveNotice] = useState("");
  useUnsavedChanges(JSON.stringify([form, selectedSeason]) !== cleanSignature || saveUnconfirmed, action.busy, () => {
    const [savedForm, savedSeason] = JSON.parse(cleanSignature) as [typeof form, string];
    setForm(savedForm);
    setSelectedSeason(savedSeason);
    setSaveUnconfirmed(false);
    onError("");
  });
  const matchingSeasons = seasons.filter((season) => season.plot_id === form.plot_id
    && (!season.started_on || season.started_on <= form.happened_on)
    && (!season.ended_on || form.happened_on <= season.ended_on));
  const automaticSeason = matchingSeasons.length === 1 && matchingSeasons[0].started_on ? matchingSeasons[0] : null;
  const seasonHint = selectedSeason === "unassigned" ? "本次明确记为未归茬。"
    : selectedSeason ? (() => {
      const season = seasons.find((item) => item.id === selectedSeason && item.plot_id === form.plot_id);
      if (!season) return "所选茬次不可用，请重新选择。";
      return (season.started_on && form.happened_on < season.started_on) || (season.ended_on && form.happened_on > season.ended_on)
        ? "作业日期不在所选茬次内，请调整日期、选择对应历史茬次或明确选择未归茬。"
        : `本次记入 ${season.crop}（${season.started_on ?? "开始日未填"} 至 ${season.ended_on ?? "进行中"}）。`;
    })() : automaticSeason
      ? `按作业日期匹配：${automaticSeason.crop}（${automaticSeason.started_on} 至 ${automaticSeason.ended_on ?? "进行中"}）。`
      : matchingSeasons.length ? "日期对应的茬次有歧义或开始日未填，请选择具体茬次或未归茬。" : "此日期没有匹配茬次，将记为未归茬。";
  const safety = plots.find((item) => item.id === form.plot_id)?.harvest_safety ?? null;
  const harvestWarning =
    form.kind === "采收" && safety?.in_safe_window
      ? `该地块 ${safety.happened_on} 施过 ${safety.material}，登记的安全间隔期 ${safety.safe_days} 天，按输入计算最早采收日为 ${safety.earliest_harvest_on}（还有 ${safety.days_remaining} 天）。提醒覆盖该地块全部茬次与作物，请以产品标签为准。`
      : "";
  function handlePlotChange(plotId: string) {
    setSelectedSeason("");
    setForm((current) => ({ ...current, plot_id: plotId, crop: "" }));
  }
  function handleSeasonChange(seasonId: string) {
    setSelectedSeason(seasonId);
    setForm((current) => ({ ...current, crop: "" }));
  }
  function handleKindChange(kind: FarmRecordKind) {
    if (kind === form.kind) return;
    setForm((current) => ({
      ...current, kind, material: "", quantity: "",
      safe_days: kind === "打药" ? current.safe_days : "",
      yield_kg: kind === "采收" ? current.yield_kg : "",
      unit_price: kind === "采收" ? current.unit_price : "",
    }));
  }
  function cancelEdit() {
    const next = emptyForm(form.plot_id);
    setEditingRecord(null);
    setForm(next);
    setSelectedSeason("");
    setCleanSignature(JSON.stringify([next, ""]));

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 103 页 / 共 184 页
    setSaveUnconfirmed(false);
    onError("");
  }
  function startEdit(record: FarmRecord) {
    setEditingRecord(record);
    setSelectedSeason(record.season_id ?? "unassigned");
    setFollowUp(null);
    const next = {
      plot_id: record.plot_id, kind: record.kind, happened_on: record.happened_on,
      crop: record.crop, quantity: record.quantity, detail: record.detail,
      cost: record.cost === null ? "" : String(record.cost),
      material: ["施肥", "打药"].includes(record.kind) ? record.material : "",
      safe_days: record.kind === "打药" && record.safe_days !== null ? String(record.safe_days) : "",
      yield_kg: record.kind === "采收" && record.yield_kg !== null ? String(record.yield_kg) : "",
      unit_price: record.kind === "采收" && record.unit_price !== null ? String(record.unit_price) : "",
    };
    setForm(next);
    setCleanSignature(JSON.stringify([next, record.season_id ?? "unassigned"]));
    setSaveUnconfirmed(false);
    setSaveNotice("");
    onError("");
    document.getElementById("ledger-record-form")?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
  }
  async function handleSubmit() {
    if (!sessionCurrent()) return;
    if (!form.plot_id) {
      onError("请先选择地块。");
      return;
    }
    await action.run(async () => {
      setSaveNotice("");
      setSaveUnconfirmed(true);
      try {
        const payload = {
          plot_id: form.plot_id,
          ...(selectedSeason ? { season_id: selectedSeason === "unassigned" ? null : selectedSeason } : {}),
          kind: form.kind,
          happened_on: form.happened_on,
          crop: form.crop,
          quantity: form.quantity,
          cost: form.cost.trim() === "" ? null : Number(form.cost),
          material: ["施肥", "打药"].includes(form.kind) ? form.material : "",
          safe_days: form.kind !== "打药" || form.safe_days.trim() === "" ? null : Number(form.safe_days),
          yield_kg: form.kind !== "采收" || form.yield_kg.trim() === "" ? null : Number(form.yield_kg),
          unit_price: form.kind !== "采收" || form.unit_price.trim() === "" ? null : Number(form.unit_price),
          detail: form.detail,
        };
        if (editingRecord) {
          await api.patch<RecordResponse>(`/api/farm-records/${editingRecord.id}`, payload);
          if (!sessionCurrent()) return;

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 104 页 / 共 184 页
          cancelEdit();
          setSaveNotice("修改已保存，收支将按更新后的台账重新汇总。");
        } else {
          const response = await api.post<RecordResponse>("/api/farm-records", { ...payload, submission_id: submissionId });
          if (!sessionCurrent()) return;
          setSubmissionId(newSubmissionId());
          onError("");
          onSaved?.(response.data.record);
          const next = {
            ...form, quantity: "", cost: "", material: "", safe_days: "",
            yield_kg: "", unit_price: "", detail: "",
          };
          setForm(next);
          setCleanSignature(JSON.stringify([next, selectedSeason]));
          setSaveUnconfirmed(false);
          setSaveNotice("已记入台账，可随时编辑补全费用、产量等信息。");
        }
        const rule = editingRecord ? undefined : followUps[form.kind];
        setFollowUp(
          rule
            ? {
                plot_id: form.plot_id,
                title: rule.label,
                due_on: addDays(form.happened_on, rule.days),
                notes: "",
              }
            : null,
        );
        onError("");
        await onRecordsChanged();
      } catch (error) {
        if (sessionCurrent()) onError(getErrorMessage(error));
      }
    });
  }
  useImperativeHandle(ref, () => ({
    edit: startEdit,
    discardDeletedRecord: (recordId) => { if (editingRecord?.id === recordId) cancelEdit(); },
  }));
  return (
    <form className="panel panel--full ledger-form" id="ledger-record-form" onSubmit={(event) => { event.preventDefault(); void handleSubmit(); }}>
      <div className="panel__header">
        <div>
          <h3>{editingRecord ? "编辑农事记录" : "记一次农事"}</h3>
          <p>确认地块、日期和作业类型即可保存，其余可留空，稍后编辑补齐。</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      {plots.find((plot) => plot.id === form.plot_id)?.is_demo && <p className="demo-notice">当前为演示地块。自行新增的台账会保留，清空演示时不会删除。</p>}
      <fieldset className="ledger-fields" disabled={action.busy}>
      <div className="record-kind-buttons" role="group" aria-label="快捷农活">

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 105 页 / 共 184 页
        {recordKinds.map((kind) => <button type="button" key={kind} aria-pressed={form.kind === kind}
          className={form.kind === kind ? "secondary-button is-selected" : "ghost-button"}
          onClick={() => handleKindChange(kind)}>{kind}</button>)}
      </div>
      <div className="field-grid">
        <label className="field">
          <span>记录到地块</span>
          <div className="field-control field-control--select">
            <select value={form.plot_id} onChange={(event) => handlePlotChange(event.target.value)}>
              <option value="">请选择地块</option>
              {plots.map((plot) => (
                <option key={plot.id} value={plot.id}>
                  {plot.name}{plot.is_demo ? "【演示】" : ""}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
        </label>
        <label className="field">
          <span>所属茬次</span>
          <div className="field-control field-control--select">
            <select value={selectedSeason} onChange={(event) => handleSeasonChange(event.target.value)}>
              <option value="">{automaticSeason ? `自动匹配：${automaticSeason.crop}（${automaticSeason.started_on} 至 ${automaticSeason.ended_on ?? "进行中"}）` : "按作业日期自动匹配（有歧义请明确选择）"}</option>
              <option value="unassigned">未归茬</option>
              {seasons.filter((season) => season.plot_id === form.plot_id).map((season) => (
                <option key={season.id} value={season.id}>
                  {season.crop}（{season.started_on ?? "开始日未填"} 至 {season.ended_on ?? "进行中"}）{season.is_demo ? "【演示】" : ""}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
          {form.plot_id && <small className="season-hint">{seasonHint}</small>}
        </label>
        <label className="field ledger-kind-field">
          <span>作业类型</span>
          <div className="field-control field-control--select">
            <select
              value={form.kind}
              onChange={(event) => handleKindChange(event.target.value as FarmRecordKind)}
            >
              {recordKinds.map((item) => (
                <option key={item} value={item}>
                  {item}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
        </label>
        <label className="field">
          <span>作业日期</span>

          <div className="field-control">
            <input
              type="date"
              value={form.happened_on}
              onChange={(event) => {
                setForm((current) => ({ ...current, happened_on: event.target.value, crop: "" }));
                if (!editingRecord) setSelectedSeason("");
              }}
            />
          </div>
        </label>
        <label className="field">
          <span>费用（元）</span>
          <div className="field-control">
            <input
              type="number"
              inputMode="decimal"
              value={form.cost}
              min={0}
              step={0.01}
              placeholder="可留空"
              onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, cost: event.target.value }))}
            />
          </div>
        </label>
        {["施肥", "打药"].includes(form.kind) && (

                onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, material: event.target.value }))} />

                  min={0}
                  max={365}
                  placeholder="按产品标签填写"
                  onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, safe_days: event.target.value }))}
                />
              </div>

                  type="number"
                  inputMode="decimal"
                  value={form.yield_kg}
                  min={0}

                  onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, yield_kg: event.target.value }))}

            <label className="field">
              <span>单价（元/公斤）</span>
              <div className="field-control">
                <input
                  type="number"
                  inputMode="decimal"
                  value={form.unit_price}
                  min={0}
                  step={0.01}
                  placeholder="例如：2.4"
                  onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, unit_price: event.target.value }))}
                />
              </div>

          <label className="field">
            <span>作物</span>
            <div className="field-control">
              <input
                value={form.crop}
                maxLength={20}
                placeholder="留空则使用匹配茬次的作物"
                onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, crop: event.target.value }))}
              />
            </div>
          </label>

        <CalendarPlus size={16} />
        {action.busy ? "保存中…" : editingRecord ? "保存修改" : "记入台账"}
      </button>
      {editingRecord && <button className="secondary-button" type="button" disabled={action.busy} onClick={() => requestLeave(cancelEdit)}>取消编辑</button>}
      {onCancel && <button className="secondary-button" type="button" disabled={action.busy} onClick={() => requestLeave(onCancel)}>取消记账</button>}
      </div>

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 109 页 / 共 184 页
    </form>
  );
});
import { FormEvent, useState } from "react";
import { useAsyncGuard } from "../hooks/useAsyncGuard";
import { useSessionGuard } from "../hooks/useSessionBoundary";
import { api, getApiErrorInfo, getErrorMessage } from "../lib/api";
import { User } from "../types";
interface GuestAccountPanelProps {
  user: User;
  onSaved: (user: User, sessionId: string) => void;
  onReturnToLogin: (username: string) => void;
}
export function GuestAccountPanel({ user, onSaved, onReturnToLogin }: GuestAccountPanelProps) {
  const isCurrent = useSessionGuard();
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ username: "", password: "", confirmation: "", display_name: user.display_name });
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [needsCheck, setNeedsCheck] = useState(false);
  const [needsLogin, setNeedsLogin] = useState(false);
  const action = useAsyncGuard();
  async function checkResult(fallback: string) {
    if (!isCurrent()) return;
    try {
      const response = await api.get<{ user: User; session_id: string }>("/api/me");
      if (!isCurrent()) return;
      if (response.data.user.id === user.id && !response.data.user.is_guest) {
        onSaved(response.data.user, response.data.session_id);
        return;
      }
      if (response.data.user.id !== user.id) {
        setError("当前登录账号已变化，请返回登录后确认账号。");
        setNeedsLogin(true);
        return;
      }
      setNeedsCheck(false);
      setNeedsLogin(false);
      setError(fallback);
    } catch (requestError) {
      if (!isCurrent()) return;
      if (getApiErrorInfo(requestError).status === 401) {
        setNeedsLogin(true);
        setError("会话已失效，账号可能已经保存。请使用刚设置的用户名和密码登录确认，原有数据仍归原账号。");
      } else {
        setNeedsCheck(true);
        setError("暂时无法核对保存结果。请保留已填写的凭据，不要退出当前访客会话，网络恢复后再核对。");
      }
    }
  }
  async function submit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 110 页 / 共 184 页
    event.preventDefault();
    const payload = { username: form.username.trim(), password: form.password, display_name: form.display_name.trim() };
    if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_]{3,32}$/.test(payload.username)) { setError("用户名需为 3–32 位英文字母、数字或下划线。"); return; }
    if (payload.password.length < 4 || payload.password.length > 64 || !payload.password.trim()) { setError("密码需为 4–64 位，不能全为空格。"); return; }
    if (payload.password !== form.confirmation) { setError("两次输入的密码不一致。"); return; }
    if (!payload.display_name || payload.display_name.length > 32) { setError("显示名称需为 1–32 个字符。"); return; }
    await action.run(async () => {
      setError("");
      try {
        const response = await api.post<{ user: User; session_id: string }>("/api/auth/upgrade-guest", payload);
        if (!isCurrent()) return;
        onSaved(response.data.user, response.data.session_id);
      } catch (requestError) {
        if (!isCurrent()) return;
        const info = getApiErrorInfo(requestError);
        const failureMessage = info.status === 403 ? "账号或安全校验未通过，请刷新页面后重试。" : getErrorMessage(requestError);
        setError(failureMessage);
        if (info.retryable || info.status === 401 || info.status === 403 || info.code === "GUEST_ALREADY_UPGRADED") {
          setNeedsCheck(true);
          await checkResult(info.status === 403 ? failureMessage : "当前仍是访客，尚未保存账号，可以重试。");
        }
      }
    });
  }
  function close() {
    setExpanded(false);
    setForm({ username: "", password: "", confirmation: "", display_name: user.display_name });
    setError("");
    setNeedsCheck(false);
    setNeedsLogin(false);
  }
  return <section className="panel guest-account-panel" aria-label="保存访客账号">
    {!expanded ? <div className="home-actions"><div><h3>保存账号，下次接着用</h3><p>你正在使用访客。设置账号后，原有地块和记录全部保留。</p></div>
      <button className="primary-button" type="button" onClick={() => { setForm((current) => ({ ...current, display_name: user.display_name })); setExpanded(true); }}>保存账号</button></div>
      : <form onSubmit={(event) => void submit(event)} noValidate>
        <h3>保存为正式账号</h3><p>已有地块与记录全部保留，下次使用新用户名和密码登录。</p>
        <fieldset disabled={action.busy} className="guest-account-fields">
          <div className="field-grid">
            <label className="field"><span>正式用户名</span><div className="field-control"><input disabled={needsLogin} autoComplete="username" maxLength={32} value={form.username} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, username: event.target.value }))} /></div></label>
            <label className="field"><span>账号显示名称</span><div className="field-control"><input disabled={needsLogin} maxLength={32} value={form.display_name} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, display_name: event.target.value }))} /></div></label>
            <label className="field"><span>登录密码</span><div className="field-control"><input disabled={needsLogin} type="password" autoComplete="new-password" maxLength={64} value={form.password} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, password: event.target.value }))} /></div></label>
            <label className="field"><span>再次输入密码</span><div className="field-control"><input disabled={needsLogin} type="password" autoComplete="new-password" maxLength={64} value={form.confirmation} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, confirmation: event.target.value }))} /></div></label>
          </div>
          <div className="inline-actions"><button className="primary-button" type="submit" disabled={needsLogin || needsCheck}>{action.busy ? "保存中…" : "确认保存账号"}</button>
            {!needsLogin && !needsCheck && <button className="ghost-button" type="button" onClick={close}>取消</button>}
            {needsCheck && !needsLogin && <button className="ghost-button" type="button" onClick={() => void action.run(() => checkResult("当前仍是访客，可以重试保存。"))}>核对保存结果</button>}
            {needsLogin && <button className="secondary-button" type="button" onClick={() => onReturnToLogin(form.username.trim())}>返回登录</button>}
          </div>
        </fieldset>
        {error && <p className="form-warning" role="alert">{error}</p>}

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 111 页 / 共 184 页
      </form>}
  </section>;
}

interface HomeWorkspaceProps {
  tasks: FarmTask[];
  plots: Plot[];
  loading: boolean;
  busy: boolean;
  onNavigate: (feature: FeatureKey) => void;
  onComplete: (task: FarmTask) => Promise<unknown>;
  onRecord: (plotId?: string) => void;
}
export function HomeWorkspace({ tasks, plots, loading, busy, onNavigate, onComplete, onRecord }: HomeWorkspaceProps) {
  const today = todayIso();
  const overdue = tasks.filter((task) => task.due_on < today);
  const dueToday = tasks.filter((task) => task.due_on === today);
  const relevantIds = new Set([...overdue, ...dueToday].map((task) => task.plot_id));
  const relevantPlots = plots.filter((plot) => relevantIds.has(plot.id));
  const reminders = plots.filter((plot) => plot.harvest_safety?.in_safe_window);
  if (loading) return <div className="panel panel--loading">正在加载首页...</div>;
  return <section className="home-workspace">
    <div className="stat-grid home-summary">
      <div className="stat-card"><span>已逾期待办</span><strong>{overdue.length}</strong></div>
      <div className="stat-card"><span>今日待办</span><strong>{dueToday.length}</strong></div>
      <div className="stat-card"><span>已登记地块</span><strong>{plots.length}</strong></div>
      <div className="stat-card"><span>已记录农活</span><strong>{plots.reduce((sum, plot) => sum + plot.record_count, 0)}</strong></div>
    </div>
    <div className="home-task-grid">
      {[{ title: "已逾期", items: overdue }, { title: "今日待办", items: dueToday }].map((group) =>
        <section className="panel" key={group.title} aria-label={group.title}>
          <h3>{group.title}（{group.items.length}）</h3>
          {group.items.length === 0 ? <p className="stats-empty">{group.title === "已逾期" ? "没有已逾期待办。" : "今天没有到期的待办。"}</p> :
            <ul className="task-list">{group.items.map((task) => <li className="task-card" key={task.id}>
              <div className="task-card__head"><strong>{task.title}</strong>{task.is_demo && <span className="demo-badge">演示</span>}<span>{formatDate(task.due_on)}</span></div>
              <p>{task.plot_name || "未关联地块（杂事）"}</p>
              {task.notes && <p className="task-card__notes">{task.notes}</p>}
              <button className="ghost-button" type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void onComplete(task)}>标记完成</button>
            </li>)}</ul>}
        </section>)}
    </div>
    <section className="panel">
      <h3>{tasks.length === 0 ? "还没有未完成待办" : `全部待办 ${tasks.length} 项`}</h3>
      <p>{tasks.length === 0 ? "把下一步要做的事记下来；也可以先看看今日农活建议。" : "可以到待办清单查看后续安排和最近完成的事项。"}</p>
      <div className="inline-actions"><button className="secondary-button" type="button" onClick={() => onNavigate("tasks")}>安排待办</button><button className="ghost-button" type="button" onClick={() => onNavigate("decision")}>查看今日农活建议</button></div>
    </section>
    <section className="panel">
      <h3>{plots.length === 0 ? "先登记一块地" : relevantPlots.length ? "今日与逾期待办相关地块" : "已登记地块"}</h3>
      {plots.length === 0 ? <p>还没有地块。登记后，就能按地块记农活、查看每一茬的收支。</p> :

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 112 页 / 共 184 页
        <ul className="home-plots">{(relevantPlots.length ? relevantPlots : plots).map((plot) => <li key={plot.id}>
          <strong>{plot.name}{plot.is_demo && <span className="demo-badge">演示</span>}</strong><span>{plot.area_mu} 亩 · {plot.active_season?.crop || "暂无进行中茬次"} · 农活 {plot.record_count} 笔 · 待办 {plot.open_task_count} 项</span>

        </li>)}</ul>}
      <button className="secondary-button" type="button" onClick={() => onNavigate("plots")}>{plots.length ? "管理地块与茬次" : "登记地块"}</button>
    </section>
    {reminders.length > 0 && <section className="panel" aria-label="登记间隔期提醒">
      <h3>登记间隔期提醒</h3><p>复用登记施药日与间隔天数的计算结果，覆盖该地块全部茬次与作物；请核对具体产品标签。</p>
      <ul>{reminders.map((plot) => <li key={plot.id}>{plot.name}：登记的 {plot.harvest_safety!.material || "施药"} 间隔期尚未结束，按登记数据计算最早采收日为 {plot.harvest_safety!.earliest_harvest_on}。</li>)}</ul>
      <button className="ghost-button" type="button" onClick={() => onNavigate("ledger")}>查看施药台账</button>
    </section>}
  </section>;
}

import { useAsyncGuard } from "../hooks/useAsyncGuard";
import { useSessionGuard } from "../hooks/useSessionBoundary";
import { useRequestLeave } from "../hooks/useUnsavedChanges";
import { api, getErrorMessage } from "../lib/api";
import { formatDate } from "../lib/date";
import { EconomicsScope, FarmRecord, FarmRecordFilters, Plot, PlotSeason } from "../types";
const PAGE_SIZE = 20;
interface RecordsResponse {
  success: true;
  records: FarmRecord[];
  pagination: { has_more: boolean; next_cursor: string | null };
}
interface LedgerWorkspaceProps {
  plots: Plot[];
  seasons: PlotSeason[];
  initialFilters?: Partial<FarmRecordFilters>;
  onError: (message: string) => void;
  onRecordsChanged: () => void | Promise<void>;
  onQuickTask: (input: TaskInput) => Promise<unknown>;
}
const emptyPagination = { has_more: false, next_cursor: null as string | null };
function formatCost(value: number | null): string {
  return value === null ? "费用未填" : `¥${value}`;
}
function round2(value: number): number {
  return Math.round(value * 100) / 100;
}
export function LedgerWorkspace({ plots, seasons, initialFilters, onError, onRecordsChanged, onQuickTask }: LedgerWorkspaceProps) {
  const recordForm = useRef<FarmRecordFormHandle>(null);
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<FarmRecordFilters>(() => ({
    data_scope: "all",

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 113 页 / 共 184 页
    plot_id: "", season_id: "", economics_scope: "all", kind: "", date_from: "", date_to: "", ...initialFilters,
  }));
  const [deletionNotice, setDeletionNotice] = useState("");
  const [records, setRecords] = useState<FarmRecord[]>([]);
  const [pagination, setPagination] = useState(emptyPagination);
  const listVersion = useRef(0);
  const loadMoreInFlight = useRef(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [loadingMore, setLoadingMore] = useState(false);
  const [pendingDelete, setPendingDelete] = useState<FarmRecord | null>(null);
  const action = useAsyncGuard();
  const requestLeave = useRequestLeave();
  const sessionCurrent = useSessionGuard();
  const loadRecords = useCallback(
    async (cursor: string | null, recordFilters: FarmRecordFilters) => {
      const params: Record<string, string | number> = { limit: PAGE_SIZE };
      for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(recordFilters)) {
        if (value) params[key] = value;
      }
      if (cursor) params.cursor = cursor;
      const response = await api.get<RecordsResponse>("/api/farm-records", { params });
      return response.data;
    },
    [],
  );
  const refreshRecords = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!sessionCurrent()) return;
    const version = ++listVersion.current;
    setRecords([]);
    setPagination(emptyPagination);
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const data = await loadRecords(null, filters);
      if (version !== listVersion.current || !sessionCurrent()) return;
      setRecords(data.records);
      setPagination(data.pagination);
    } catch (error) {
      if (version === listVersion.current && sessionCurrent()) onError(getErrorMessage(error));
    } finally {
      if (version === listVersion.current && sessionCurrent()) setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [loadRecords, filters, onError, sessionCurrent]);
  useEffect(() => {
    void refreshRecords();
    return () => { listVersion.current += 1; };
  }, [refreshRecords]);
  function handleFilterChange(field: keyof FarmRecordFilters, value: string) {
    listVersion.current += 1;
    setRecords([]);
    setPagination(emptyPagination);

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 114 页 / 共 184 页
    setFilters((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: value, ...(field === "plot_id" ? { season_id: "" } : {}) }));
    onError("");
  }
  async function handleLoadMore() {
    if (!sessionCurrent() || !pagination.next_cursor || loading || loadMoreInFlight.current) return;
    const version = listVersion.current;
    loadMoreInFlight.current = true;
    setLoadingMore(true);
    try {
      const data = await loadRecords(pagination.next_cursor, filters);
      if (version !== listVersion.current || !sessionCurrent()) return;
      setRecords((current) => [...current, ...data.records]);
      setPagination(data.pagination);
    } catch (error) {
      if (version === listVersion.current && sessionCurrent()) onError(getErrorMessage(error));
    } finally {
      loadMoreInFlight.current = false;
      setLoadingMore(false);
    }
  }
  async function confirmDelete() {
    if (!pendingDelete || !sessionCurrent()) return;
    await action.run(async () => {
      try {
        await api.delete(`/api/farm-records/${pendingDelete.id}`);
        if (!sessionCurrent()) return;
        setDeletionNotice("台账已移入回收站，可从回收站恢复；最终删除前会保留。");
        onError("");
        recordForm.current?.discardDeletedRecord(pendingDelete.id);
        setPendingDelete(null);
        await onRecordsChanged();
        await refreshRecords();
      } catch (error) {
        if (sessionCurrent()) onError(getErrorMessage(error));
      }
    });
  }
  if (plots.length === 0) {
    return (
      <section className="workspace-grid">
        <div className="panel panel--full">

              <h3>还没有地块</h3>
              <p>农事台账要挂在地块上，先去「地块档案」登记一块地。</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    );

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 115 页 / 共 184 页

        onError={onError} onQuickTask={onQuickTask}
        onRecordsChanged={async () => { await onRecordsChanged(); await refreshRecords(); }} />
      <div className="panel panel--full">
        <div className="panel__header">
          <div>
            <h3>台账记录</h3>
            <p>按作业日期倒序排列。</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="field-grid">
          <label className="field"><span>台账数据来源</span><div className="field-control field-control--select">
            <select value={filters.data_scope ?? "all"} onChange={(event) => handleFilterChange("data_scope", event.target.value)} disabled={action.busy}>
              <option value="all">全部记录</option><option value="real">真实记录</option><option value="demo">演示记录</option>
            </select>
          </div></label>
          <label className="field">
            <span>按地块筛选</span>
            <div className="field-control field-control--select">
              <select value={filters.plot_id} disabled={action.busy} onChange={(event) => handleFilterChange("plot_id", event.target.value)}>
                <option value="">全部地块</option>
                {plots.map((plot) => (
                  <option key={plot.id} value={plot.id}>
                    {plot.name}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          </label>
          <label className="field">
            <span>按作业类型筛选</span>
            <div className="field-control field-control--select">
              <select value={filters.kind} disabled={action.busy} onChange={(event) => handleFilterChange("kind", event.target.value)}>
                <option value="">全部作业类型</option>
                {recordKinds.map((kind) => <option key={kind} value={kind}>{kind}</option>)}
              </select>
            </div>
          </label>
          <label className="field">
            <span>按茬次筛选</span>
            <div className="field-control field-control--select">
              <select value={filters.season_id} disabled={action.busy} onChange={(event) => handleFilterChange("season_id", event.target.value)}>
                <option value="">全部茬次</option>
                <option value="unassigned">未归茬</option>
                {seasons.filter((season) => !filters.plot_id || season.plot_id === filters.plot_id).map((season) => (
                  <option key={season.id} value={season.id}>

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 116 页 / 共 184 页

                  {record.season_id === null && <span className="stats-record__time">未归茬</span>}
                  <span className="stats-record__time">{formatCost(record.cost)}</span>

                  {record.earliest_harvest_on && (
                    <span className="stats-record__time">最早采收 {record.earliest_harvest_on}</span>
                  )}
                  {record.yield_kg !== null && <span className="stats-record__time">产量 {record.yield_kg} 公斤</span>}
                  {record.unit_price !== null && <span className="stats-record__time">{record.unit_price} 元/公斤</span>}
                  {record.kind === "采收" && (record.yield_kg === null || record.unit_price === null) && (
                    <span className="stats-record__time">采收待补录：{[record.yield_kg === null ? "产量" : "", record.unit_price === null ? "单价" : ""].filter(Boolean).join("、")}</span>
                  )}
                  {record.yield_kg !== null && record.unit_price !== null && (
                    <span className="stats-record__time">收入 ¥{round2(record.yield_kg * record.unit_price)}</span>
                  )}
                  <button
                    className="ghost-button"
                    type="button"
                    aria-label={`编辑 ${record.kind} 记录`}
                    onClick={() => requestLeave(() => recordForm.current?.edit(record))}
                    disabled={action.busy}
                  >
                    <Pencil size={15} />编辑
                  </button>
                  <button
                    className="ghost-button danger ledger-delete"
                    type="button"
                    aria-label={`删除 ${record.kind} 记录`}
                    onClick={() => setPendingDelete(record)}
                    disabled={action.busy}
                  >
                    <Trash2 size={15} />
                    删除
                  </button>
                </div>
                {record.detail && <p>{record.detail}</p>}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
        {pagination.has_more && (
          <button className="secondary-button" type="button" onClick={() => void handleLoadMore()} disabled={loadingMore || action.busy}>
            {loadingMore ? "加载中…" : "加载更多"}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      <ConfirmDialog
        open={pendingDelete !== null}
        title="删除这条农事记录？"
        description={
          pendingDelete
            ? `${pendingDelete.plot_name} 在 ${pendingDelete.happened_on} 的「${pendingDelete.kind}」记录将移入回收站，可恢复；不影响所属地块和其他台账。`
            : ""
        }

        confirmLabel="确认删除"
        busy={action.busy}
        onConfirm={() => void confirmDelete()}
        onCancel={() => setPendingDelete(null)}
      />
    </section>
  );
}
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useAsyncGuard } from "../hooks/useAsyncGuard";
import { useRequestLeave } from "../hooks/useUnsavedChanges";
import { api, getErrorMessage } from "../lib/api";
import { FeatureKey, OnboardingStatus, Plot, PlotSeason, User } from "../types";
interface OnboardingPanelProps {
  user: User;
  plots: Plot[];
  seasons: PlotSeason[];
  onStep: (step: "plot" | "season" | "record", plotId: string | null) => void;
  onNavigate: (feature: FeatureKey) => void;
  onDemoChanged: (loaded: boolean) => Promise<void>;
  onDemoStats: () => void;
}
export function OnboardingPanel({ user, plots, seasons, onStep, onNavigate, onDemoChanged, onDemoStats }: OnboardingPanelProps) {
  const [state, setState] = useState<OnboardingStatus | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [showCompleted, setShowCompleted] = useState(false);
  const action = useAsyncGuard();
  const requestLeave = useRequestLeave();
  const requestVersion = useRef(0);
  const reload = useCallback(async () => {
    const version = ++requestVersion.current;
    try {
      const response = await api.get<{ onboarding: OnboardingStatus }>("/api/onboarding");
      if (version !== requestVersion.current) return;
      setState(response.data.onboarding);
      setError("");
    } catch (requestError) { if (version === requestVersion.current) setError(getErrorMessage(requestError)); }
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => { void reload(); }, [reload, plots, seasons]);
  async function skip(skipped: boolean) {
    await action.run(async () => {
      requestVersion.current++;
      try {
        const response = await api.patch<{ onboarding: OnboardingStatus }>("/api/onboarding", { skipped });
        setState(response.data.onboarding);
        setError("");
      } catch (requestError) { setError(getErrorMessage(requestError)); }
    });
  }

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 119 页 / 共 184 页
  async function changeDemo(load: boolean) {
    await action.run(async () => {
      requestVersion.current++;
      try {
        const response = await api.request<{ onboarding: OnboardingStatus; retained?: { plots: number; plot_seasons: number } }>({ url: "/api/demo-data", method: load ? "POST" : "DELETE" });
        setState(response.data.onboarding);
        const retained = response.data.retained;
        setMessage(load ? "演示已加载，可查看地块、台账和收支。" : retained && retained.plots + retained.plot_seasons > 0
          ? `演示记录已清空。为保留你新增的数据，${retained.plots} 个地块、${retained.plot_seasons} 个茬次已转为普通资料。`
          : "演示数据已清空，自己新增的数据已保留。");
        setError("");
        await onDemoChanged(load);
      } catch (requestError) { setError(getErrorMessage(requestError)); }
    });
  }
  if (!state) return <section className="panel onboarding-panel" aria-label="首次使用引导">
    {error ? <><p role="alert">{error}</p><button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={() => void reload()}>重试引导</button></> : <p>正在核对首次使用进度…</p>}
  </section>;
  const count = Number(state.plot_done) + Number(state.season_done) + Number(state.record_done);
  const complete = count === 3;
  const expanded = !state.skipped && (!complete || showCompleted);
  const steps = [
    { key: "plot" as const, title: "登记第一块地", done: state.plot_done, hint: "保存地块时会同时建立第一茬。", label: "去登记地块" },
    { key: "season" as const, title: "建立第一茬", done: state.season_done, hint: state.season_done ? "第一茬已建立，无需重复创建。" : "已有地块无茬次时再补建。", label: "去建立第一茬" },
    { key: "record" as const, title: "记第一笔农活", done: state.record_done, hint: "填写实际日期、作业和费用。", label: "去记第一笔农活" },
  ];
  return <section className="panel onboarding-panel" aria-label="首次使用引导">
    <div className="home-actions"><h3>{complete ? "已完成第一条真实业务流程" : `首次使用 · ${count}/3 步已完成`}</h3>
      <div className="inline-actions">{expanded ? <button type="button" className="ghost-button" disabled={action.busy} onClick={() => void skip(true)}>暂时跳过</button>
        : <button type="button" className="ghost-button" disabled={action.busy} onClick={() => { if (complete) setShowCompleted(true); void skip(false); }}>{complete ? "查看步骤" : "继续引导"}</button>}
        {complete && <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={() => onNavigate("stats")}>查看收支</button>}</div>
    </div>
    {expanded && <><p>按真实数据核对进度，可跳过后继续。</p><ol className="onboarding-steps">{steps.map((step) => <li key={step.key}>
      <strong>{step.done ? "✓ 已完成：" : "待完成："}{step.title}</strong><p>{step.hint}</p>
      <button className="secondary-button" type="button" disabled={action.busy || (step.key !== "plot" && !state.plot_done)}
        onClick={() => step.done ? onNavigate(step.key === "record" ? "ledger" : "plots") : onStep(step.key, state.first_plot_id)}>
        {step.done ? step.key === "record" ? "查看台账" : "查看地块与茬次" : step.label}
      </button>
    </li>)}</ol></>}
    {state.demo_count > 0 && <p className="demo-notice" role="status">含演示资料，示例金额不代表真实经营成果，不计入引导进度。</p>}
    {(user.is_guest || state.demo_loaded || state.demo_count > 0) && <div className="onboarding-demo">
      <strong>{user.is_guest ? "先看看示例（可选）" : "已保留的演示资料"}</strong><p>了解地块、茬次、台账、待办与收支如何相连。</p>
      <div className="inline-actions">{state.demo_loaded ? <><button className="secondary-button" type="button" disabled={action.busy} onClick={onDemoStats}>查看演示收支</button><button className="ghost-button" type="button" disabled={action.busy} onClick={() => requestLeave(() => { void changeDemo(false); })}>只清空演示数据</button></>
        : user.is_guest ? <button className="secondary-button" type="button" disabled={action.busy} onClick={() => requestLeave(() => { void changeDemo(true); })}>{action.busy ? "处理中…" : "加载演示数据"}</button> : null}</div>
      {state.demo_loaded && <p>演示记录修改后仍属演示；清空会保留自行新增数据及必要关联。</p>}
    </div>}
    {message && <p role="status">{message}</p>}
    {error && <p role="alert">{error}</p>}
  </section>;
}

import { CalendarPlus, MapPin, Pencil, Plus, Sprout, Trash2, X } from "lucide-react";

import { api, getErrorMessage } from "../lib/api";
import { PlotDeleteImpact } from "./TrashWorkspace";
const crops = ["玉米", "水稻", "小麦", "大豆", "番茄", "黄瓜", "辣椒", "苹果", "柑橘"];
const soilTypes = ["壤土", "砂壤土", "砂土", "黏土", "砾质土"];
const irrigations = ["井灌", "渠灌", "喷灌", "滴灌", "雨养"];
const emptyForm: PlotInput = {
  name: "",
  area_mu: 1,
  soil_type: "壤土",
  irrigation: "井灌",
  crop: "玉米",
  planted_on: null,
  notes: "",
};
type Panel = "create" | "edit" | "new-season" | "end-season";
interface PlotsWorkspaceProps {
  initialSeasonPlotId?: string;
  plots: Plot[];
  seasons: PlotSeason[];
  loading: boolean;
  busy: boolean;
  seasonBusy: boolean;
  onCreate: (payload: PlotInput) => Promise<Plot | undefined>;
  onUpdate: (plotId: string, payload: PlotInput) => Promise<Plot | undefined>;
  onRemove: (plotId: string, revision?: string) => Promise<number | undefined>;
  onCreateSeason: (payload: SeasonInput) => Promise<PlotSeason | undefined>;
  onUpdateSeason: (seasonId: string, payload: SeasonUpdateInput) => Promise<PlotSeason | undefined>;
  onRecord: (plotId: string) => void;
}
export function PlotsWorkspace(props: PlotsWorkspaceProps) {
  const {
    plots, seasons, loading, busy, seasonBusy, onCreate, onUpdate, onRemove,
    onCreateSeason, onUpdateSeason,
  } = props;
  const [panel, setPanel] = useState<Panel>(props.initialSeasonPlotId ? "new-season" : "create");
  const [form, setForm] = useState<PlotInput>(emptyForm);
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [targetPlotId, setTargetPlotId] = useState<string | null>(props.initialSeasonPlotId ?? null);
  const [seasonForm, setSeasonForm] = useState({
    crop: "玉米",
    started_on: todayIso(),
    ended_on: todayIso(),
  });

  const [pendingDelete, setPendingDelete] = useState<Plot | null>(null);
  const [impact, setImpact] = useState<PlotDeleteImpact | null>(null);
  const [deleteError, setDeleteError] = useState("");
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pendingDelete) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    setImpact(null); setDeleteError("");
    void api.get<{ impact: PlotDeleteImpact }>(`/api/plots/${pendingDelete.id}/delete-impact`)
      .then((response) => { if (!cancelled) setImpact(response.data.impact); })
      .catch((error) => { if (!cancelled) setDeleteError(getErrorMessage(error)); });
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [pendingDelete]);
  const seasonCounts = useMemo(() => {
    const counts = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const season of seasons) {
      counts.set(season.plot_id, (counts.get(season.plot_id) ?? 0) + 1);
    }
    return counts;
  }, [seasons]);
  const targetPlot = plots.find((item) => item.id === targetPlotId) ?? null;
  const targetSeason = seasons.find((item) => item.plot_id === targetPlotId && item.active) ?? null;
  function setField<Key extends keyof PlotInput>(field: Key, value: PlotInput[Key]) {
    setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: value }));
  }
  function openCreate() {
    setForm(emptyForm);
    setEditingId(null);
    setTargetPlotId(null);
    setPanel("create");
  }
  function handleEdit(plot: Plot) {
    setEditingId(plot.id);
    setTargetPlotId(null);
    setForm({
      name: plot.name,
      area_mu: plot.area_mu,
      soil_type: plot.soil_type,
      irrigation: plot.irrigation,
      crop: plot.crop || "玉米",
      planted_on: plot.planted_on,
      notes: plot.notes,
    });
    setPanel("edit");
  }
  function openNewSeason(plot: Plot) {
    setTargetPlotId(plot.id);
    setSeasonForm({ crop: plot.crop || "玉米", started_on: todayIso(), ended_on: todayIso() });
    setPanel("new-season");
  }
  function openEndSeason(plot: Plot) {

    setPanel("end-season");

  async function handlePlotSubmit() {
    if (!form.name.trim()) return;
    const saved = editingId ? await onUpdate(editingId, form) : await onCreate(form);
    if (saved) openCreate();
  }
  async function handleSeasonSubmit() {
    if (!targetPlotId) return;
    if (panel === "new-season") {
      if (!seasonForm.crop.trim()) return;
      const created = await onCreateSeason({
        plot_id: targetPlotId,
        crop: seasonForm.crop,
        started_on: seasonForm.started_on || null,
        notes: "",
      });
      if (created) openCreate();
      return;

    if (!targetSeason) return;
    const updated = await onUpdateSeason(targetSeason.id, {
      crop: targetSeason.crop,
      started_on: targetSeason.started_on,
      ended_on: seasonForm.ended_on || null,
      notes: targetSeason.notes,
    });
    if (updated) openCreate();
  }
  async function confirmDelete() {
    if (!pendingDelete || !impact) return;
    const removed = await onRemove(pendingDelete.id, impact.revision);
    if (removed !== undefined) {
      if (editingId === pendingDelete.id) openCreate();
      setPendingDelete(null);
    } else {
      setImpact(null);
      setDeleteError("删除结果未确认或资料已变化，请取消并重新核对；若已移入回收站，可到回收站查看。");
    }
  }

    "new-season": "开始新茬",

                (targetSeason

                </div>

            : ""
        }
        confirmLabel="确认删除"
        confirmDisabled={!impact || !!deleteError}
        busy={busy}
        onConfirm={() => void confirmDelete()}
        onCancel={() => setPendingDelete(null)}
      />
    </section>
  );
}

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { api, getErrorMessage } from "../lib/api";
import { addDays } from "../lib/date";
import { FarmStatsPayload, ToolRecord, ToolStatsPayload } from "../types";
import { EconomicsPanel } from "./EconomicsPanel";
const PAGE_SIZE = 20;
type StatsWorkspaceProps = Parameters<typeof EconomicsPanel>[0];
interface RecordsResponse {
  success: true;
  records: ToolRecord[];
  pagination: { has_more: boolean; next_cursor: string | null };
}
function formatDay(day: string): string {
  const [, month, date] = day.split("-");
  return `${Number(month)}/${Number(date)}`;
}
function formatDateTime(value: string): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("zh-CN", {
    month: "numeric",
    day: "numeric",
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
  }).format(new Date(value));
}
function excerpt(text: string, max = 60): string {
  const compact = text.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  return compact.length > max ? `${compact.slice(0, max)}…` : compact;
}
export function StatsWorkspace(props: StatsWorkspaceProps) {
  const { onError } = props;
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<ToolStatsPayload | null>(null);
  const [farmStats, setFarmStats] = useState<FarmStatsPayload | null>(null);
  const [records, setRecords] = useState<ToolRecord[]>([]);
  const [pagination, setPagination] = useState<{ has_more: boolean; next_cursor: string | null }>({
    has_more: false,
    next_cursor: null,
  });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [loadingMore, setLoadingMore] = useState(false);

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 130 页 / 共 184 页
  const loadStats = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const [adviceResponse, farmResponse] = await Promise.all([
        api.get<{ success: true } & ToolStatsPayload>("/api/tool-records/stats"),
        api.get<{ success: true } & FarmStatsPayload>("/api/farm-records/stats"),
      ]);
      setStats(adviceResponse.data);
      setFarmStats(farmResponse.data);
      onError("");
    } catch (error) {
      onError(getErrorMessage(error));
    }
  }, [onError]);
  const loadRecords = useCallback(async (cursor: string | null) => {
    const params: Record<string, string | number> = { limit: PAGE_SIZE };
    if (cursor) params.cursor = cursor;
    const response = await api.get<RecordsResponse>("/api/tool-records", { params });
    return response.data;
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    setLoading(true);
    Promise.all([loadStats(), loadRecords(null)])
      .then(([, recordsData]) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setRecords(recordsData.records);
        setPagination(recordsData.pagination);
      })
      .catch((error: unknown) => {
        if (!cancelled) onError(getErrorMessage(error));
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [loadRecords, loadStats, onError]);
  const handleLoadMore = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!pagination.next_cursor || loadingMore) return;
    setLoadingMore(true);
    try {
      const data = await loadRecords(pagination.next_cursor);
      setRecords((current) => [...current, ...data.records]);
      setPagination(data.pagination);
    } catch (error) {
      onError(getErrorMessage(error));
    } finally {
      setLoadingMore(false);
    }

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 131 页 / 共 184 页
  }, [loadRecords, loadingMore, onError, pagination.next_cursor]);
  const trend = useMemo(() => {
    if (!stats) return [];
    return Array.from({ length: stats.by_day_days }, (_, offset) => {
      const key = addDays(stats.by_day_since, offset);
      return { day: key, total: stats.by_day[key] ?? 0 };
    });
  }, [stats]);
  const maxDaily = useMemo(() => Math.max(1, ...trend.map((item) => item.total)), [trend]);
  const decisionCount = stats?.counts_by_kind.decision ?? 0;
  return (
    <section className="workspace-grid workspace-grid--stats">
      <div className="panel panel--full">
        <div className="panel__header">
          <div>
            <h3>使用汇总</h3>
            <p>地块、台账与农活建议的累计情况。</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="stats-summary">
          <div className="stat-card"><span>地块数</span><strong>{loading ? "—" : farmStats?.plot_count ?? 0}</strong></div>
          <div className="stat-card"><span>作业记录</span><strong>{loading ? "—" : farmStats?.record_count ?? 0}</strong></div>
          <div className="stat-card"><span>农活建议</span><strong>{loading ? "—" : decisionCount}</strong></div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <EconomicsPanel {...props} />
      <div className="panel">
        <div className="panel__header">
          <div>
            <h3>近 {stats?.by_day_days ?? 14} 天建议次数</h3>
            <p>按天统计的农活建议次数。</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="stats-trend" role="img" aria-label="近两周农活建议趋势">
          {trend.map((item) => (
            <div className="stats-trend__col" key={item.day} title={`${item.day}：${item.total} 次`}>
              <div className="stats-trend__bar" style={{ height: `${Math.round((item.total / maxDaily) * 100)}%` }} />
              <span className="stats-trend__day">{formatDay(item.day)}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="panel">
        <div className="panel__header">
          <div>
            <h3>作物分布</h3>
            <p>历史记录中最常生成建议的作物。</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        {stats && stats.top_crops.length > 0 ? (

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 132 页 / 共 184 页
          <ul className="stats-crops">
            {stats.top_crops.map((item) => (
              <li key={item.crop}>
                <span>{item.crop}</span>
                <strong>{item.total} 次</strong>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        ) : (
          <p className="stats-empty">还没有记录，先去生成一次今日农活建议吧。</p>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="panel panel--full">
        <div className="panel__header">
          <div>
            <h3>历史记录</h3>
            <p>最近的农活建议结果。</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        {records.length === 0 && !loading ? (
          <p className="stats-empty">还没有生成过建议，去「今日农活」试一次吧。</p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="stats-records">
            {records.map((record) => (
              <li className="stats-record" key={record.id}>
                <div className="stats-record__meta">
                  <strong>{record.crop}</strong>
                  <span className="stats-record__time">{formatDateTime(record.created_at)}</span>
                </div>
                <p>{excerpt(record.result)}</p>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
        {pagination.has_more && (
          <button className="secondary-button" type="button" onClick={() => void handleLoadMore()} disabled={loadingMore}>
            {loadingMore ? "加载中…" : "加载更多"}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
import { CalendarPlus, Check, Pencil, RotateCcw, Trash2, X } from "lucide-react";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";

import { TaskInput } from "../hooks/useTasks";
import { addDays, diffDays, formatDate, todayIso } from "../lib/date";
import { FarmTask, Plot } from "../types";
interface TasksWorkspaceProps {

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 133 页 / 共 184 页
  tasks: FarmTask[];
  recentDone: FarmTask[];
  plots: Plot[];
  loading: boolean;
  busy: boolean;
  onCreate: (payload: TaskInput) => Promise<FarmTask | undefined>;
  onUpdate: (taskId: string, payload: TaskInput, done: boolean) => Promise<FarmTask | undefined>;
  onToggle: (task: FarmTask) => Promise<FarmTask | undefined>;
  onRemove: (taskId: string) => Promise<boolean>;
  onRecord: (task: FarmTask) => void;
}
function groupLabel(dueOn: string, today: string): string {
  const offset = diffDays(today, dueOn);
  if (offset < 0) return "已逾期";
  if (offset === 0) return "今天";
  if (offset <= 7) return "本周内";
  return "更远";
}
export function TasksWorkspace(props: TasksWorkspaceProps) {
  const { tasks, recentDone, plots, loading, busy, onCreate, onUpdate, onToggle, onRemove, onRecord } = props;
  const today = todayIso();
  const [form, setForm] = useState<TaskInput>({
    plot_id: null,
    title: "",
    due_on: addDays(today, 1),
    notes: "",
  });
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pendingDelete, setPendingDelete] = useState<FarmTask | null>(null);
  const grouped = useMemo(() => {
    const order = ["已逾期", "今天", "本周内", "更远"];
    const buckets = new Map<string, FarmTask[]>(order.map((label) => [label, []]));
    for (const task of tasks) {
      buckets.get(groupLabel(task.due_on, today))?.push(task);
    }
    return order
      .map((label) => ({ label, items: buckets.get(label) ?? [] }))
      .filter((group) => group.items.length > 0);
  }, [tasks, today]);
  function resetForm() {
    setForm({ plot_id: null, title: "", due_on: addDays(today, 1), notes: "" });
    setEditingId(null);
  }
  async function handleSubmit() {
    if (!form.title.trim()) return;
    const saved = editingId ? await onUpdate(editingId, form, false) : await onCreate(form);
    if (saved) resetForm();
  }
  function handleEdit(task: FarmTask) {
    setEditingId(task.id);

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 134 页 / 共 184 页
    setForm({
      plot_id: task.plot_id,
      title: task.title,
      due_on: task.due_on,
      notes: task.notes,
    });
  }
  async function confirmDelete() {
    if (!pendingDelete) return;
    const removed = await onRemove(pendingDelete.id);
    if (removed) {
      if (editingId === pendingDelete.id) resetForm();
      setPendingDelete(null);
    }
  }
  return (
    <section className="workspace-grid workspace-grid--tasks">
      <div className="panel">
        <div className="panel__header">
          <div>
            <h3>{editingId ? "编辑待办" : "新增待办"}</h3>
            <p>把接下来要做的事记下来，到期会在顶栏提醒。</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="field-stack">
          <label className="field">
            <span>要做的事</span>
            <div className="field-control">
              <input
                value={form.title}
                maxLength={60}
                placeholder="例如：追肥后 7 天复查长势"
                onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, title: event.target.value }))}
              />
            </div>
          </label>
          <label className="field">
            <span>到期日</span>
            <div className="field-control">
              <input
                type="date"
                value={form.due_on}
                onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, due_on: event.target.value }))}
              />
            </div>
          </label>
          <label className="field">
            <span>关联地块（可不选）</span>
            <div className="field-control field-control--select">
              <select

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 135 页 / 共 184 页
                value={form.plot_id ?? ""}
                onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, plot_id: event.target.value || null }))}
              >
                <option value="">不关联地块</option>
                {plots.map((plot) => (
                  <option key={plot.id} value={plot.id}>
                    {plot.name}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          </label>
          <label className="field">
            <span>备注</span>
            <div className="field-control field-control--textarea">
              <textarea
                value={form.notes}
                maxLength={1000}
                placeholder="补充说明，也可以粘贴整段农活建议"
                onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, notes: event.target.value }))}
              />
            </div>
          </label>
        </div>
        <div className="inline-actions">
          <button className="primary-button" type="button" onClick={() => void handleSubmit()} disabled={busy || !form.title.trim()}>
            {editingId ? <Pencil size={16} /> : <CalendarPlus size={16} />}
            {busy ? "保存中…" : editingId ? "保存修改" : "新增待办"}
          </button>
          {editingId && (

            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="panel">
        <div className="panel__header">

        </div>

          <p className="stats-empty">没有待办事项，先在左边加一条吧。</p>

            <div className="task-group" key={group.label}>
              <div className={group.label === "已逾期" ? "task-group__title task-group__title--overdue" : "task-group__title"}>

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 136 页 / 共 184 页

              <ul className="task-list">

                    <div className="task-card__head">
                      <span className="task-card__title">{task.title}</span>
                      {task.is_demo && <span className="demo-badge">演示</span>}
                      <span className="stats-record__time">{formatDate(task.due_on)}</span>

                    {task.plot_name && <div className="plot-card__meta"><span>{task.plot_name}</span></div>}
                    {task.notes && <p className="task-card__notes">{task.notes}</p>}

                      <button className="ghost-button" type="button" onClick={() => void onToggle(task)} disabled={busy}>
                        <Check size={15} />

          <div className="task-group">

            <ul className="task-list">

                <li className="task-card task-card--done" key={task.id}>
                  <div className="task-card__head">

                    {task.is_demo && <span className="demo-badge">演示</span>}
                    <span className="stats-record__time">{formatDate(task.due_on)}</span>

import { memo } from "react";
import { FeatureKey, HealthPayload } from "../types";

type PendingPurge = { kind: "plots" | "records"; id: string; revision: string; description: string };
export function TrashWorkspace({ onChanged }: { onChanged: () => Promise<void> }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<TrashResponse | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<PendingPurge | null>(null);
  const action = useAsyncGuard();
  const reload = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true); setData(null);
    try { setData((await api.get<TrashResponse>("/api/trash")).data); }
    catch (requestError) { setError(getErrorMessage(requestError)); }
    finally { setLoading(false); }
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => { void reload(); }, [reload]);
  async function operate(kind: string, id: string, revision?: string) {
    await action.run(async () => {
      setError(""); setMessage("");
      try {
        const response = revision
          ? await api.delete<{ message: string }>(`/api/trash/${kind}/${id}`, { params: { confirm: true, expected_revision: revision } })
          : await api.post<{ message: string }>(`/api/trash/${kind}/${id}/restore`);
        setPending(null); setMessage(response.data.message);
        await onChanged(); await reload();
      } catch (requestError) { setError(getErrorMessage(requestError)); setPending(null); await reload(); }
    });
  }
  return <section className="panel panel--full" aria-label="回收站资料">
    <h3>回收站</h3>
    <p>资料保留至你主动最终删除；当前没有自动清理。最终删除后无法从回收站恢复，历史已硬删除的数据也无法在这里找回。</p>
    <p>地块的茬次、台账和待办一起保留。恢复地块会恢复关联资料的可见性，此前单独删除的台账需要再单独恢复。</p>
    {message && <p role="status">{message}</p>}{error && <p role="alert">{error}</p>}
    <button type="button" className="ghost-button" disabled={loading || action.busy} onClick={() => { setError(""); void reload(); }}>刷新回收站</button>
    {loading ? <p role="status">正在读取回收站…</p> : data && <>
      <section aria-label="回收站地块"><h4>地块</h4>{data.plots.length === 0 ? <p>没有已删除地块。</p> : data.plots.map((plot) => <article className="trash-card" key={plot.id}>

        <h4>{plot.name}{plot.is_demo && <span className="demo-badge">演示资料</span>}</h4>
        <p>{plot.season_count} 茬、{plot.record_count} 条台账（含单独删除 {plot.trashed_record_count} 条）、{plot.task_count} 项待办（含已完成）</p>
        <p>移入时间：{new Date(plot.deleted_at).toLocaleString("zh-CN")}</p>
        <div className="inline-actions"><button className="secondary-button" type="button" disabled={action.busy} onClick={() => void operate("plots", plot.id)}>恢复地块 {plot.name}</button>
          <button className="ghost-button danger" type="button" disabled={action.busy} onClick={() => setPending({ kind: "plots", id: plot.id, revision: plot.revision,

        <h4>{record.plot_name} · {record.kind} · {record.happened_on}{record.is_demo && <span className="demo-badge">演示记录</span>}</h4>
        <p>移入时间：{new Date(record.deleted_at).toLocaleString("zh-CN")}</p>

            description: `${record.plot_name} 在 ${record.happened_on} 的「${record.kind}」台账将永久删除，无法再从回收站恢复。所属地块不受影响。` })}>最终删除台账 {record.kind} {record.happened_on}</button></div>
      </article>)}</section>
    </>}
    <ConfirmDialog open={pending !== null} title="确认最终删除？" description={pending?.description ?? ""} confirmLabel="最终删除（不可恢复）" busy={action.busy}

import { useCallback, useRef } from "react";
import { useSessionGuard } from "./useSessionBoundary";
export function useAccountGuard(accountId: string | null) {
  const current = useRef(accountId);
  current.current = accountId;
  const sessionCurrent = useSessionGuard();
  return useCallback(() => sessionCurrent() && accountId !== null && current.current === accountId, [accountId, sessionCurrent]);
}
import { useCallback, useRef, useState } from "react";
export function useAsyncGuard() {
  const inFlightRef = useRef(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const run = useCallback(async <T>(operation: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T | undefined> => {
    if (inFlightRef.current) {
      return undefined;
    }
    inFlightRef.current = true;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      return await operation();
    } finally {
      inFlightRef.current = false;
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }, []);
  const isRunning = useCallback(() => inFlightRef.current, []);
  return { busy, run, isRunning };
}
