import { useSessionBoundary, useSessionGuard } from "./useSessionBoundary";
import { api, getApiErrorInfo, getErrorMessage } from "../lib/api";
import { clearLogoutIntent, readLogoutIntent, saveLogoutIntent } from "../lib/logoutIntent";
import { HealthPayload, User } from "../types";
export type SessionResetReason = "login" | "logout" | "expired";
interface AuthSessionOptions {
  onError: (message: string) => void;
  onNotice: (message: string) => void;
  onSessionReset: (reason: SessionResetReason) => void;
}
export function useAuthSession({ onError, onNotice, onSessionReset }: AuthSessionOptions) {
  const { advance, capture, isCurrent } = useSessionBoundary();
  const sessionCurrent = useSessionGuard();
  const sessionId = useRef("");
  const [logoutPending, setLogoutPending] = useState(readLogoutIntent);
  const { run: runLogout, busy: logoutBusy } = useAsyncGuard();
  const [health, setHealth] = useState<HealthPayload | null>(null);
  const [bootLoading, setBootLoading] = useState(true);
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [authMode, setAuthMode] = useState<"login" | "register">("login");
  const [recovering, setRecovering] = useState(false);
  const [authForm, setAuthForm] = useState({ username: "", password: "", displayName: "" });
  const [settingsName, setSettingsName] = useState("");
  const authAction = useAsyncGuard();
  const settingsAction = useAsyncGuard();
  const confirmLogout = useCallback(async (intent: string, ticket: number) => {
    await runLogout(async () => {
      if (!isCurrent(ticket) || readLogoutIntent() !== intent) return;
      let confirmed = false;
      let failureMessage = "退出尚未确认，当前资料已隐藏。请检查网络后重试；确认前请勿将设备交给他人。";
      try {
        await api.post("/api/auth/logout", { session_id: intent });
        confirmed = true;
      } catch (requestError) {
        const status = getApiErrorInfo(requestError).status;
        if (status === 409) failureMessage = "退出尚未确认：当前会话已变化，已停止旧退出请求以保护新登录账号，资料继续隐藏。";
        if (status === 403) failureMessage = "退出尚未确认：安全校验未通过，请刷新页面后重试。当前资料已隐藏。";
        if (!isCurrent(ticket)) return;
        try { await api.get("/api/me"); }
        catch (requestError) { confirmed = getApiErrorInfo(requestError).status === 401; }
      }
      if (!isCurrent(ticket) || readLogoutIntent() !== intent) return;
      if (confirmed) {
        clearLogoutIntent(intent);
        setLogoutPending(null);
        onError("");
        onNotice("已确认退出登录。");
      } else {
        onError(failureMessage);
      }

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 142 页 / 共 184 页
    });
  }, [runLogout, isCurrent, onError, onNotice]);
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    const ticket = capture();
    const current = () => !cancelled && isCurrent(ticket);
    async function bootstrap() {
      try {
        const response = await api.get<HealthPayload>("/api/health");
        if (!current()) return;
        setHealth(response.data);
        await api.get("/api/auth/csrf");
        if (!current()) return;
        const pending = readLogoutIntent();
        if (pending) {
          await confirmLogout(pending, ticket);
          return;
        }
        try {
          const me = await api.get<{ user: User; session_id: string }>("/api/me");
          if (!current() || readLogoutIntent()) return;
          sessionId.current = me.data.session_id;
          setUser(me.data.user);
          setSettingsName(me.data.user.display_name);
        } catch {
        }
      } catch (requestError) {
        if (current()) onError(getErrorMessage(requestError, "后端未连接，请先启动服务。"));
      } finally {
        if (current()) setBootLoading(false);
      }
    }
    void bootstrap();
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [capture, isCurrent, confirmLogout, onError]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const retry = () => {
      const pending = readLogoutIntent();
      if (pending) void confirmLogout(pending, capture());
    };
    window.addEventListener("online", retry);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("online", retry);
  }, [capture, confirmLogout]);
  function applyAuthenticatedUser(nextUser: User, nextSessionId: string, ticket: number) {
    if (!isCurrent(ticket)) return;
    sessionId.current = nextSessionId;
    onSessionReset("login");
    onError("");
    onNotice("");
    setUser(nextUser);

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 143 页 / 共 184 页
    setSettingsName(nextUser.display_name);
    setAuthForm({ username: "", password: "", displayName: "" });
  }
  async function handleAuthSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (readLogoutIntent() || logoutBusy) return;
    await authAction.run(async () => {
      const ticket = advance();
      onError("");
      try {
        const endpoint = authMode === "login" ? "/api/auth/login" : "/api/auth/register";
        const payload = authMode === "login"
          ? { username: authForm.username, password: authForm.password }
          : { username: authForm.username, password: authForm.password, display_name: authForm.displayName };
        const response = await api.post<{ user: User; session_id: string }>(endpoint, payload);
        applyAuthenticatedUser(response.data.user, response.data.session_id, ticket);
      } catch (requestError) {
        if (isCurrent(ticket)) onError(getErrorMessage(requestError));
      }
    });
  }
  async function handleGuestLogin() {
    if (readLogoutIntent() || logoutBusy) return;
    await authAction.run(async () => {
      const ticket = advance();
      onError("");
      try {
        const response = await api.post<{ user: User; session_id: string }>("/api/auth/guest");
        applyAuthenticatedUser(response.data.user, response.data.session_id, ticket);
      } catch (requestError) {
        if (isCurrent(ticket)) onError(getErrorMessage(requestError));
      }
    });
  }
  async function handleSaveSettings() {
    if (!sessionCurrent()) return;
    await settingsAction.run(async () => {
      const ticket = capture();
      if (!settingsName.trim()) { onError("显示名称不能为空。"); return; }
      try {
        const response = await api.patch<{ user: User }>("/api/me/profile", { display_name: settingsName });
        if (!isCurrent(ticket)) return;
        setUser(response.data.user);
        setSettingsName(response.data.user.display_name);
        onError("");
      } catch (requestError) {
        if (isCurrent(ticket)) onError(getErrorMessage(requestError));
      }
    });
  }

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 144 页 / 共 184 页
  async function handleLogout(username = "") {
    if (!sessionCurrent() || logoutBusy) return;
    const intent = sessionId.current;
    try { saveLogoutIntent(intent); }
    catch { onError("无法保存退出状态，请允许浏览器保存本站数据后重试退出。"); return; }
    const ticket = advance();
    setLogoutPending(intent);
    onSessionReset("logout");
    setUser(null);
    setSettingsName("");
    setAuthMode("login");
    setAuthForm({ username, password: "", displayName: "" });
    onNotice("");
    onError("");
    await confirmLogout(intent, ticket);
  }
  function returnToLogin(username: string, message = "") {
    if (!sessionCurrent()) return;
    advance();
    onSessionReset("expired");
    setUser(null);
    setRecovering(false);
    onError("");
    onNotice(message);
    setAuthMode("login");
    setAuthForm({ username, password: "", displayName: "" });
  }
  function acceptGuestUpgrade(saved: User, nextSessionId: string) {
    if (!sessionCurrent()) return;
    advance();
    sessionId.current = nextSessionId;
    setUser(saved);
    setSettingsName(saved.display_name);
    onError("");
    onNotice(`账号已保存为 ${saved.username}，原有数据全部保留。`);
  }
  function startRecovery() {
    setRecovering(true);
    onError("");
    onNotice("");
    setAuthForm({ username: "", password: "", displayName: "" });
  }
  return {
    health, bootLoading, user, logoutPending, logoutBusy,
    retryLogout: () => { if (logoutPending) void confirmLogout(logoutPending, capture()); },
    authMode, setAuthMode, authForm, setAuthForm, authBusy: authAction.busy,
    handleAuthSubmit, handleGuestLogin, handleLogout,
    settingsName, setSettingsName, settingsBusy: settingsAction.busy, handleSaveSettings,
    recovering, startRecovery, returnToLogin, acceptGuestUpgrade,
  };

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 145 页 / 共 184 页
}
import { RefObject, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
let openDialogs = 0;
let previousOverflow = "";
export function useDialogFocus(ref: RefObject<HTMLElement>, open: boolean, onClose: () => void) {
  const close = useRef(onClose);
  close.current = onClose;
  useEffect(() => {
    const dialog = ref.current;
    if (!open || !dialog) return;
    const previous = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
    if (openDialogs++ === 0) previousOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    dialog.focus();
    const focusables = () => [...dialog.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>("button, input, select, textarea, a[href], [tabindex='0']")]
      .filter((element) => !element.matches(":disabled") && element.getClientRects().length > 0);
    function keydown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
      const dialogs = document.querySelectorAll('[role="dialog"]');
      if (dialogs[dialogs.length - 1] !== dialog) return;
      if (event.key === "Escape") { event.preventDefault(); close.current(); }
      if (event.key !== "Tab") return;
      const items = focusables();
      const first = items[0];
      const last = items[items.length - 1];
      if (!first) { event.preventDefault(); return; }
      if (event.shiftKey && (document.activeElement === first || document.activeElement === dialog)) {
        event.preventDefault(); last.focus();
      } else if (!event.shiftKey && (document.activeElement === last || document.activeElement === dialog)) {
        event.preventDefault(); first.focus();
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener("keydown", keydown);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", keydown);
      if (--openDialogs === 0) document.body.style.overflow = previousOverflow;
      if (previous?.isConnected) previous.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    };
  }, [ref, open]);
}

interface UsePlotsOptions {
  onError: (message: string) => void;
  enabled: boolean;
  accountId: string | null;

export function usePlots({ onError, enabled, accountId }: UsePlotsOptions) {
  const [data, setData] = useState({ accountId, items: [] as Plot[] });
  const items = data.accountId === accountId ? data.items : [];
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const action = useAsyncGuard();
  const isCurrent = useAccountGuard(accountId);
  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!isCurrent()) return;
    try {
      const response = await api.get<PlotsResponse>("/api/plots");
      if (!isCurrent()) return;
      setData({ accountId, items: response.data.plots });
      onError("");
    } catch (error) {
      if (isCurrent()) onError(getErrorMessage(error));
    }
  }, [onError, accountId, isCurrent]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) {
      setData({ accountId, items: [] });
      return;
    }
    let cancelled = false;
    setLoading(true);
    void refresh().finally(() => {
      if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
    });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 147 页 / 共 184 页
    };
  }, [enabled, refresh, accountId]);
  const create = useCallback(
    async (payload: PlotInput) => {
      let created: Plot | undefined;
      await action.run(async () => {
        if (!isCurrent()) return;
        try {
          const response = await api.post<PlotResponse>("/api/plots", payload);
          if (!isCurrent()) return;
          created = response.data.plot;
          onError("");
        } catch (error) {
          if (isCurrent()) onError(getErrorMessage(error));
        }
      });
      if (created) await refresh();
      return isCurrent() ? created : undefined;
    },
    [action, onError, refresh, isCurrent],

import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
const SessionContext = createContext<{
  epoch: number;
  advance: () => number;
  capture: () => number;
  isCurrent: (epoch: number) => boolean;
} | null>(null);
export function SessionBoundary({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 151 页 / 共 184 页
  const current = useRef(0);
  const [epoch, setEpoch] = useState(0);
  const advance = useCallback(() => {
    const next = ++current.current;
    setEpoch(next);
    return next;
  }, []);
  const capture = useCallback(() => current.current, []);
  const isCurrent = useCallback((ticket: number) => current.current === ticket, []);
  const value = useMemo(() => ({ epoch, advance, capture, isCurrent }), [epoch, advance, capture, isCurrent]);
  return <SessionContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</SessionContext.Provider>;
}
export function useSessionBoundary() {
  const session = useContext(SessionContext);
  if (!session) throw new Error("缺少 SessionBoundary");
  return session;
}
export function useSessionGuard() {
  const { epoch, isCurrent } = useSessionBoundary();
  return useCallback(() => isCurrent(epoch), [epoch, isCurrent]);
}

import { createContext, ReactNode, useCallback, useContext, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { ConfirmDialog } from "../components/ConfirmDialog";
type FormState = { dirty: boolean; busy: boolean; discard: () => void };
type LeaveAction = () => void;
const UnsavedContext = createContext<{
  register: (key: symbol, state: FormState) => () => void;
  requestLeave: (action: LeaveAction) => void;
} | null>(null);
export function UnsavedChangesProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const forms = useRef(new Map<symbol, FormState>());
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<{ action: LeaveAction } | null>(null);
  const register = useCallback((key: symbol, state: FormState) => {
    forms.current.set(key, state);
    return () => { forms.current.delete(key); };
  }, []);
  const requestLeave = useCallback((action: LeaveAction) => {
    const states = [...forms.current.values()];
    if (states.some((state) => state.busy)) return;
    if (states.some((state) => state.dirty)) setPending({ action });
    else action();
  }, []);
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    function preventUnload(event: BeforeUnloadEvent) {
      if (![...forms.current.values()].some((state) => state.dirty || state.busy)) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      event.returnValue = "";
    }
    window.addEventListener("beforeunload", preventUnload);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("beforeunload", preventUnload);
  }, []);
  return <UnsavedContext.Provider value={{ register, requestLeave }}>
    {children}
    <ConfirmDialog open={pending !== null} title="放弃未保存的农活？"
      description="已填写的内容尚未确认保存，离开会丢弃本次填写。若刚才网络中断，请先重试保存或核对台账。"
      confirmLabel="放弃并离开" busy={false}
      onCancel={() => setPending(null)}
      onConfirm={() => {

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 155 页 / 共 184 页
        const action = pending?.action;
        setPending(null);
        forms.current.forEach((state) => { if (state.dirty) state.discard(); });
        action?.();
      }} />
  </UnsavedContext.Provider>;
}
export function useRequestLeave() {
  const context = useContext(UnsavedContext);
  if (!context) throw new Error("Missing UnsavedChangesProvider");
  return context.requestLeave;
}
export function useUnsavedChanges(dirty: boolean, busy: boolean, onDiscard: () => void) {
  const context = useContext(UnsavedContext);
  const key = useRef(Symbol("ledger form"));
  const register = context?.register;
  const discard = useRef(onDiscard);
  discard.current = onDiscard;
  useLayoutEffect(() => register?.(key.current, { dirty, busy, discard: () => discard.current() }), [register, dirty, busy]);
}

export function readCookie(name: string): string {
  const prefix = `${name}=`;
  return document.cookie.split(";").map((item) => item.trim()).find((item) => item.startsWith(prefix))?.slice(prefix.length) ?? "";
}
api.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
  if (config.method && !["get", "head", "options"].includes(config.method.toLowerCase())) {
    const csrfToken = readCookie("yunxun_csrf");
    if (csrfToken) config.headers.set("X-CSRF-Token", csrfToken);
  }
  return config;
});
interface ApiErrorPayload {
  success?: false;
  error?: string;
  code?: string;
  detail?: unknown;
}
export interface ApiErrorInfo {
  code?: string;
  message: string;
  status?: number;

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 156 页 / 共 184 页
  requestId?: string;
  retryable: boolean;
}
function isApiErrorPayload(value: unknown): value is ApiErrorPayload {
  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null;
}
function messageByStatus(status: number | undefined): string {
  if (status === 401) {
    return "登录已失效，请重新登录。";
  }
  if (status === 403) {
    return "你没有权限执行这个操作。";
  }
  if (status === 404) {
    return "要访问的内容不存在或已被删除。";
  }
  if (status === 413) {
    return "上传内容过大，请压缩后再试。";
  }
  if (status === 422) {
    return "提交内容格式不正确，请检查后再试。";
  }
  if (status === 429) {
    return "操作太频繁了，请稍后再试。";
  }
  if (status && status >= 500) {
    return "后端服务暂时不可用，请稍后重试。";
  }
  return "请求失败，请稍后重试。";
}
function axiosErrorMessage(error: AxiosError, fallback: string): string {
  if (error.code === "ECONNABORTED") {
    return "请求超时，请检查网络或稍后重试。";
  }
  if (!error.response) {
    return "无法连接后端服务，请确认后端已启动。";
  }
  const payload = error.response.data;
  if (isApiErrorPayload(payload) && typeof payload.error === "string" && payload.error.trim()) {
    return payload.error;
  }
  return messageByStatus(error.response.status) || fallback;
}
export function getErrorMessage(error: unknown, fallback = "请求失败，请稍后重试。"): string {
  return getApiErrorInfo(error, fallback).message;
}
export function getApiErrorInfo(error: unknown, fallback = "请求失败，请稍后重试。"): ApiErrorInfo {
  if (axios.isAxiosError(error)) {
    const status = error.response?.status;
    const payload = error.response?.data;

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 157 页 / 共 184 页
    return {
      code: isApiErrorPayload(payload) && typeof payload.code === "string" ? payload.code : undefined,
      message: axiosErrorMessage(error, fallback),
      status,
      requestId: error.response?.headers?.["x-request-id"],
      retryable: !status || status === 408 || status === 429 || status >= 500,
    };
  }
  if (error instanceof Error && error.message) {
    return { message: error.message, retryable: false };
  }
  return { message: fallback, retryable: false };
}
export function formatAppVersion(value: string): string {
  const version = value.trim();
  if (!version) return "V—";
  return `V${version.replace(/^v/i, "")}`;
}
import axios from "axios";
import { api } from "./api";
export async function downloadCsv(endpoint: string, params: Record<string, string>, fallbackName: string) {
  try {
    const response = await api.get<Blob>(endpoint, {
      params: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(params).filter(([, value]) => value)), responseType: "blob",
    });
    if (!String(response.headers["content-type"] ?? "").toLowerCase().startsWith("text/csv")) {
      throw new Error("导出响应格式异常，未下载文件。请稍后重试。");
    }
    const disposition = String(response.headers["content-disposition"] ?? "");
    const encoded = disposition.match(/filename\*=UTF-8''([^;]+)/i)?.[1];
    let filename = fallbackName;
    if (encoded) {
      try { filename = decodeURIComponent(encoded); } catch { /* 采用本地默认文件名。 */ }
    }
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(response.data);
    const anchor = document.createElement("a");
    anchor.href = url;
    anchor.download = filename;
    document.body.appendChild(anchor);
    try { anchor.click(); } finally {
      anchor.remove();
      window.setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
    }
    const count = Number(response.headers["x-export-row-count"]);
    return { filename, count: Number.isInteger(count) && count >= 0 ? count : undefined };
  } catch (requestError) {
    if (axios.isAxiosError(requestError) && requestError.response?.data instanceof Blob) {
      try { requestError.response.data = JSON.parse(await requestError.response.data.text()); } catch { /* 非 JSON 时用统一错误提示。 */ }
    }
    throw requestError;

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 158 页 / 共 184 页
  }
}
export function todayIso(): string {
  const now = new Date();
  return new Date(now.getTime() - now.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}
export function addDays(isoDate: string, days: number): string {
  const base = new Date(`${isoDate}T00:00:00`);
  base.setDate(base.getDate() + days);
  return `${base.getFullYear()}-${String(base.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(base.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}
export function diffDays(fromIso: string, toIso: string): number {
  const from = new Date(`${fromIso}T00:00:00`).getTime();
  const to = new Date(`${toIso}T00:00:00`).getTime();
  return Math.round((to - from) / 86400000);
}
export function formatDate(isoDate: string): string {
  const [year, month, date] = isoDate.split("-");
  return `${year}/${Number(month)}/${Number(date)}`;
}
const key = "yunxun.logout-pending";
export function readLogoutIntent(): string | null {
  return localStorage.getItem(key);
}
export function saveLogoutIntent(sessionId: string) {
  localStorage.setItem(key, sessionId);
}
export function clearLogoutIntent(sessionId: string) {
  if (readLogoutIntent() === sessionId) localStorage.removeItem(key);
}
export function newSubmissionId(): string {
  if (typeof crypto.randomUUID === "function") return crypto.randomUUID();
  const bytes = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16));
  bytes[6] = (bytes[6] & 0x0f) | 0x40;
  bytes[8] = (bytes[8] & 0x3f) | 0x80;
  const hex = Array.from(bytes, (value) => value.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
  return `${hex.slice(0, 8)}-${hex.slice(8, 12)}-${hex.slice(12, 16)}-${hex.slice(16, 20)}-${hex.slice(20)}`;
}
import { FarmRecordKind, FarmTask, PlotSeason } from "../types";
import { todayIso } from "./date";
export interface LedgerDraft {
  plot_id: string;
  happened_on: string;
  kind: FarmRecordKind;
  detail: string;
  season_id?: string;
}
export function taskLedgerDraft(task: FarmTask, seasons: PlotSeason[]): LedgerDraft {
  const completed = task.done_at ? new Date(task.done_at) : null;
  const happened_on = completed && Number.isFinite(completed.getTime())

云寻智慧农业工作台软件 1.0.0    第 159 页 / 共 184 页
    ? `${completed.getFullYear()}-${String(completed.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(completed.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`
    : todayIso();
  const candidates = seasons.filter((season) => season.plot_id === task.plot_id
    && (!season.started_on || season.started_on <= happened_on)
    && (!season.ended_on || happened_on <= season.ended_on));
  const season = candidates.length === 1 && candidates[0].started_on ? candidates[0] : undefined;
  return {
    plot_id: task.plot_id ?? "", happened_on, kind: "其他",
    detail: [task.title, task.notes].filter(Boolean).join("；").slice(0, 300),
    ...(season ? { season_id: season.id } : {}),
  };
}
